+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0

+ * Copyright 2022 HabanaLabs, Ltd.

+
+#ifndef __HL_HBM_API_H
+#define __HL_HBM_API_H
+
+#include <device.h>
+#include <kernel.h>
+#include <common/hl_hbm.h>
+#include <hl_binning_defs.h>
+
+/**
+ * @brief runs full hbm init flow
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ *
+ * @warning before calling: must ensure Linux is not loaded and no traffic towards DRAM
+ */
+int hbm_drv_run_hbm_init(const struct device *dev);
+
+/**
+ * @brief injecting information into dev.hbm_info
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param info: the information we wish to inject
+ */
+void hbm_drv_inject_info(const struct device *dev,  void *info);
+
+/**
+ * @brief gets HBM's temperature
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param hbm_dev: device number
+ */
+uint8_t hbm_drv_get_temperature(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev);
+
+#if CONFIG_HL_HBM_STORE_TEST_INFO
+/**
+ * @brief retrieves hardware training status
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ */
+void hbm_drv_get_status(const struct device *dev, uint8_t hbm_dev, uint8_t channel, uint8_t dword,
+			void *res);
+
+/**
+ * @brief run MCBIST
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param hbm_dev: HBM index
+ * @param pattern: enum representing MCBIST configuration pattern
+ * @param res: where to store MCBIST result
+ * @param res_len: length of memory allocated for res
+ */
+int hbm_drv_run_mcbist(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev, const void *cfg, void *res,
+		       unsigned int res_len);
+#endif /* CONFIG_HL_HBM_STORE_TEST_INFO */
+
+/**
+ * @brief resets memory controller
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ */
+void hbm_drv_mc_reset_all(const struct device *dev);
+
+/**
+ * @brief sets debug mode
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param is_debug: debug mode boolean
+ */
+int hbm_drv_set_debug_mode(const struct device *dev, bool is_debug);
+
+/**
+ * @brief get result of hbm init
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ */
+void hbm_drv_init_get_result(const struct device *dev, uint32_t *res);
+
+/**
+ * @brief run MBIST. returns 0 if MBIST completed, 1 otherwise
+ * NOTE: completion doesn't mean pass/fail (see data_out)
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param hbm_dev: HBM index
+ * @param mode: 1-PBT, 2-legacy
+ * @param data_out: holds MBIST output, needed for row repair or for extracting MBIST results
+ */
+int hbm_drv_mbist(int hbm_dev, int mode, void *data_out);
+
+/**
+ * @brief apply row replacement in HBM. returns 0 if operation passed, 1 otherwise
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param hbm_dev: HBM index
+ * @param repair_vec: holds the row location which caller would like the replace
+ * @param repair_vecs_len: Amount of repair vectors
+ * @param repair_type: HARD - permanant. SOFT - expires on HBM reset
+ */
+int hbm_drv_row_repair(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev, struct hl_hbm_repair_vec *repair_vec,
+		       int repair_vecs_len, enum repair_type repair_type);
+
+/**
+ * @brief scan HBM fuse for spare rows per bank
+ * @param hbm_dev: HBM index
+ * @param scan_vec: holds scan command input data: sid, mc_pc, bank
+ * @param total_spare_rows: holds the output, amount of spare rows for the requested bank
+ */
+int hbm_drv_fuse_scan(int hbm_dev, struct hl_hbm_fuse_scan_vec *scan_vec,
+		      uint8_t *total_spare_rows);
+
+/**
+ * @brief convert ASIC MC PC index to HBM device PC index
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ * @param pc_idx: pseudo channel (0-15)
+ */
+int hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel(int pc_idx);
+
+/**
+ * @brief apply HBM lane repair. returns 0 if operation passed, 1 otherwise
+ * @param hbm_dev: HBM index
+ * @param mc_pc: Pseudo channel index from MC POV
+ * @param pc_repair_code: Holds the repair code for mc_pc
+ * @param type: HARD - permanant. SOFT - expires on HBM reset
+ */
+int hbm_drv_lane_repair(int hbm_dev, int mc_pc, uint32_t pc_repair_code, enum repair_type type);
+
+/**
+ * @brief get number of active HBMs
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ */
+int hl_hbm_drv_get_num_active_hbms(const struct device *dev);
+
+/**
+ * @brief configure sram in cache mode for hbm
+ * @param dev: hl_hbm device
+ */
+int hbm_drv_sram_cache_mode(const struct device *dev);
+
+/**
+ * @brief Adds a row replacement entry to rma table after performing all necessary checks
+ * @param row_rep: holds the row_replacement entry that needs to be added to rma table
+ * @param repair_type: HARD - permanant. SOFT - expires on HBM reset
+ */
+int hbm_drv_add_row_rep(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep,
+				enum repair_type repair_type);
+
+#endif /* __HL_HBM_API_H */

b/drivers/hbm/gaudi3/common/common.c

@@ -0,0 +1,122 @@
+#include <common.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(gaudi3_hbm);
+
+int pldm = 0;
+bool hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints = 0;
+// #ifdef TARGET_IS_PLDM
+// pldm = 1;
+// #endif
+
+struct hl_hbm_info hbm_info;
+struct gaudi3_hbm hbm_cfg;
+
+
+enum hbm_pll_freqs freq_to_enum(uint32_t freq)
+{
+	switch (freq) {
+	case 800:
+		return HBM_PLL_800;
+	case 1200:
+		return HBM_PLL_1200;
+	case 1600:
+		return HBM_PLL_1600;
+	case 1800:
+		return HBM_PLL_1800;
+	default:
+		return -EINVAL;
+	}
+}
+
+struct gaudi3_hbm hbm_cfg = {
+	.ecc_enable = 1,
+	.dbi_enable = 1,
+	.dfi_phy_update = 1,
+	.fast_zq_cal = 1,
+	.hbm_io_drive = DRIVE_STRENGTH_12MA,
+	.phy_io_drive = DRIVE_STRENGTH_12MA,
+	.train_cfg.cmd_repeat = CMD_REPEAT_LONG,
+	.train_cfg.data_pattern = LFSR,
+	.train_cfg.training_type = TRAIN_TYPE_SEMI_AUTO,
+};
+
+uint32_t phy_ch_mapping(uint32_t ch)
+{
+	uint32_t phy_map[] = {0, 1, 4, 5, 2, 3, 6, 7};
+	return phy_map[ch];
+}
+
+int phy_poll_on_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc = 0;
+	// uint32_t timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : PHY_TIMEOUT_US;
+	// uint32_t sleep = pldm ? PLDM_POLL_INTERVAL : POLL_INTERVAL_US;
+	uint32_t reg_val = 0;
+
+	// rc = PHY_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS),
+	// 			 reg_val, (reg_val & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINDONE_M),
+	// 			 sleep, timeout);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d Timeout while polling for training FSM completion. TrainStatus: 0x%x",
+			hbm_dev, reg_val);
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int hbm_reset(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val, train_fsm_status_valid;
+
+	reg_val = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINRUN_M |
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_DRAMINITRESET_M;
+	train_fsm_status_valid = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINDONE_M |
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_DRAMINITRSTDONE_M;
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), reg_val);
+
+	/* de-assert TrainRun for future training and/or DRAM init */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), reg_val & ~(1ULL))
+	;
+
+	if (phy_poll_on_training(hbm_dev))
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS));
+	if (reg_val != train_fsm_status_valid) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d - FAILED to reset HBM device. TrainStatus: 0x%x", hbm_dev,
+			    reg_val);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+int hbm_mrs(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val, train_fsm_status_valid;
+
+	reg_val = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINRUN_M |
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_DRAMINITMRS_M;
+	train_fsm_status_valid = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINDONE_M |
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_DRAMINITMRSDONE_M;
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), reg_val);
+
+	/* de-assert TrainRun for future training and/or DRAM init */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), reg_val & ~(1ULL))
+	;
+
+	if (phy_poll_on_training(hbm_dev))
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS));
+	if (reg_val != train_fsm_status_valid) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d - FAILED to set mode registers. TrainStatus: 0x%x", hbm_dev,
+			reg_val);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}

+#define HBM_RREG32(addr, is_poll_needed)({			    \
+	if (is_poll_needed)							        \
+		HL_ARC_W_BARRIER();						        \
+	CFG_RREG32(addr);							        \
+})
+
+#define HBM_RMWREG32(addr, val, mask, is_poll_needed)({	\
+	if (is_poll_needed)							        \
+		HL_ARC_W_BARRIER();						        \
+	CFG_RMWREG32(addr, val, mask);						\
+})
+
+#define HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(addr, val, cond, sleep_us, timeout_us)({		\
+	HL_ARC_W_BARRIER();							\
+	hl_poll_timeout(addr + HL_CFG_BASE, val, cond, sleep_us, timeout_us);	\
+})
+
+#define PHY_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_dev, addr, val, cond, sleep_us, timeout_us)({	\
+	HL_ARC_W_BARRIER();							\
+	phy_poll_timeout(hbm_dev, addr, val, cond, sleep_us, timeout_us);	\
+})

+/*
+ * Patrol scrubbing
+ * 24 hours divided by number of transactions to cover a PC's memory region,
+ * and convert the result to MC cycles (900MHz = 1.111ns)
+ * i.e.:
+ * 86,400sec / (2^30bytes / 256bytes-per-transaction) =  86400/4194304 = 20599365ns
+ * to cycles: 20599365/1.1111111111 = 18539428 = 0x11AE3A4 --> 0x11ABCDE
+ */
+#define PATROL_SCRB_START_ADDR			0x0
+#define PATROL_SCRB_END_ADDR			0x3fffffff
+#define PATROL_SCRB_DLY_CNTR			0x11abcde
+#define PATROL_SCRB_RELOAD_EN			0x1
+
+/* CALCS */
+#define CEIL(numerator, denominator) ((numerator + denominator - 1) / (denominator))
+
+#define MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS	(MBIST_HL_MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS_NUM * HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM)
+
+struct sec_repair_data_reg {
+	uint32_t ra :15;
+	uint32_t rb :4;
+	uint32_t dq_position :1;
+	uint32_t sid : 1;
+	uint32_t reserved :11;
+	uint32_t ch;
+};

+struct phy_ac_params {
+	/* DFI_CFG */
+	uint32_t trd_data_en;		/* RL - 2 */
+	uint32_t tphy_wr_lat;		/* WL - 2 */
+	/* DFI_CFG_2 */
+	uint32_t ctrlupd_max_t;		/* 54 + extend_phased_time */
+	/* MR1 */
+	uint32_t twr;			/* Write recovery (nCK) */
+	/* MR2*/
+	uint32_t wr_lat;
+	uint32_t rd_lat;
+	/* MR4 */
+	uint32_t parity_lat;
+	uint32_t extend_phased_time;	/* lcdlTrainParam CSR */
+	uint32_t t_pwreset;		/* nCK (1000ns/clk_period) */
+};
+
+/* Mode registers are taken from phy_ac_params */
+struct hbm_ac_params {
+	uint32_t twtr_s;
+	uint32_t twtr_l;
+	uint32_t tinit5;
+	uint32_t tmod;
+};

+struct lane_remap_info {
+	uint32_t dw_remap_code[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM][HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM];
+	uint32_t dw_remap_fix_code[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM][HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM];
+	uint32_t aw_remap_code[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM];
+	uint32_t aw_remap_fix_code[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM];
+};

+/**
+ * struct hbm_hw_training - specifies remapped lanes information per HBM channel
+ * @training_type: selector for PHY training algorithm
+ * @train_ca: enable flag for C/A training
+ * @train_rl: enable flag for read latnecy training
+ * @train_rdeye: enable flag for read-eye training
+ * @train_wreye: enable flag for write-eye training
+ * @train_vref: enable flag for VREF training
+ * @mrs: enable flag for HBM mode registers set command
+ * @hbm_reset: enable flag for HBM reset and initialization sequence
+ * @cmd_repeat: number of times an HBM command has to be executed
+ * @data_pattern: 0 for LFSR preset mode, 1 for LFSR mode
+ */
+struct hbm_hw_training {
+	enum hbm_training_type training_type;
+	uint32_t train_ca;
+	uint32_t train_rl;
+	uint32_t train_rdeye;
+	uint32_t train_wreye;
+	uint32_t train_vref;
+	uint32_t mrs;
+	uint32_t hbm_reset;
+	uint8_t cmd_repeat;
+	uint8_t data_pattern;
+};
+
+struct hbm_ch_lane_remap_info {
+	uint8_t aw;
+	uint16_t dw[HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM];
+};
+
+struct phy_indirect_access {
+	uint8_t chiplet;
+	uint8_t pstate;
+	uint32_t offset;
+};
+
+struct pc_err_bitmap {
+	uint64_t dq;
+	uint8_t dm;
+	uint8_t pc_status;
+};
+
+struct gaudi3_hbm {
+	enum hbm_pll_freqs ck_freq;
+	uint8_t ecc_enable;
+	uint8_t dbi_enable;
+	uint8_t dfi_phy_update;
+	uint8_t fast_zq_cal;
+	uint8_t hbm_io_drive;
+	uint8_t phy_io_drive;
+	struct hbm_ac_params hbm_ac_param;
+	struct mc_ac_params mc_ac_param;
+	struct phy_ac_params phy_ac_param;
+	struct hbm_hw_training train_cfg;
+	struct hbm_ch_lane_remap_info slr_info[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM];
+};
+
+struct dram_lane_remap {
+	struct lane_remap header;
+	struct lane_remap_data remap_info[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM * GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS];
+};
+
+/* Global */
+extern struct gaudi3_hbm hbm_cfg;
+extern struct hl_hbm_info hbm_info;
+
+void hbm_phy_write(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t addr, uint32_t val);
+uint32_t hbm_phy_read(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t addr);
+enum hbm_pll_freqs freq_to_enum(uint32_t freq);
+uint32_t phy_ch_mapping(uint32_t ch);
+int phy_poll_on_training(uint32_t hbm_dev);
+int hbm_reset(uint32_t hbm_dev);
+int hbm_mrs(uint32_t hbm_dev);
+
+#endif

+const uint32_t fuse_sid0_bit = 23;
+const uint32_t fuse_sid1_bit = 24;
+
+/*
+ * The fuse array size is 512 vectors:
+ *	8 spare rows per Bank
+ *	16 Banks per PC
+ *	2 PCs per channel
+ *	2 channel groups (group0 -> ch0/1/4/5, group1 -> ch2/3/6/7
+ * Each scan cmd will return 4 vectors for all channels per MC (MC0 -> CH0/1/4/5, MC1 -> CH2/3/6/7)
+ */
+/* DO NOT CHANGE THE ORDER OF THE ARRAY */
+

+
+
+#include <mbist.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_DECLARE(gaudi3_hbm);
+
+/*
+ * Parse MBIST raw output and consruct repair vectors if possible.
+ * @param dev - hl_hbm device
+ * @param hbm_dev - HBM index
+ * @param pattern_idx - PBT pattern index
+ * @param result_row - pointer to the MBIST raw output (IEEE1500 WDR)
+ * @param data_out - pointer to mbist_data_out struct to hold the repair vectors
+ * returns status_fail(1) if MBIST haven't completed, status_pass(0) otherwise.
+ */
+
+
+
+/* shift and sizes are +1 due to garbage bit to be ignored per channel using IEEE1500 */
+#define LEGACY_PATTERNS_NUM_PER_SID			(2)
+#define MBIST_PBT_STATUS_BITS_MASK			(0x3fe)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_STATUS_BITS_MASK			(0xfe)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_PBT_STATUS_BITS_SHIFT			(10)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_PBT_DATA_BITS_SHIFT			(22)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_STATUS_BIT_MASK				(0x1)
+#define MBIST_FAIL_RA_CNT_MASK				(0x3e)
+#define MBIST_PBT_RUN_FAIL_MASK				(0x40)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_RDQS_FAIL_MASK			(0x40)
+#define MBIST_SID_MASK					(0x180)
+#define MBIST_DATA_RA_MASTER_BIT_MASK			(0x1)
+#define MBIST_PBT_DATA_RA_MASK				(0x3ffe)
+#define MBIST_DATA_PBT_FINISH_MASK			(0xc000)
+#define MBIST_PBT_DATA_DQ_POSITION_MASK			(0xffff0000)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_DATA_RB_MASK			(0x1e00)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_DATA_RA_MASK			(0xfffe000)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_DATA_DQ_POSITION_MASK		(0x30000000)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_STATUS_FIRST_BIT			(8)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_REPAIR_VECTOR_SIZE			(23)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_ERROR_CNT_FIRST_BIT		(376)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_ERROR_CNT_SIZE			(20)
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_IGNORE_FIRST_BIT			(396)		/* garbage bit modified */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_IGNORE_SIZE			(80)
+#define MBIST_PBT_BUSY_RETRY_COUNT			(5)
+#define MBIST_PBT_FAIL_RETRY_COUNT			(3)
+#define MBIST_PBT_SCAN_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS		(2500)		/* 2.5s */
+#define MBIST_PBT_MARCH_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS		(1300)		/* 1.3s */
+#define MBIST_PBT_TSV_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS		(300)		/* 0.3s */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_SCAN_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_US		(6800000)	/* 6.8s */
+#define MBIST_LEGACY_MARCH_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_US		(15000000)	/* 15s */
+#define MBIST_DRAM_CLK_ENABLE_DLY_MS			(30)
+
+enum pbt_vec_shift {
+	ba_shift_vec_0 = 1,
+	ba_shift_vec_1 = 5,
+	ba_shift_vec_2 = 9,
+	ba_shift_vec_3 = 13,
+};
+
+/*
+ *MBIST modes:
+ *PBT mode - 10 patterns in total, 2 of them (TSV patterns) are using LEGACY mode
+ *LEGACY mode - 4 patterns in total
+ */
+/* DO NOT CHANGE THE ORDER OF THE STRINGS */
+char pbt_pattern_names[HBM_STACKS_NUM][PBT_PATTERNS_NUM_PER_SID][20] = {{"s0_r14_pbt_march",
+	"s0_r14b_pbt_march", "s0_r14_pbt_scan", "s0_r14b_pbt_scan", "s0_tsv"}, {"s1_r14_pbt_march",
+	"s1_r14b_pbt_march", "s1_r14_pbt_scan", "s1_r14b_pbt_scan", "s1_tsv"}};
+char legacy_pattern_names[HBM_STACKS_NUM][LEGACY_PATTERNS_NUM_PER_SID][20] = {{"s0_legacy_march",
+	"s0_legacy_scan"}, {"s1_legacy_march", "s1_legacy_scan"}};
+
+uint32_t hbm_mbist_config_pattern[] = {0x00000000, 0x00000000, 0x00000000, 0x00000000, 0x00000000,

+static struct hl_hbm_repair_vec mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(struct sec_repair_data_reg sec_repair_dr)
+{
+	struct hl_hbm_repair_vec hl_vec = {0};
+
+	hl_vec.row = sec_repair_dr.ra;
+	hl_vec.bank = sec_repair_dr.rb;
+	hl_vec.pc = (sec_repair_dr.ch << 1) | sec_repair_dr.dq_position;
+	hl_vec.sid = sec_repair_dr.sid;
+
+	return hl_vec;
+}
+
+static void mbist_pbt_mode_parser(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t pattern_idx, uint32_t *result_raw,
+				     struct mbist_data_out *data_out)
+{
+	uint32_t status_data, sid, pbt_run_fail, fail_ra_cnt, mbist_status, phy_ch,
+		 ra_master_bit, ra, pbt_finish, dq_position, ch_regs_count, vec_idx,
+		 vec_cnt = 0, curr_repair_vec;
+	struct sec_repair_data_reg *repair_vec_arr;
+	uint32_t repair_vec_arr_sz = sizeof(struct sec_repair_data_reg) * MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS;
+
+	repair_vec_arr = (struct sec_repair_data_reg *)k_malloc(repair_vec_arr_sz);
+	if (!repair_vec_arr) {
+		LOG_ERR("Memory allocation failed for HBM%d MBIST PBT run", hbm_dev);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	memset(repair_vec_arr, 0x0, sizeof(struct sec_repair_data_reg) * MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS);
+
+	ch_regs_count = CEIL(MBIST_PBT_RESULT_SIZE, BITS_PER_REG32);
+	for (phy_ch = 0; phy_ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; phy_ch++) {
+
+		/*  Parse MBIST output STATUS[8:0] */
+		status_data = FIELD_GET(MBIST_PBT_STATUS_BITS_MASK, *result_raw);
+		mbist_status = FIELD_GET(MBIST_STATUS_BIT_MASK, status_data);
+		fail_ra_cnt = FIELD_GET(MBIST_FAIL_RA_CNT_MASK, status_data);
+		pbt_run_fail = FIELD_GET(MBIST_PBT_RUN_FAIL_MASK, status_data);
+		sid = FIELD_GET(MBIST_SID_MASK, status_data);
+
+		if (mbist_status) {
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_BUSY;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		if (pbt_run_fail) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d MBIST PBT run FAILED", hbm_dev, phy_ch);
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_INTERNAL_FAULT;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		if (fail_ra_cnt > MBIST_SEC_MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS_NUM) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d MBIST SID%d pattern%d detected over 16 defect rows. fail_ra_cnt: 0x%x",
+				hbm_dev, phy_ch, sid, pattern_idx, fail_ra_cnt);
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+			result_raw += ch_regs_count;
+			continue;
+		} else if (fail_ra_cnt == 0) {
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_PASS;
+			result_raw += ch_regs_count;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE;
+
+		/* Fetch repair vectors */
+		LOG_INF("  repair vector : RA Master Bit%s   RA%sPBT Finish%sDQ Position%s\n",
+			SPACES5, SPACES10, SPACES5, SPACES5);
+		LOG_INF("  ------------- : -----------------------------------------------------");
+
+		for (vec_idx = 0; vec_idx < fail_ra_cnt; vec_idx++) {
+			/* Each repair_vec data is stored in 2 consecutive regs 22/10 bit per reg */
+			curr_repair_vec = (uint32_t)((*(result_raw + vec_idx)) >>
+				MBIST_PBT_STATUS_BITS_SHIFT) |
+				(uint32_t)(*(result_raw + vec_idx + 1) <<
+				MBIST_PBT_DATA_BITS_SHIFT);
+			ra_master_bit = FIELD_GET(MBIST_DATA_RA_MASTER_BIT_MASK, curr_repair_vec);
+			ra = FIELD_GET(MBIST_PBT_DATA_RA_MASK, curr_repair_vec);
+			pbt_finish = FIELD_GET(MBIST_DATA_PBT_FINISH_MASK, curr_repair_vec);
+			dq_position = FIELD_GET(MBIST_PBT_DATA_DQ_POSITION_MASK, curr_repair_vec);
+
+			LOG_INF("%svec-%2d%s: %s0x%x%s 0x%03x%s 0x%x%s 0x%x\n",
+				SPACES5, vec_idx, SPACES5, SPACES5, ra_master_bit, SPACES10,
+				ra, SPACES10, pbt_finish, SPACES10, dq_position);
+
+			/* Parse MBIST PBT DR REPAIR_VECTOR[520:9] field and construct
+			 * soft/hard-repair DR, as provided by SEC.
+			 * The constructed DR can be used directly to initiate soft/hard repair
+			 * instruction. However, we construct a new vector in the format of
+			 * hl_hbm_repair_vec so caller can understand the failure location in
+			 * a simple matter.
+			 */
+			repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ch = phy_ch_mapping(phy_ch);
+			/* RA[13:1] directly from SEC repair vector. RA[0] == RA[1] */
+			repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra = ((ra << 1) | (ra & 0x1));
+			if (strstr(pbt_pattern_names[sid][pattern_idx], "r14b") == NULL)
+				repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra |= 0x4000;
+			repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].sid = sid;
+			if (ra_master_bit) {
+				ra = repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra;
+				if (dq_position & BIT(0)) {
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].dq_position = 0;
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].rb =
+						(dq_position & GENMASK(2, 1)) >> ba_shift_vec_0 |
+						(FIELD_GET(BIT(3), dq_position) << 3);
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra = ra;
+					data_out->repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt] =
+						mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt]);
+					vec_cnt++;
+				}
+				if (dq_position & BIT(4)) {
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].dq_position = 0;
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].rb =
+						(dq_position & GENMASK(6, 5)) >> ba_shift_vec_1 |
+						(FIELD_GET(BIT(7), dq_position) << 3) | BIT(2);
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra = ra;
+					data_out->repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt] =
+						mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt]);
+					vec_cnt++;
+
+				}
+				if (dq_position & BIT(8)) {
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].dq_position = 1;
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].rb =
+						(dq_position & GENMASK(10, 9)) >> ba_shift_vec_2 |
+						(FIELD_GET(BIT(11), dq_position) << 3);
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra = ra;
+					data_out->repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt] =
+						mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt]);
+					vec_cnt++;
+				}
+				if (dq_position & BIT(12)) {
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].dq_position = 1;
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].rb =
+						(dq_position & GENMASK(14, 13)) >> ba_shift_vec_3 |
+						(FIELD_GET(BIT(15), dq_position) << 3) | BIT(2);
+					repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt].ra = ra;
+					data_out->repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt] =
+						mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt]);
+					vec_cnt++;
+				}
+			} else {
+				data_out->repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt] =
+					mbist_convert_sec_to_hl(repair_vec_arr[vec_cnt]);
+				vec_cnt++;
+			}
+			LOG_INF("Errors found for HBM%d HBM-CH%d SID%d pattern%d - #repair vectors: %d",
+				hbm_dev, phy_ch, sid, pattern_idx, vec_cnt);
+		}
+		result_raw += ch_regs_count;
+	}
+	data_out->repair_vec_cnt += vec_cnt;
+	k_free(repair_vec_arr);
+}
+/* Wait times are defined by SEC on MBIST manual (Legacy scan/march patterns are not supported) */
+static int mbist_get_wait_time(enum mbist_mode mode, int pattern_idx)
+{
+	if (mode == MBIST_MODE_PBT) {
+		if (pattern_idx < 2)
+			return MBIST_PBT_MARCH_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS;
+		else
+			return MBIST_PBT_SCAN_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS;
+	} else {
+		return MBIST_PBT_TSV_PATTERN_WAIT_TIME_MS;
+	}
+}
+
+static void mbist_legacy_mode_parser(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t pattern_idx, uint32_t *result_raw,
+				    struct mbist_data_out *data_out)
+{
+	uint32_t phy_ch, status_data, rdqs_fail, fail_ra_cnt, mbist_status, ch_regs_count;
+
+	ch_regs_count = CEIL(MBIST_LEGACY_RESULT_SIZE, BITS_PER_REG32);
+
+	for (phy_ch = 0; phy_ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; phy_ch++) {
+		/*  Parse MBIST output STATUS[6:0] */
+		status_data = FIELD_GET(MBIST_LEGACY_STATUS_BITS_MASK, *result_raw);
+		mbist_status = FIELD_GET(MBIST_STATUS_BIT_MASK, status_data);
+		fail_ra_cnt = FIELD_GET(MBIST_FAIL_RA_CNT_MASK, status_data);
+		rdqs_fail = FIELD_GET(MBIST_LEGACY_RDQS_FAIL_MASK, status_data);
+
+		if (mbist_status) {
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_BUSY;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		if (rdqs_fail) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d MBIST RDQS_Fail", hbm_dev, phy_ch);
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_INTERNAL_FAULT;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		if (fail_ra_cnt > MBIST_SEC_MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS_NUM) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d MBIST has over %d defect rows. fail_ra_cnt: 0x%x",
+				hbm_dev, phy_ch, MBIST_SEC_MAX_REPAIR_VECTORS_NUM, fail_ra_cnt);
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+			result_raw += ch_regs_count;
+			continue;
+		} else if (fail_ra_cnt == 0) {
+			data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_PASS;
+			result_raw += ch_regs_count;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d pattern%d err_cnt: %d",
+			hbm_dev, phy_ch, pattern_idx, fail_ra_cnt);
+		/* TSV pattern failure is always UNREPAIRABLE */
+		data_out->completion_status |= MBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+	}
+}
+
+typedef void (*mbist_parser_ptr_t)(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t pattern_idx, uint32_t *result_raw,
+				   struct mbist_data_out *data_out);
+
+static uint32_t mbist_obtain_result(int hbm_dev, int mode, uint32_t pattern_idx,
+				    mbist_parser_ptr_t mbist_parser_func,
+				    struct mbist_data_out *mbist_output)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t *wdr, retry_idx;
+	uint32_t mbist_res[CEIL(MBIST_PBT_RESULT_SIZE, BITS_PER_REG32) * HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM] = {};
+	const int total_wait_time_ms = mbist_get_wait_time(mode, pattern_idx);
+	const int iteration_wait_time_ms = total_wait_time_ms / MBIST_PBT_BUSY_RETRY_COUNT;
+
+	for (retry_idx = 0; retry_idx < MBIST_PBT_BUSY_RETRY_COUNT; retry_idx++) {
+		memset(mbist_output, 0x0, sizeof(struct mbist_data_out));
+		memset(mbist_res, 0x0, sizeof(mbist_res));
+
+		/* Wait for MBIST to finish */
+		k_msleep(iteration_wait_time_ms);
+
+		/* Collect MBIST results for all channels*/
+		wdr = mbist_res;
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_mbist_a6, wir_read, wdr, mode);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST A6 read instruction FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			return MBIST_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+		}
+
+		mbist_parser_func(hbm_dev, pattern_idx, mbist_res, mbist_output);
+
+		/* Once completion status is not busy, we've obtained our results and can return */
+		if (!(mbist_output->completion_status & MBIST_STS_FAIL_BUSY))
+			return MBIST_STS_PASS;
+	}
+
+	LOG_WRN("HBM%d: MBIST timed out after %dms (completion_status 0x%x)",
+		hbm_dev, total_wait_time_ms, mbist_output->completion_status);
+
+	return MBIST_STS_PASS;
+}
+
+static uint32_t hbm_mbist_run(int hbm_dev, int mode, uint32_t sid, uint32_t pattern_idx,
+			      mbist_parser_ptr_t mbist_parser_func,
+			      struct mbist_data_out *mbist_output)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t retry_idx, mc_idx, mc_offset, reg_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), *wdr;
+	/* DO NOT CHANGE THE ORDER OF THE PATTERNS */
+	uint32_t *pbt_patterns_arr[HBM_STACKS_NUM][PBT_PATTERNS_NUM_PER_SID] = {{s0_r14_pbt_march,
+		s0_r14b_pbt_march, s0_r14_pbt_scan, s0_r14b_pbt_scan, s0_tsv}, {s1_r14_pbt_march,
+		s1_r14b_pbt_march, s1_r14_pbt_scan, s1_r14b_pbt_scan, s1_tsv}};
+
+	for (retry_idx = 0; retry_idx < MBIST_PBT_FAIL_RETRY_COUNT; retry_idx++) {
+		/* Disable Dram clock */
+		for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+			mc_offset = hbm_offset + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET;
+			reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH) + mc_offset,
+					     POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH) + mc_offset,
+				reg_val | MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_DRAM_CLK_DISABLE_M);
+		}
+
+		/* Trigger MBIST */
+		wdr = hbm_mbist_config_pattern;
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_mbist_a7, wir_write, wdr, mode);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST A7 write instruction FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			return MBIST_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+		}
+
+		wdr = pbt_patterns_arr[sid][pattern_idx];
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_mbist_a6, wir_only, wdr, mode);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST A6 wir only instruction FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			return MBIST_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+		}
+
+		k_msleep(MBIST_DRAM_CLK_ENABLE_DLY_MS);
+		/* Enable Dram clock */
+		for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+			mc_offset = hbm_offset + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET;
+			reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH) + mc_offset,
+					     POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH) + mc_offset,
+				reg_val & ~MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_DRAM_CLK_DISABLE_M);
+		}
+		k_msleep(MBIST_DRAM_CLK_ENABLE_DLY_MS);
+
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_mbist_a6, write_only, wdr, mode);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST A6 write only instruction FAILED",
+				hbm_dev);
+			return MBIST_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+		}
+
+		rc = mbist_obtain_result(hbm_dev, mode, pattern_idx, mbist_parser_func,
+					 mbist_output);
+		if (rc != MBIST_STS_PASS)
+			return rc;
+
+		if (mbist_output->completion_status &
+		    (MBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE | MBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE)) {
+			LOG_WRN("HBM%d MBIST SID%d pattern%d (%s) failed - 0x%x (try %d/%d)",
+				hbm_dev, sid, pattern_idx, pbt_pattern_names[sid][pattern_idx],
+				mbist_output->completion_status, retry_idx+1,
+				MBIST_PBT_FAIL_RETRY_COUNT);
+		} else {
+			/* Got good results, no need to retry */
+			break;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int hbm_drv_mbist(int hbm_dev, int mode, void *data_out)
+{
+	struct mbist_data_out *mbist_output = (struct mbist_data_out *)(data_out);
+	mbist_parser_ptr_t mbist_parser_func = NULL;
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t sid_idx, pattern_idx, res, patterns_num_per_stack;
+	enum hbm_pll_freqs freq_e;
+
+	freq_e = freq_to_enum(hbm_info.hbm_freq);
+	if (freq_e != HBM_PLL_800) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PLL frequency: %dMHz, MBIST PLL must be set to 800MHz. Aborting..",
+			hbm_dev, hbm_info.hbm_freq);
+		return MBIST_STS_FAIL_PLL_LOCK;
+	}
+
+	switch (mode) {
+	case MBIST_MODE_PBT:
+		patterns_num_per_stack = PBT_PATTERNS_NUM_PER_SID;
+		break;
+	case MBIST_MODE_LEGACY:
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST - Legacy mode is not supported. Aborting..", hbm_dev);
+		return MBIST_STS_INVALID_INPUT;
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d MBIST - Invalid mode provided by caller. Aborting..", hbm_dev);
+		return MBIST_STS_INVALID_INPUT;
+	}
+
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL), 0x0,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL_DEVICEIDREADONRST_M);
+
+	for (sid_idx = 0; sid_idx < HBM_STACKS_NUM; sid_idx++) {
+		for (pattern_idx = 0; pattern_idx < patterns_num_per_stack; pattern_idx++) {
+			if (strstr(pbt_pattern_names[sid_idx][pattern_idx], "tsv") != NULL) {
+				mode = MBIST_MODE_LEGACY;
+				mbist_parser_func = mbist_legacy_mode_parser;
+			} else {
+				mode = MBIST_MODE_PBT;
+				mbist_parser_func = mbist_pbt_mode_parser;
+			}
+
+			res = hbm_mbist_run(hbm_dev, mode, sid_idx, pattern_idx,
+					    mbist_parser_func, mbist_output);
+			if (res != MBIST_STS_PASS)
+				return res;
+
+			LOG_INF("HBM%d MBIST SID%d pattern%d (%s) COMPLETED", hbm_dev, sid_idx,
+				pattern_idx, pbt_pattern_names[sid_idx][pattern_idx]);
+
+			rc = hbm_reset(hbm_dev);
+			if (rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d SID%d MBIST failed to reset device after pattern%d. Aborting..",
+					hbm_dev, sid_idx, pattern_idx);
+				return rc;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	/* For production line monitoring */
+	LOG_INF("HBM%d MBIST result: %s. res = 0x%03x", hbm_dev, res ? "FAIL" : "PASS", res);
+
+	return res;
+}
+

b/drivers/hbm/gaudi3/mc/mc.c

@@ -0,0 +1,800 @@
+#include <mc.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_DECLARE(gaudi3_hbm);
+
+static const uint32_t hbm_order_per_die_val[] = {
+
+	[die0_hbm0] = 0x1,
+	[die0_hbm1] = 0x2,
+	[die0_hbm2] = 0x4,
+	[die0_hbm3] = 0x8,
+	[die1_hbm4] = 0x8,
+	[die1_hbm5] = 0x4,
+	[die1_hbm6] = 0x2,
+	[die1_hbm7] = 0x1,
+};
+static const uint32_t ieee1500_wdr_len_t[] = {
+	[ieee1500_bypass] = 1,				/* BYPASS */
+	[ieee1500_extest_rx] = 215,			/* EXTEST_RX */
+	[ieee1500_extest_tx] = 215,			/* EXTEST_TX */
+	[ieee1500_intest_rx] = 0,			/* INTEST_RX */
+	[ieee1500_intest_tx] = 0,			/* INTEST_TX */
+	[ieee1500_hbm_reset] = 1,			/* HBM_RESET */
+	[ieee1500_mbist] = 375,				/* MBIST */
+	[ieee1500_soft_repair] = 22,			/* SOFT_REPAIR */
+	[ieee1500_hard_repair] = 22,			/* HARD_REPAIR */
+	[ieee1500_dword_misr] = 320,			/* DWORD_MISR */
+	[ieee1500_aword_misr] = 30,			/* AWORD_MISR */
+	[ieee1500_channel_id] = 1,			/* CHANNEL_ID */
+	[ieee1500_misr_mask] = 72,			/* MISR_MASK */
+	[ieee1500_aword_misr_config] = 8,		/* AWORD_MISR_CONFIG */
+	[ieee1500_device_id] = 82,			/* DEVICE_ID */
+	[ieee1500_temperature] = 8,			/* TEMPERATURE */
+	[ieee1500_mode_register_dump_set] = 128,	/* MODE_REGISTER_DUMP_SET */
+	[ieee1500_read_lfsr_compare_sticky] = 175,	/* READ_LFSR_COMPARE_STICKY */
+	[ieee1500_soft_lane_repair] = 72,		/* SOFT_LANE_REPAIR */
+	[ieee1500_hard_lane_repair] = 72,		/* HARD_LANE_REPAIR */
+	[ieee1500_mbist_a6] = 2077,			/* MBIST_A6 */
+	[ieee1500_mbist_a7] = 301,			/* MBIST_A7 */
+	[ieee1500_tmrs_gen] = 37,			/* TMRS_GEN */
+	[ieee1500_fuse_scan] = 26,			/* ROWS_FUSE_SCAN */
+};
+
+static int mc_p1500_get_wdr_len(enum ieee1500_instruction inst, enum ieee1500_op op,
+				uint32_t mbist_mode)
+{
+	int wdr_len;
+
+	/*
+	 * In case of MBIST read cmd wdr len depends on the mbist mode, legacy/pbt.
+	 * In case of WIR only cmd wdr len is a don't care, set to 0
+	 * In any other case the wdr len depends only on the instruction as defined in the spec.
+	 */
+	if ((op == wir_read || op == read_only) && inst == ieee1500_mbist_a6) {
+		wdr_len = (mbist_mode == MBIST_MODE_PBT) ? MBIST_PBT_RESULT_SIZE :
+			MBIST_LEGACY_RESULT_SIZE;
+	} else
+		wdr_len = ieee1500_wdr_len_t[inst];
+
+	/*
+	 * PLDM bug, IEEE1500 returns -1 bit (MSB) unless WDR len is configured +1
+	 * TODO - remove once solved by cadence
+	 */
+	if (pldm)
+		wdr_len++;
+
+	return wdr_len;
+}
+
+int mc_p1500_inst(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t ch, enum ieee1500_instruction inst,
+			 enum ieee1500_op op, uint32_t *wdr_ptr, uint32_t mbist_mode)
+{
+	/*
+	 * Before calling this function a WDR reg/s should be declared as follow:
+	 * Write instructions - WDR registers should be written prior to calling this function
+	 * Read instructions - WDR registers should be read after calling this function
+	 * WIR only instructions - WDR is not needed
+	 */
+	uint64_t timeout = (pldm) ? (PLDM_TIMEOUT) : PHY_TIMEOUT_US;
+	int remain_wdr_bits;
+	uint32_t rc, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), reg_val = 0, wdr_ptr_idx = 0, reg_poll,
+		 trig_bit, sleep = (pldm) ? PLDM_POLL_INTERVAL : POLL_INTERVAL_US, wir_conf_ch,
+		 wdr_regs_count, wdr_regs_total, wso_ch_offset, wdr_idx, ch_idx, auto_temp_val,
+		 wso_offset, wdr_offset, wso_addr, cmd_addr;
+
+	/*
+	 * Check if auto temp is enabled (since it is using ieee1500 bus) and if so fail the current
+	 * ieee1500 cmd to avoid collision on the ieee1500 bus
+	 */
+	auto_temp_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset,
+				   POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	if (auto_temp_val & HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP_ENABLE_M) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d IEEE15000 instruction FAILED - auto temperature is enabled\n",
+			hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	cmd_addr = D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD);
+	wso_addr = D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_WSO_DATA_0);
+
+	remain_wdr_bits = mc_p1500_get_wdr_len(inst, op, mbist_mode);
+	wdr_regs_total = CEIL(remain_wdr_bits, BITS_PER_REG32);
+
+	/* Initial trig bit is set to start and then configured according to OP and INST */
+	trig_bit = HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_START_M;
+	/* In case of fuse scan only broadcast is allowed as ch inst for both cmds wir read/write */
+	wir_conf_ch = (inst == ieee1500_fuse_scan ? BROADCAST_CH : ch);
+	/* Configure WIR[11:0] - {ch[11:8], instruction[7:0]} */
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_WIR_M,
+			      inst | (wir_conf_ch << P1500_INST_CMD_WIR_CH_S));
+	/* Configure the OP - WIR only / WIR read / WIR read only / WIR write / WIR write only */
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_OP_M, op);
+
+	/* Handle the ieee1500 cmd according to OP */
+	if (op == wir_only) {
+		/* trigger IEEE1500 transaction with no WDR use in case of WIR only cmd */
+		reg_val |= trig_bit;
+		/* No WDR is needed for WIR only cmd hence, we need to set last data bit */
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_LAST_DATA_M, 0x1);
+
+		CFG_WREG32(cmd_addr + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+		/* Polling on start bit, should be set to 0 once ieee1500 cmd is done */
+		rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_offset + cmd_addr, reg_poll,
+					 ((reg_poll & HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_START_M) == 0x0),
+					 sleep, timeout);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d IEEE15000 instruction code: 0x%x op code: 0x%x failed\n",
+				hbm_dev, inst, op);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+	} else if (op == wir_write || op == write_only) {
+		while (remain_wdr_bits > 0) {
+			/* Set WDR length to 0 and reconfigure WDR length */
+			reg_val &= ~HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_WDR_LEN_M;
+			reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_WDR_LEN_M,
+					      MIN(remain_wdr_bits, MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX) - 1);
+
+			wdr_regs_count = CEIL(MIN(remain_wdr_bits, MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX),
+					      BITS_PER_REG32);
+			/* Loading WDR via WSI regs */
+			for (wdr_idx = 0; wdr_idx < wdr_regs_count; wdr_idx++) {
+				CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_WSI_DATA_0) +
+					   hbm_offset + wdr_idx * BYTE_OFFSET,
+					   *(wdr_ptr + wdr_ptr_idx));
+				wdr_ptr_idx++;
+			}
+
+			/*
+			 * In case we are left with less then 128 bit(max ieee1500 bus size)
+			 * need to set last data cmd bit
+			 */
+			if (remain_wdr_bits <= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX)
+				reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_LAST_DATA_M, 0x1);
+
+			/* trigger IEEE1500 transaction after loading WDR using WSI DATA regs */
+			reg_val |= trig_bit;
+			CFG_WREG32(cmd_addr + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+			/* Polling on start/pause bit in case less/more then 128 bits remained */
+			rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_offset + cmd_addr, reg_poll,
+					((remain_wdr_bits <= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX) ?
+					((reg_poll & HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_START_M) == 0x0) :
+					((FIELD_GET(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_PAUSE_M,
+					reg_poll)) == 0x1)), sleep, timeout);
+
+			if (rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d IEEE15000 instruction code: 0x%x op code: 0x%x failed\n",
+					hbm_dev, inst, op);
+				return STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+			trig_bit = HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_RESUME_M;
+			remain_wdr_bits -= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX;
+		}
+	} else if (op == wir_read || op == read_only) {
+		while (remain_wdr_bits > 0) {
+			/* Set WDR length to 0 and reconfigure WDR length */
+			reg_val &= ~HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_WDR_LEN_M;
+			reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_WDR_LEN_M,
+					      MIN(remain_wdr_bits, MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX) - 1);
+
+			wdr_regs_count = CEIL(MIN(remain_wdr_bits, MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX),
+					      BITS_PER_REG32);
+			/*
+			 * In case we are left with less then 128 bit(max ieee1500 bus size)
+			 * need to set last data cmd bit
+			 */
+			if (remain_wdr_bits <= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX)
+				reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_LAST_DATA_M, 0x1);
+
+			/* trigger IEEE1500 transaction before reading WDR using WSO DATA regs */
+			reg_val |= trig_bit;
+			CFG_WREG32(cmd_addr + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+			/* Polling on start/pause bit in case less/more then 128 bits remained */
+			rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_offset + cmd_addr, reg_poll,
+					((remain_wdr_bits <= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX) ?
+					((reg_poll & HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_START_M) == 0x0) :
+					((FIELD_GET(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_PAUSE_M,
+					reg_poll)) == 0x1)), sleep, timeout);
+
+			if (rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d IEEE15000 instruction code: 0x%x op code: 0x%x failed\n",
+					hbm_dev, inst, op);
+				return STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+
+			/*
+			 * IEEE1500 WSO output, 128bit in 4 regs, are per channel
+			 * 0/1/2/3-ch0, 4/5/6/7-ch1.... 28/29/30/31-ch7
+			 * In order to arrange the data per channel in sequence the readout is in
+			 * wdr_regs_count steps.
+			 * read command is up to wdr size output u32 regs.
+			 * fuse_scan cmd will give 4 vectors as output per 1 read cmd, 1 for each
+			 * channel per channel group(0/1/4/5, 2/3/6/7). reading only 1 out of 4
+			 * outputs according to ch input givent in the original cmd.
+			 */
+			if (inst == ieee1500_fuse_scan) {
+				wso_ch_offset = hbm_offset + ch * 4 * BYTE_OFFSET;
+				wdr_ptr[wdr_ptr_idx] = HBM_RREG32(wso_addr + wso_ch_offset,
+								  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+			}
+
+			/* Specific channel */
+			else if (ch != BROADCAST_CH) {
+				wso_ch_offset = hbm_offset + ch * 4 * BYTE_OFFSET;
+				for (wdr_idx = 0; wdr_idx < wdr_regs_count; wdr_idx++) {
+					wso_offset = wso_ch_offset + wdr_idx * BYTE_OFFSET;
+					wdr_offset = wdr_ptr_idx + wdr_idx;
+					*((uint32_t *)(wdr_ptr + wdr_offset)) =
+								HBM_RREG32(wso_addr + wso_offset,
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+				}
+			}
+			/* All channels (broadcast) */
+			else {
+				/* Read values in order by ch: 0,1...7  */
+				for (ch_idx = 0; ch_idx < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch_idx++) {
+					wso_ch_offset = hbm_offset + ch_idx * 4 * BYTE_OFFSET;
+					for (wdr_idx = 0; wdr_idx < wdr_regs_count; wdr_idx++) {
+						wso_offset = wso_ch_offset + wdr_idx * BYTE_OFFSET;
+						wdr_offset = wdr_ptr_idx + wdr_idx +
+							     ch_idx * wdr_regs_total;
+						*((uint32_t *)(wdr_ptr + wdr_offset)) =
+								HBM_RREG32(wso_addr + wso_offset,
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			wdr_ptr_idx += wdr_regs_count;
+			trig_bit = HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_INST_CMD_RESUME_M;
+			remain_wdr_bits -= MC_P1500_WDR_SIZE_MAX;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static uint32_t mc_p1500_trigger_soft_lane_repair(int hbm_dev, uint32_t ch, uint32_t *dw_remap_info,
+			  uint32_t aw_remap_info)
+{
+	uint32_t wdr[HBM_REMAP_REGS_SIZE], rc;
+
+	/*
+	 * remap data is 72 bits (3 wdr registers) aranged as follow:
+	 * dw0 -> wdr[0][0:15]
+	 * dw1 -> wdr[0][16:31]
+	 * aw -> wdr[1][32:39], COL-[32:35] ROW-[39:36]
+	 * dw2 -> wdr[1][40:55]
+	 * dw3 -> wdr[1][56:63], wdr[2][64:71]
+	 * hence the following parsing for 1 aword and 4 dwords per channel
+	 */
+
+	wdr[0] = dw_remap_info[0] | FIELD_PREP(0xffff0000, dw_remap_info[1]);
+	wdr[1] = FIELD_PREP(WDR_LANE_REPIAR_AW_ROW_REMAP,
+			    FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_ROWREMAP_M,
+			    aw_remap_info)) |
+		 FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_COLREMAP_M, aw_remap_info);
+	wdr[1] |= FIELD_PREP(0xffff00, dw_remap_info[2]) | FIELD_PREP(0xff000000, dw_remap_info[3]);
+	wdr[2] = FIELD_GET(0xff00, dw_remap_info[3]);
+
+	rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, ch, ieee1500_soft_lane_repair, wir_write, wdr, 0);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d: IEEE1500 soft lane repair write instruction FAILED",
+			hbm_dev, ch);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+uint32_t align_hbm_lane_remap(int hbm_dev, uint32_t ch, struct lane_remap_info *remap_info)
+{
+	uint32_t rc, offset, dw;
+
+	for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+		if (remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch][dw] != DW_DEFAULT_REMAP_CODE) {
+			LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d valid remap code:0x%x, updating PHY remap reg",
+				hbm_dev, ch, dw, remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch][dw]);
+			offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+				      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP)
+				      + offset, remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch][dw]);
+		}
+	}
+
+	if (remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch] != AW_DEFAULT_REMAP_CODE) {
+		offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP)
+			      + offset, remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch]);
+	}
+
+	rc = mc_p1500_trigger_soft_lane_repair(hbm_dev, ch, remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch],
+					       remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch]);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d PHY IO-DC soft lane repair after EXTEST FAILED", hbm_dev, ch);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+	LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d PHY IO-DC soft lane repair after EXTEST DONE", hbm_dev, ch);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+int mc_mrs_cmd(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t mc_idx, uint8_t mr_num, uint8_t mr_val)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t poll1, poll2, poll3, val, timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : MC_MRS_US;
+	uint32_t auto_pwr_mode, mc_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev) + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET;
+
+	/* Temporarily disable AutoPwr modes */
+	auto_pwr_mode = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AUTO_PWR_EN) + mc_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AUTO_PWR_EN) + mc_offset, 0x0);
+
+	/* Setup the MC MRS register */
+	val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_SEQ_MRS_CMD_NUM_M, mr_num) |
+	      FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_SEQ_MRS_CMD_VAL_M, mr_val);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MRS) + mc_offset, val);
+
+	/* Verify there are no background operations by MC */
+	rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MRS_COMMAND_TRIG) + mc_offset, poll1,
+				   ((poll1 & MC_CH_SEQ_MRS_COMMAND_TRIG_VAL_M) == 0),
+				   MC_MRS_INTERVAL_US, timeout);
+
+	rc |= HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_PWR_COMMAND_TRIG) + mc_offset, poll2,
+				  ((poll2 & MC_CH_SEQ_PWR_COMMAND_TRIG_VAL_M) == 0),
+				  MC_MRS_INTERVAL_US, timeout);
+
+	rc |= HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_PWR) + mc_offset, poll3,
+				  ((poll3 & MC_CH_SEQ_PWR_STATE_M) == 0), MC_MRS_INTERVAL_US,
+				  timeout);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("MC MRS command FAILED (HBM%d MC%d) ", hbm_dev, mc_idx);
+		LOG_ERR("- Timeout while polling for idle state: MRS_COMMAND_TRIG=0x%08x, ",
+			    poll1);
+		LOG_ERR("PWR_COMMAND_TRIG=0x%08x, PWR_STATE=0x%08x\n", poll2, poll3);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	/* Trigger MRS command */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MRS_COMMAND_TRIG) + mc_offset, 0x1);
+
+	/* Poll for MRS completion */
+	rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MRS_COMMAND_TRIG) + mc_offset, poll1,
+				 ((poll1 & MC_CH_SEQ_MRS_COMMAND_TRIG_VAL_M) == 0),
+				 MC_MRS_INTERVAL_US, timeout);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("MC MRS command FAILED (HBM%d MC%d) ", hbm_dev, mc_idx);
+		LOG_ERR("- Timeout while polling for MRS completion: MRS_COMMAND_TRIG=0x%08x\n",
+		       poll1);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	/* Restore AutoPwr mode */
+	if (auto_pwr_mode)
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AUTO_PWR_EN) + mc_offset, auto_pwr_mode);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+
+/* mc config functions */
+static void mc_axis_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_AXIS_CLIP_SIZE_WCLIP_0_M, 0x3c);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_AXIS_CLIP_SIZE_WCLIP_1_M, 0x3c);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXIS_CLIP_SIZE), reg_val);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_ECC_CFG), hbm_cfg.ecc_enable);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_REG(mmMC_CMN_HCON_AXIS_RD_MAX_INFLIGHT), 0xc8);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_STALL_CNT_FORCE_TH), 0x20);
+	/*
+	 * due to HW issue , when there is a high rate of partial writes (many concurrent RMW
+	 * operations), the read response path can get blocked which leads to a deadlock.
+	 * proposed WA is to decrease the amount of write credits by 2. SW-154537.
+	 */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_REG(mmMC_CMN_HCON_AXIS_WR_MAX_CRED),
+		   HBM_WR_MAX_CRED);
+}
+

+
+static void mc_sequencer_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t i, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TWTR_S),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_s);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TWTR_L),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_l);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TWTP),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtp);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRTW),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtw);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRAS),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tras);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRTP),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtp);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRP),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trp);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRRD_L),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_l);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRRD_S),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_s);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TFAW),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tfaw);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TCCD_L),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_l);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TCCD_R),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_r);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRCD_RD),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_rd);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRCD_WR),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_wr);
+
+	for (i = 0; i < MC_TREFI_REGS_NUM; i++) {
+		CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFI_0) +
+					(i * BYTE_OFFSET), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[i]);
+	}
+
+	for (i = 0; i < MC_TREFISB_ADJ_REGS_NUM; i++) {
+		CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFISB_ADJ_0) +
+					(i * BYTE_OFFSET), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[i]);
+	}
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFI_TH), 0x3e8);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRFC), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfc);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRFCSB),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfcsb);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TWTRAP),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtrap);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TCKSRE),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksre);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TCKSRX),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksrx);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TCKPDE),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tckpde);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TXS), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txs);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TXP), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txp);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRREFD),
+								hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrefd);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TMOD), hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tmod);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REFSB_CNT_URGENT_TH), 0x8);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REFSB_BURST_NUM_URGENT), 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REFSB_CNT_HYSTERIC_TH), 0x1c);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REFSB_BURST_NUM_HYSTERIC), 0x14);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_CATTRIP_THROT_EN), 0x1);
+	/* C bus queue */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_CBQ_FIFO_FULL_TH), 0xa);
+}
+
+static void mc_dfi_master_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	uint32_t reg_val, cke_flip_val;
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_MC_CHANNEL_CFG_PC1_CBUS_CYC_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_MC_CHANNEL_CFG_T_AXI_WR_CYC_M, 0x2);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_MC_CHANNEL_CFG), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_PC_GRANT_SEL_VEC_M, 0xa69);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_PC_GRANT_ENABLE_VEC_M, 0xfff);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_PC_GRANT_ENABLE_VEC_M, 0xfff);
+	/* Keep CKE on it's current cfg asserted/not for MC reset without HBM Init from start */
+	cke_flip_val = FIELD_GET(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_DFI_TRIGGER_CKE_FLIP_M,
+	 HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH), POLL_WR_INFLIGHT));
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_DFI_TRIGGER_CKE_FLIP_M, cke_flip_val);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_DFI_ECC_ENABLE_M, hbm_cfg.ecc_enable);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_PARITY_RL_M, 0x2);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_T_RD_DATA_EN_M, hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.trd_data_en);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_T_PHY_WR_DATA_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_T_PHY_WR_LAT_M, hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.tphy_wr_lat);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_RD_DQ_PARITY_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_WR_DBI_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_RD_DBI_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_PHY_UPDATE_DLY_T_M, 0x5);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_CTRLUPD_MAX_T_M,
+			     hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.ctrlupd_max_t);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_CA_PARITY_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_WR_DQ_PARITY_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	/* Disable address XOR in PLDM mode */
+	if (!pldm)
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_ADDRESS_XOR_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_MC_PHY_UPD_INTERVAL_M, 0x3e8);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_PHY_RESET_DONE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_LP_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_LP_WAKEUP_M, 0xf);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2), reg_val);
+}
+
+/* Those configurations are done according to SCRAMBLING NOC_SOL_Setting revision 0.10 */
+static void mc_addr_map_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), ba_0, ba_1, col_3, col_4;
+
+	/*
+	 * Modify BA_0/1 and COL_3/4 is done according to 8/7 active HBM devices (binning) or to
+	 * single die mode for PLDM
+	 */
+	if (hbm_info.num_active_hbm == GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS - 1) {
+		ba_0 = 0xa;
+		ba_1 = 0xb;
+		col_3 = 0x8;
+		col_4 = 0x9;
+	} else {
+		ba_0 = 0x8;
+		ba_1 = 0xa;
+		col_3 = 0x9;
+		col_4 = 0xb;
+	}
+
+	/* Banks: BA[0-1] -> BA[0-1], BA[2-3] -> BG[0-1] */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_0), ba_0);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_1), ba_1);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_0), 0xc);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_1), 0xf);
+
+	/* Columns: COL[0-2] are kept at reset value - 0x5/0x6/0x7 */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_3), col_3);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_4), col_4);
+
+	/* Rows */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_0), 0xd);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_1), 0x10);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_2), 0x11);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_3), 0x12);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_4), 0x13);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_5), 0x14);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_6), 0x15);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_7), 0x16);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_8), 0x17);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_9), 0x18);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_10), 0x19);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_11), 0x1a);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_12), 0x1b);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_13), 0x1c);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_14), 0x1d);
+
+	/* SID: SID[1] is kept in reset value - 0x3f*/
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_0), 0xe);
+}
+
+void mc_config(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	mc_axis_init(hbm_dev);
+	mc_scheduler_init(hbm_dev);
+	mc_sequencer_init(hbm_dev);
+	mc_dfi_master_init(hbm_dev);
+	mc_addr_map_init(hbm_dev);
+}
+
+/* end of mc config functions */
+
+static void mc_enable_interrupts(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t i, cmn_offset, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Clear SEI & SPI Interrupts - clear on read */
+	for (i = 0; i < NUM_CMN_PER_HBM; i++) {
+		cmn_offset = hbm_offset + (i * HBM_CMN_OFFSET);
+		HBM_RREG32(cmn_offset + D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_STATUS),
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+		HBM_RREG32(cmn_offset + D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_STATUS),
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+		HBM_RREG32(cmn_offset + D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SPI_STATUS),
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	}
+
+	/* Enable Interrupts */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_ECC_SEC_THLD), 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_MASK), 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_MASK), 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SPI_MASK),
+		   MC_CMN_INTR_SPI_MASK_IEEE1500_OP_COMPLETE_M |
+		   MC_CMN_INTR_SPI_MASK_IEEE1500_OP_PAUSED_M);
+}
+
+static void dfi_enable_cattrip(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2) + hbm_offset,
+										POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	reg_val |= MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_CATTRIP_ENABLE_M;
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2), reg_val);
+}
+
+void open_traffic(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	if (hbm_cfg.dfi_phy_update)
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DFIPHYUPD), 0xf);
+
+	dfi_enable_cattrip(hbm_dev);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE), 0x2);
+
+	mc_enable_interrupts(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Secured + Privilliged */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_APB_PPROT_PRIV_ONLY) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+}
+
+
+/* This function is raising write protect for the HBM eFuse via the ieee1500 interface */
+void hbm_efuse_write_protect(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t wdr[2];
+
+	/* TMRS Safety Key */
+	wdr[0] = 0x0;
+	wdr[1] = 0x1e;
+	mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_tmrs_gen, wir_write, wdr, MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+	k_busy_wait(MC_P1500_EFUSE_WP_US);
+
+	/* TMRS B-065 (TMRS lock) */
+	wdr[0] = 0x08020001;
+	wdr[1] = 0x10;
+	mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, 0x0, ieee1500_tmrs_gen, wir_write, wdr, MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+	k_busy_wait(MC_P1500_EFUSE_WP_US);
+
+	/* TMRS B-454 (Prevent base die rupture) */
+	wdr[0] = 0x04010010;
+	wdr[1] = 0x10;
+	mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, 0x0, ieee1500_tmrs_gen, wir_write, wdr, MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+	k_busy_wait(MC_P1500_EFUSE_WP_US);
+
+	/* Bypass */
+	wdr[0] = 0x0;
+	wdr[1] = 0x0;
+	mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, BROADCAST_CH, ieee1500_bypass, wir_write, wdr, MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+	k_busy_wait(MC_P1500_EFUSE_WP_US);
+}
+
+/* hbm api functions */
+
+void mc_reset(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t rst_idx, die_offset = (hbm_dev / GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS_PER_DIE) * DIE_OFFSET;
+
+	if (pldm)
+		return;
+
+	/* Clear all PSOC Reset configuration units to avoid doing reset to other units */
+	for (rst_idx = 0; rst_idx <= PSOC_RST_UNIT_NUM; rst_idx++)
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_RESET_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_RESET_CONF_PSOC_UNIT_RST) + die_offset +
+			   rst_idx * BYTE_OFFSET, 0x0);
+
+	/* Reset MC */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_RESET_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_RESET_CONF_HBM_MC_UNIT_RST) + die_offset,
+		   hbm_order_per_die_val[hbm_dev]);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_RESET_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_RESET_CONF_UNIT_RST_N) + die_offset, 0x1);
+	k_msleep(MC_RST_DELAY_MS);
+
+	/* Clear reset */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_RESET_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_RESET_CONF_UNIT_RST_N) + die_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_RESET_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_RESET_CONF_HBM_MC_UNIT_RST) + die_offset, 0x0);
+}
