b/drivers/hbm/gaudi3/mcbist/mcbist.c

@@ -0,0 +1,1426 @@
+
+/*
+ * Checks that:
+ *  1. The proposed remap does not contain invalid code (0xaa) which means more than one lane
+ *     to repair within a byte pair
+ *  2. The proposed remap is consistent with the current for every byte pair, unless either of the
+ *     remaps is a "NOP" (code 0xFF)
+ */
+
+#include <mcbist.h>
+LOG_MODULE_DECLARE(gaudi3_hbm);
+
+#ifndef HBM_DBG
+#define HBM_DBG(...)								    \
+	do {									            \
+		if (hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)					    \
+			LOG_INF(__VA_ARGS__);					    \
+		else								            \
+			LOG_DBG(__VA_ARGS__);					    \
+	} while (0)
+#endif
+
+static struct dram_lane_remap lane_remap_data = {};
+
+static void add_lane_remap_info(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t mc_pc)
+{
+	uint8_t num_remap = lane_remap_data.header.num_remaps;
+
+	if (DRIVER_SLR_EN)
+		return;
+
+	if (num_remap >= ARRAY_SIZE(lane_remap_data.remap_info)) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Out of memory to store lane remap info. num_remap=%d dev=%d mc_pc=%d",
+			__func__, num_remap, hbm_dev, mc_pc);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("%s: num_remap=%d dev=%d mc_pc=%d", __func__, num_remap, hbm_dev, mc_pc);
+
+	lane_remap_data.remap_info[num_remap].ln_hbm_mc_pc = mc_pc;
+	lane_remap_data.remap_info[num_remap].ln_hbm_dev = hbm_dev;
+	lane_remap_data.header.num_remaps++;
+}
+
+
+static uint32_t pc_to_dw(uint32_t pc)
+{
+	return (pc % 2 == 0) ? 0 : 2;
+}
+
+static int change_dbi(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t dbi_mode)
+{
+	struct mrs_cmd mr0 = {.num = 0, .val = 0}, mr4 = {.num = 4, .val = 0};
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), mc_idx;
+	uint8_t  pl = dbi_mode ? 2 : 3;
+
+	uint32_t reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+				D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0));
+
+	uint32_t rd_par = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_READDQPARITY_M,
+				    reg_val);
+	uint32_t wr_par = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDQPARITY_M,
+				    reg_val);
+	uint32_t ca_par = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_ADDRESSPARITY_M,
+				    reg_val);
+	uint32_t ecc = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_ECC_M, reg_val);
+	uint32_t dm = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDATAMASK_M, reg_val);
+	uint32_t ext_rl = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_EXTREADLATENCY_M,
+				    reg_val);
+	uint32_t ext_wl = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_EXTWRITELATENCY_M,
+				    reg_val);
+
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_RD_DBI_M, dbi_mode);
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_WR_DBI_M, dbi_mode);
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_TCSR_M, 0x1);
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_RD_PAR_M, rd_par);
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_WR_PAR_M, wr_par);
+	mr0.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR0_CA_PAR_M, ca_par);
+	mr4.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR4_ECC_M, ecc);
+	mr4.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR4_DM_M, dm);
+	mr4.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR4_PAR_LAT_M, pl);
+	mr4.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR4_EXT_WR_M, ext_wl);
+	mr4.val |= FIELD_PREP(MR4_EXT_RD_M, ext_rl);
+
+	for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+		if (mc_mrs_cmd(hbm_dev, mc_idx, mr0.num, mr0.val)) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MC%d change DBI mode FAILED - MRS (MR0) command failure",
+				    hbm_dev, mc_idx);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		if (mc_mrs_cmd(hbm_dev, mc_idx, mr4.num, mr4.val)) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MC%d change DBI mode FAILED - MRS (MR4) command failure",
+				    hbm_dev, mc_idx);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+	}
+
+	/* Set DBI Mode and Parity Latency in PHY */
+	reg_val &= ~(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_READDBI_M |
+		      HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDBI_M);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_READDBI_M, dbi_mode);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDBI_M, dbi_mode);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0),
+		      reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			       D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0));
+	reg_val &= ~HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0_PARITYLATENCY_M;
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0_PARITYLATENCY_M, pl);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0),
+		      reg_val);
+
+	/* Set DBI Mode and Parity Latency in DFI Master (single cfg common for all channels) */
+	reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG) + hbm_offset,
+			     POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	reg_val &= ~(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_WR_DBI_ENABLE_M |
+		     MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_RD_DBI_ENABLE_M |
+		     MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_PARITY_RL_M);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_WR_DBI_ENABLE_M, dbi_mode);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_RD_DBI_ENABLE_M, dbi_mode);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_PARITY_RL_M, pl);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG) + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d DBI mode changed to %d", hbm_dev, dbi_mode);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+
+static void mc_clear_interrupts(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t cmn_idx, pc_idx, cmn_offset, pc_offset, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Clear Interrupts Status registers (clear on read) */
+	for (cmn_idx = 0; cmn_idx < NUM_CMN_PER_HBM; cmn_idx++) {
+		cmn_offset = hbm_offset + (cmn_idx * HBM_CMN_OFFSET);
+		HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_STATUS) + cmn_offset,
+									POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+		HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_STATUS) + cmn_offset,
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	}
+
+	/* Clear Interrupts counterts and resume recording - WA due to HW bug, Jira [H9-2348] */
+	for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc_idx++) {
+		pc_offset = pc_idx * PC_OFFSET;
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_ERR_CTL_0) + pc_offset, RST_INTR_CNT);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_ERR_CTL_0) + pc_offset,
+			   RESUME_INTR_REC);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_ERR_CTL_0) + pc_offset, 0x0);
+	}
+}
+
+static void phy_pubmode(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum pubmode state)
+{
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PUBMODE), state);
+}
+
+static bool is_valid_repair(uint32_t current_remap, uint32_t proposed_remap)
+{
+	uint32_t pair_idx, mask_offset;
+	uint8_t curr_pair, proposed_pair;
+
+	for (pair_idx = 0; pair_idx < BYTE_PAIRS_PER_PC; pair_idx++) {
+		mask_offset = pair_idx * 8;
+		curr_pair = FIELD_GET(BYTE_M << mask_offset, current_remap);
+		proposed_pair = FIELD_GET(BYTE_M << mask_offset, proposed_remap);
+		if (proposed_pair == INVALID_REMAP_CODE ||
+		   (proposed_pair != curr_pair && proposed_pair != NOP_REMAP_CODE &&
+		    curr_pair != NOP_REMAP_CODE))
+			return false;
+	}
+	return true;
+}
+
+static bool is_repair_code_mode2_valid(uint32_t pc_repair_code)
+{
+	uint8_t curr_bpair;
+	uint32_t bpair_idx;
+
+	for (bpair_idx = 0; bpair_idx < BYTE_PAIRS_PER_PC; bpair_idx++) {
+		curr_bpair = FIELD_GET(0xFF << (8 * bpair_idx), pc_repair_code);
+		if (curr_bpair == JEDEC_NO_LANE_REMAP_CODE)
+			continue;
+		/* DM or DQ0-7 or DBI requires repair */
+		else if (FIELD_GET(0x0F, curr_bpair) == JEDEC_MODE2_ADJCNT_BYTE_CODE &&
+			IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(0xF0, curr_bpair), LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DM,
+				    LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DQ7))
+			continue;
+		else if (FIELD_GET(0xF0, curr_bpair) == JEDEC_MODE2_ADJCNT_BYTE_CODE &&
+			IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(0x0F, curr_bpair), LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DM,
+				    LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DQ7))
+			continue;
+		/* All other option are invalid */
+		return false;
+	}
+
+	return true;
+}
+
+static bool is_dw_bpair_repair_code_mode2_valid(uint32_t dw_bpair_repair_code)
+{
+	/* No repair is required */
+	if (dw_bpair_repair_code == JEDEC_NO_LANE_REMAP_CODE)
+		return true;
+
+	/* DM or DQ0-7 or DBI requires repair */
+	if (FIELD_GET(0x0f, dw_bpair_repair_code) == JEDEC_MODE2_ADJCNT_BYTE_CODE &&
+		IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(0xf0, dw_bpair_repair_code), LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DM,
+				LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DQ7))
+		return true;
+	if (FIELD_GET(0xf0, dw_bpair_repair_code) == JEDEC_MODE2_ADJCNT_BYTE_CODE &&
+		IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(0x0f, dw_bpair_repair_code), LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DM,
+				LANE_REPAIR_CODE_DQ7))
+		return true;
+	/* All other option are invalid */
+	return false;
+}
+
+static bool is_aw_row_repair_code_valid(uint32_t aw_repair_code)
+{
+	if (FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_ROWREMAP_M, aw_repair_code) == 0xf)
+		return true;
+
+	/* Row bus requires repair */
+	if (IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_ROWREMAP_M, aw_repair_code),
+			LANE_REPAIR_CODE_ROW_BUS_LANE0, LANE_REPAIR_CODE_ROW_BUS_LANE6))
+		return true;
+
+	/* All other option are invalid */
+	return false;
+}
+
+static bool is_aw_col_repair_code_valid(uint32_t aw_repair_code)
+{
+	if (FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_COLREMAP_M, aw_repair_code) == 0xf)
+		return true;
+
+	/* Column bus requires repair */
+	if (IN_RANGE(FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_COLREMAP_M, aw_repair_code),
+			LANE_REPAIR_CODE_COL_BUS_LANE0, LANE_REPAIR_CODE_COL_BUS_LANE8))
+		return true;
+
+	/* All other option are invalid */
+	return false;
+}
+
+/* Triggers MCBIST FSM and poll for completion */
+static int hbm_trigger_mcbist_fsm(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t reg_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), mc_idx, pc_idx, mcbist_offset;
+	uint32_t cmn_idx, cmn_offset, timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : MCBIST_TIMEOUT_US;
+
+	mc_clear_interrupts(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Trigger all PCs together for parallelism */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_BIST_START) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	if (pldm)
+		k_msleep(MCBIST_TRIGGER_DELAY_MS);
+
+	/* Poll for MCBIST end */
+	for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+		for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc_idx++) {
+			mcbist_offset = hbm_offset + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET + pc_idx * MCBIST_OFFSET;
+
+			rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_BIST_DONE) +
+						 mcbist_offset, reg_val, (reg_val == 0x1),
+						 POLL_MCBIST_INTERVAL_US, timeout);
+			if (rc) {
+				cmn_idx = mc_idx / 2;
+				cmn_offset = hbm_offset + (cmn_idx * HBM_CMN_OFFSET);
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST FAILED - Timeout while polling for MCBIST FSM completion - MC%d PC%d",
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_idx);
+				LOG_ERR("SEI0: 0x%x, SEI1: 0x%x",
+					HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_STATUS)
+						   + cmn_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT),
+					HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_STATUS)
+						   + cmn_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT));
+				return STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+/* Read HBM interrupts status registers and parse the errors per PC */
+static uint32_t mcbist_error_parser(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct pc_err_bitmap *err_bitmaps, bool mode)
+{
+	struct hbm_mcbist_pc_res mcbist_res;
+	uint64_t exp = 0, rcv = 0;
+	uint32_t mc_cmn_idx, mc_idx, pc_num, pc_idx, beat_idx, rc = STATUS_PASS;
+	uint32_t addr, mc_cmn_sei0_stat, mc_cmn_sei1_stat, mcbist_pc_offset;
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), mask_offset, cmn_offset;
+	uint16_t ca_par = 0, rd_par = 0, wr_par = 0, bist_err = 0;
+	uint8_t exp_dm, rcv_dm;
+	bool is_repairable, is_unrepairable;
+
+	/* Check for various IO errors (note interrupt status registers are clear-on-read) */
+	for (mc_cmn_idx = 0; mc_cmn_idx < NUM_CMN_PER_HBM; mc_cmn_idx++) {
+		mask_offset = mc_cmn_idx * NUM_PC_PER_CH * NUM_CH_PER_CMN;
+		cmn_offset = hbm_offset + (mc_cmn_idx * HBM_CMN_OFFSET);
+		mc_cmn_sei0_stat = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_STATUS)
+					      + cmn_offset, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+		mc_cmn_sei1_stat = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_STATUS)
+					      + cmn_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+		/* Holds all HBM 16 PCs Interrupts status according to bit location (bit per PC) */
+		ca_par |= (FIELD_GET(MC_CMN_SEI0_CA_PAR_M, mc_cmn_sei0_stat) << mask_offset);
+		wr_par |= (FIELD_GET(MC_CMN_SEI0_WR_PAR_M, mc_cmn_sei0_stat) << mask_offset);
+		rd_par |= (FIELD_GET(MC_CMN_SEI0_RD_PAR_M, mc_cmn_sei0_stat) << mask_offset);
+		bist_err |=  (FIELD_GET(MC_CMN_SEI1_BIST_FAIL_M, mc_cmn_sei1_stat) << mask_offset);
+	}
+
+	/* Check results */
+	for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+		for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc_idx++) {
+			pc_num = mc_idx * NUM_PC_PER_CH + pc_idx;
+			mcbist_pc_offset = hbm_offset + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET + pc_idx * MCBIST_OFFSET;
+			is_unrepairable = false;
+			is_repairable = false;
+
+			memset(&mcbist_res, 0, sizeof(struct hbm_mcbist_pc_res));
+
+			/* Parity errors */
+			if (ca_par & BIT(pc_num)) {
+				is_unrepairable = true;
+				LOG_ERR("HBM MCBIST FAILED - CA parity error(s) - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_num);
+				mcbist_res.error_mask |= MCBIST_CA_PARITY_ERR;
+			}
+
+			if (rd_par & BIT(pc_num)) {
+				is_repairable = true;
+				LOG_ERR("HBM MCBIST FAILED - RD parity error(s) - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_num);
+				mcbist_res.error_mask |= MCBIST_RD_PARITY_ERR;
+			}
+
+			if (wr_par & BIT(pc_num)) {
+				is_repairable = true;
+				LOG_ERR("HBM MCBIST FAILED - WR parity error(s) - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_num);
+				mcbist_res.error_mask |= MCBIST_WR_PARITY_ERR;
+			}
+
+			/* Parity errors without data mismatch */
+			if (((wr_par | rd_par) & ~bist_err) & BIT(pc_num)) {
+				is_unrepairable = true;
+				LOG_ERR("HBM MCBIST FAILED - parity error(s) without data mismatch and ecc error(s) - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_num);
+			}
+
+			/* Data mismatch errors */
+			if (bist_err & BIT(pc_num)) {
+				is_repairable = true;
+				LOG_ERR("HBM MCBIST FAILED - %d data mismatch errors - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_MISMATCH_CNT)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT),
+					hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_num);
+				mcbist_res.error_mask |= MCBIST_DATA_ERR;
+
+				addr = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							mmAXI_MEM_BIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_0)
+						  + mcbist_pc_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+				mcbist_res.error_sid = FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_SID_M,
+								 addr);
+				mcbist_res.error_bank_group =
+							  FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_BG_M,
+								    addr);
+				mcbist_res.error_bank = FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_BA_M,
+								  addr);
+				mcbist_res.error_row =
+						   (FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_ROW_5_14_M,
+							      addr) <<
+						   MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_ROW_5_14_S) |
+						   FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_ROW_0_4_M,
+							     addr);
+				mcbist_res.error_column =
+						    (FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_COL_3_5_M,
+							     addr) <<
+						    MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_COL_3_5_S) |
+						    (FIELD_GET(MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_COL_1_2_M,
+							     addr) <<
+						  MCBIST_MISMATCH_FIRST_ADDR_COL_1_2_S);
+				LOG_ERR("1st mismatch HBM address: SID=%01x BG=%01x BA=%01x COL=0x%02x ROW=0x%04x",
+					mcbist_res.error_sid, mcbist_res.error_bank_group,
+					mcbist_res.error_bank, mcbist_res.error_column,
+					mcbist_res.error_row);
+
+				/* Accumulating info from 4 latched 64b data beats */
+				for (beat_idx = 0; beat_idx < NUM_BEAT_PER_TRANSAC; beat_idx++) {
+					/* Reading 32b MSB data */
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_EXPECTED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(beat_idx * 2 + 1));
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RECEIVED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(beat_idx * 2 + 1));
+					exp = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_EXPECTED)
+							 + mcbist_pc_offset, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+					rcv = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_RECEIVED)
+							 + mcbist_pc_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+					exp <<= 32;
+					rcv <<= 32;
+					/* Reading 32b LSB data */
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_EXPECTED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(beat_idx * 2));
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RECEIVED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(beat_idx * 2));
+					exp |= HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_EXPECTED)
+							  + mcbist_pc_offset, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+					rcv |= HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_RECEIVED)
+							  + mcbist_pc_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+					HBM_DBG("1st mismatch beat %d (DQ): expected = 0x%"PRIx64", received = 0x%"PRIx64"",
+						beat_idx, exp, rcv);
+					HBM_DBG("error bitmap beat %d: DQ = 0x%"PRIx64"", beat_idx,
+						exp ^ rcv);
+
+					err_bitmaps[pc_num].dq |= (exp ^ rcv);
+					mcbist_res.exp_data[beat_idx] = exp;
+					mcbist_res.rcv_data[beat_idx] = rcv;
+				}
+
+				/*
+				 * In case of MCBIST as IOAC test DM pins are referred as DQs so
+				 * another 32b read is needed, 8 DM bits per beat
+				 */
+				if (mode == HBM_MCBIST_IOAC) {
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_EXPECTED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(DM_BEAT_MISMATCH));
+					CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							       mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RECEIVED_MISMATCH_SEL)
+						   + mcbist_pc_offset, BIT(DM_BEAT_MISMATCH));
+					exp = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_EXPECTED)
+							 + mcbist_pc_offset, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+					rcv = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0BIST0_REG(
+							     mmAXI_MEM_BIST_FIRST_MISMATCH_RECEIVED)
+							 + mcbist_pc_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+					for (beat_idx = 0; beat_idx < NUM_BEAT_PER_TRANSAC;
+					     beat_idx++) {
+						mask_offset = beat_idx * DM_BITS_S;
+						exp_dm = FIELD_GET(DM_BITS_M << mask_offset, exp);
+						rcv_dm = FIELD_GET(DM_BITS_M << mask_offset, rcv);
+
+						HBM_DBG("1st mismatch beat %d (DM): expected = 0x%08x, received = 0x%08x",
+							beat_idx, exp_dm, rcv_dm);
+						HBM_DBG("error bitmap beat %d: DM = 0x%08x",
+							beat_idx, exp_dm ^ rcv_dm);
+
+						err_bitmaps[pc_num].dm |= exp_dm ^ rcv_dm;
+						mcbist_res.exp_dm[beat_idx] = exp_dm;
+						mcbist_res.rcv_dm[beat_idx] = rcv_dm;
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			if (is_unrepairable) {
+				err_bitmaps[pc_num].pc_status = MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+				rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+			} else if (is_repairable) {
+				err_bitmaps[pc_num].pc_status = MCBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE;
+				rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+uint32_t hbm_mcbist(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct pc_err_bitmap *err_bitmaps,
+			   const struct mcbist_cfg *cfg)
+{
+	uint32_t rc, pc_idx, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	uint32_t prefetch_idx, prefetch_num[PREFETCH_NUM_PER_MC];
+
+	/* Store original configurations (assuming identical in both MCs) */
+	uint32_t sid0 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_0) + hbm_offset,
+				   POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t sid1 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_1) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t bg0 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_0) + hbm_offset,
+				  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t bg1 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_1) + hbm_offset,
+				  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t ba0 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_0) + hbm_offset,
+				  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t ba1 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_1) + hbm_offset,
+				  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row0 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_0) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row1 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_1) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row2 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_2) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row3 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_3) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row4 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_4) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row5 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_5) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row6 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_6) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row7 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_7) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row8 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_8) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row9 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_9) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row10 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_10) + hbm_offset,
+				    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row11 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_11) + hbm_offset,
+				    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row12 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_12) + hbm_offset,
+				    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row13 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_13) + hbm_offset,
+				    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t row14 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_14) + hbm_offset,
+				    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t col0 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_0) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t col1 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_1) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t col2 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_2) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t col3 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_3) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t col4 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_4) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t ref_mode = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset,
+				       NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t axi_glbl_cfg = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_GLBL_CFG) + hbm_offset,
+					   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t sei0_intr_mask = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_MASK)
+					     + hbm_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t sei1_intr_mask = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CMN01_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_MASK)
+					     + hbm_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t trfc = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRFC) + hbm_offset,
+				   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t trefi_th = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFI_TH) + hbm_offset,
+				       NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t rd_bg_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+					  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t rd_sid_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+					   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t rd_open_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE)
+					    + hbm_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t wr_bg_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+					  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t wr_sid_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+					   NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t wr_open_score = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE)
+					    + hbm_offset, NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	uint32_t phy_cfg2 = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2) + hbm_offset,
+					    NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	if (MC_COMPRESSION_EN && (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_SCRUBBING)) {
+		for (prefetch_idx = 0; prefetch_idx < PREFETCH_NUM_PER_MC; prefetch_idx++)
+			prefetch_num[prefetch_idx] =
+					     HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_PREFETCH_NUM_0)
+					     + hbm_offset + prefetch_idx * BYTE_OFFSET,
+					     NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	}
+
+	bool orig_dbi_mode = FIELD_GET(MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_RD_DBI_ENABLE_M,
+				       HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG) + hbm_offset,
+						  NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT));
+
+	/* Use All-bank Refresh to avoid reordering due to REFSB */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	k_busy_wait(REFRESH_ALL_BANK_US);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRFC) + hbm_offset,
+		   hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfc + 1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFI_TH) + hbm_offset, 0x7d0);
+
+	/* Clear all Scheduling weights to avoid reordering */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	if (MC_COMPRESSION_EN && (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_SCRUBBING)) {
+		for (prefetch_idx = 0; prefetch_idx < PREFETCH_NUM_PER_MC; prefetch_idx++)
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_PREFETCH_NUM_0)
+				   + hbm_offset + prefetch_idx * BYTE_OFFSET, 0x8);
+	}
+
+	/* Reconfig addr mapping to break linear addr into BGs and BAs (optimize bus utilization) */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_0) + hbm_offset, 17);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_1) + hbm_offset, 63);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_0) + hbm_offset, 8);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_1) + hbm_offset, 9);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_0) + hbm_offset, 10);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_1) + hbm_offset, 11);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_0) + hbm_offset, 12);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_1) + hbm_offset, 13);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_2) + hbm_offset, 14);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_3) + hbm_offset, 15);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_4) + hbm_offset, 16);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_5) + hbm_offset, 18);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_6) + hbm_offset, 19);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_7) + hbm_offset, 20);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_8) + hbm_offset, 21);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_9) + hbm_offset, 22);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_10) + hbm_offset, 23);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_11) + hbm_offset, 24);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_12) + hbm_offset, 25);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_13) + hbm_offset, 26);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_14) + hbm_offset, 27);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_0) + hbm_offset, 5);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_1) + hbm_offset, 6);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_2) + hbm_offset, 7);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_3) + hbm_offset, 28);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_4) + hbm_offset, 29);
+
+	/*
+	 * enable AXIS rdy detour -
+	 * disable buffering within AXIS to avoid reordering at the AXIS arbiter
+	 */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_GLBL_CFG) + hbm_offset, axi_glbl_cfg |
+		   MC_CH_AXI_GLBL_CFG_SCH_RDY_TO_AW_M | MC_CH_AXI_GLBL_CFG_SCH_RDY_TO_AR_M);
+
+	/* Disable all interrupts */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_MASK) + hbm_offset,
+		   MASK_ALL_SEI0_INTR);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_MASK) + hbm_offset,
+		   MASK_ALL_SEI1_INTR);
+
+	/* Configure required DBI mode */
+	if (cfg->dbi_mode != orig_dbi_mode) {
+		if (change_dbi(hbm_dev, cfg->dbi_mode)) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST FAILED - cannot set DBI mode to %d",
+					hbm_dev, cfg->dbi_mode);
+			return MCBIST_STS_ERR;
+		}
+	}
+
+	/* Configure MCBIST */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_MEM_BYPASS) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_DATA_MODE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->data_mode);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_WDATA_PSEUDO_POLY_0) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->poly);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_RDATA_PSEUDO_POLY_0) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->poly);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_WDATA_PSEUDO_SEED_0) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->seed);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_RDATA_PSEUDO_SEED_0) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->seed);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_RDATA_PSEUDO_SET) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RB_WDATA_PSEUDO_SET) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_LOOP_NUM) + hbm_offset, cfg->loop_num);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WR_BLOCK_SIZE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->block_size);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WR_REP_NUM) + hbm_offset, cfg->rep_num);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RD_BLOCK_SIZE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->block_size);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_RD_REP_NUM) + hbm_offset, cfg->rep_num);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_STOP_ON_ERR) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->stop_on_err);
+	/* following values are mandatory */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ADDR_MEM_STRIDE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->mem_stride);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ADDR_LOOP_STRIDE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->loop_stride);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ADDR_INCR_STRIDE) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->incr_stride);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WRBLOCK_FENCE_EN) + hbm_offset, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WR_DELAY) + hbm_offset, 0x300);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_AXLEN) + hbm_offset, cfg->axlen);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_AXSIZE) + hbm_offset, cfg->axsize);
+	/*
+	 * Enable ECC bypass, meaning DM pins will toggle as DQ pins. Enabled only for IOAC test
+	 * so in full memory scrubbing so ECC will be Initialized.
+	 */
+	if (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_IOAC)
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2) + hbm_offset,
+			   phy_cfg2 | MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2_DFI_MCBIST_ECC_MODE_M);
+
+	if (MC_COMPRESSION_EN && (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_SCRUBBING)) {
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ARUSER) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_AWUSER) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WUSER) + hbm_offset, 0xfbe0);
+	} else {
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ARUSER) + hbm_offset, 0x4);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_AWUSER) + hbm_offset, 0x4);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_WUSER) + hbm_offset, 0x4);
+
+	}
+
+	/* Initialize error bitmaps for all PCs */
+	for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_HBM; pc_idx++) {
+		err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dq = 0;
+		err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dm = 0;
+		err_bitmaps[pc_idx].pc_status = MCBIST_STS_PASS;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d MCBIST settings: DATA_MODE=%d, POLY=0x%04x, SEED=0x%04x", hbm_dev,
+		      cfg->data_mode, cfg->poly, cfg->seed);
+
+	/* Enable MCBIST */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_BIST_EN) + hbm_offset, MC_CH_BIST_EN_VAL_M);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MCBIST_REG(mmAXI_MEM_BIST_ADDR_INCR_INIT_0) + hbm_offset,
+		   cfg->start_addr);
+
+	if (hbm_trigger_mcbist_fsm(hbm_dev))
+		return MCBIST_STS_ERR;
+
+	rc = mcbist_error_parser(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps, cfg->mcbist_mode);
+
+	/* Restore MC configurations for functional mode */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_0) + hbm_offset, sid0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_SID_1) + hbm_offset, sid1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_0) + hbm_offset, bg0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BG_1) + hbm_offset, bg1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_0) + hbm_offset, ba0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_BA_1) + hbm_offset, ba1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_0) + hbm_offset, row0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_1) + hbm_offset, row1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_2) + hbm_offset, row2);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_3) + hbm_offset, row3);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_4) + hbm_offset, row4);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_5) + hbm_offset, row5);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_6) + hbm_offset, row6);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_7) + hbm_offset, row7);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_8) + hbm_offset, row8);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_9) + hbm_offset, row9);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_10) + hbm_offset, row10);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_11) + hbm_offset, row11);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_12) + hbm_offset, row12);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_13) + hbm_offset, row13);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_ROW_14) + hbm_offset, row14);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_0) + hbm_offset, col0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_1) + hbm_offset, col1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_2) + hbm_offset, col2);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_3) + hbm_offset, col3);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_ADMAP_COL_4) + hbm_offset, col4);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_AXI_GLBL_CFG) + hbm_offset, axi_glbl_cfg);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI0_MASK) + hbm_offset,
+		   sei0_intr_mask);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_INTR_REG(mmMC_CMN_INTR_SEI1_MASK) + hbm_offset,
+		   sei1_intr_mask);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_BIST_EN) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TRFC) + hbm_offset, trfc);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_TREFI_TH) + hbm_offset, trefi_th);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset, rd_bg_score);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset, rd_sid_score);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_RD_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+		   rd_open_score);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_BG_SCORE) + hbm_offset, wr_bg_score);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_SID_SCORE) + hbm_offset, wr_sid_score);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_WR_OPEN_PAGE_SCORE) + hbm_offset,
+		   wr_open_score);
+	/*
+	 * Disable ECC bypass, meaning DM pins will toggle according to ECC calculation.
+	 * Must be enabled full memory scrubbing so ECC will be Initialized
+	 */
+	if (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_IOAC)
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_2) + hbm_offset, phy_cfg2);
+
+	if (MC_COMPRESSION_EN && (cfg->mcbist_mode == HBM_MCBIST_SCRUBBING)) {
+		for (prefetch_idx = 0; prefetch_idx < PREFETCH_NUM_PER_MC; prefetch_idx++)
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_RSCH_PREFETCH_NUM_0)
+				   + hbm_offset + prefetch_idx * BYTE_OFFSET,
+				   prefetch_num[prefetch_idx]);
+	}
+
+	/* delay to ensure at least one REF cmd + tRFC */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset, 0x0);
+	k_busy_wait(REFRESH_AND_TRFC_CYCL_US);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset, ref_mode);
+
+	/* Restore DBI mode */
+	if (cfg->dbi_mode != orig_dbi_mode) {
+		if (change_dbi(hbm_dev, orig_dbi_mode)) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST FAILED - cannot restore DBI mode to %d",
+					hbm_dev, cfg->dbi_mode);
+			return MCBIST_STS_ERR;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+static int hbm_mcbist_io_ac_config(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum mcbist_io_patterns pattern,
+				   struct mcbist_cfg *cfg)
+{
+	/* Common MCBIST congigurations for all patterns */
+	cfg->transaction_num = MCBIST_IO_AC_TRANSACTION_NUM;	/* 8KB (1K per beat) */
+	cfg->poly = MCBIST_RD_WR_DATA_POLY;
+	cfg->seed = MCBIST_RD_WR_DATA_SEED;
+	/* AXLEN and AXSIZE configured to guarantee transaction size of 256B */
+	cfg->axlen = MCBIST_AXI_AXLEN_256B;
+	cfg->axsize = MCBIST_AXI_AXSIZE_256B;
+	cfg->loop_num = MCBIST_TRANSACTION_LOOP_NUM;
+	cfg->rep_num = MCBIST_IO_AC_TRANSACTION_REP_NUM;
+	cfg->block_size = MCBIST_IO_AC_BLOCK_SIZE;		/* 64KB (8K per beat) */
+	cfg->incr_stride = MCBIST_STRIDE_BETWEEN_TRANASCTIONS;
+	cfg->mem_stride = 0x0;
+	cfg->loop_stride = MCBIST_IO_AC_LOOP_STRIDE;
+	cfg->stop_on_err = STOP_ON_ERR_ENABLED;
+	cfg->mcbist_mode = HBM_MCBIST_IOAC;
+	/*
+	 * set bit RA[14] (mapped to addr bit 26) to guarantee
+	 * toggling of pin R[6]. set bit 15 to guarantee coverage of both
+	 * SID (partial coverage for each)
+	 */
+	cfg->start_addr = MCBIST_IO_AC_START_ADDR;
+
+	switch (pattern) {
+	case PRBS_DBI_OFF:
+		cfg->data_mode = MCBIST_PRBS;
+		cfg->dbi_mode = DBI_DISABLED;
+		HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d MCBIST pattern PRBS_DBI_OFF (iter%d) is configured",
+			hbm_dev, pattern);
+		break;
+	case FAST_00_FF_DBI_OFF:
+		cfg->data_mode = MCBIST_FAST_00_FF;
+		cfg->dbi_mode = DBI_DISABLED;
+		HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d MCBIST pattern FAST_00_FF_DBI_OFF (iter%d) is configured",
+			hbm_dev, pattern);
+		break;
+	case PRBS_DBI_ON:
+		cfg->data_mode = MCBIST_PRBS;
+		cfg->dbi_mode = DBI_ENABLED;
+		HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d MCBIST pattern PRBS_DBI_ON (iter%d) is configured",
+			hbm_dev, pattern);
+		break;
+	case FAST_00_FF_DBI_ON:
+		cfg->data_mode = MCBIST_FAST_00_FF;
+		cfg->dbi_mode = DBI_ENABLED;
+		HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d MCBIST pattern FAST_00_FF_DBI_ON (iter%d) is configured",
+			hbm_dev, pattern);
+		break;
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST pattern %d config FAILED, no such pattern",
+			hbm_dev, pattern);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static uint8_t encode_remap_from_bitmap(uint32_t byte_pair)
+{
+	uint8_t code;
+
+	switch (byte_pair) {
+	/* DQ */
+	case 0x00000:
+		code = DEFAULT_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00001:
+		code = DQ0_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00002:
+		code = DQ1_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00004:
+		code = DQ2_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00008:
+		code = DQ3_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00010:
+		code = DQ4_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00020:
+		code = DQ5_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00040:
+		code = DQ6_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00080:
+		code = DQ7_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00100:
+		code = DQ8_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00200:
+		code = DQ9_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00400:
+		code = DQ10_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x00800:
+		code = DQ11_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x01000:
+		code = DQ12_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x02000:
+		code = DQ13_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x04000:
+		code = DQ14_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x08000:
+		code = DQ15_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	/* DM */
+	case 0x10000:
+		code = DM0_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	case 0x20000:
+		code = DM1_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+		break;
+	/*
+	 * Any other value means more than one bit error (invalid for remap) -
+	 * shouldn't have reached this function
+	 */
+	default:
+		code = INVALID_LANE_MAP_CODE;
+	}
+
+	return code;
+}
+
+/*
+ * Repair defect lanes that were detected during MCBIST
+ * Relevant for debug only as lane repair should be triggered only
+ * from ATE or HBM margin-tool (flag DRIVER_SLR_EN)
+ */
+static uint32_t post_mcbist_lane_repair(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct pc_err_bitmap *err_bitmaps)
+{
+	uint32_t pc_repair_code, pc_idx, phy_ch, dw_idx, rc;
+
+	if (!DRIVER_SLR_EN) {
+		LOG_ERR("SOFT lane repair is disabled");
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_HBM; pc_idx++) {
+		if (err_bitmaps[pc_idx].pc_status == MCBIST_STS_PASS)
+			continue;
+
+		phy_ch = phy_ch_mapping(pc_idx / 2);
+		dw_idx = pc_to_dw(pc_idx);
+
+		pc_repair_code = hbm_cfg.slr_info[phy_ch].dw[dw_idx] |
+				 (hbm_cfg.slr_info[phy_ch].dw[dw_idx + 1] << DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S);
+
+		rc = hbm_drv_lane_repair(hbm_dev, pc_idx, pc_repair_code, REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT);
+		if (rc)
+			return rc;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static uint32_t mcbist_analyzer(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t mcbist_rc,
+				struct pc_err_bitmap *err_bitmaps)
+{
+	uint32_t phy_ch_offset, proposed_remap = 0, pc_current_remap = 0, combined_remap = 0;
+	uint32_t byte_pair, analyzer_rc = MCBIST_STS_PASS, mc_idx, pc_idx, phy_ch, dw_idx;
+	uint32_t pair_idx, mask_offset;
+	uint16_t dq;
+	uint8_t dm;
+
+	if (mcbist_rc == STATUS_PASS || mcbist_rc == MCBIST_STS_ERR)
+		return mcbist_rc;
+
+	/* Analyze failing PCs */
+	for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_HBM; pc_idx++) {
+		if (err_bitmaps[pc_idx].pc_status == MCBIST_STS_PASS)
+			continue;
+
+		mc_idx = pc_idx / 2;
+		if (err_bitmaps[pc_idx].pc_status == MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE) {
+			LOG_ERR("MCBIST - Unrepairable FAIL - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+				hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_idx);
+			return MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+		}
+
+		/* If reached here, we suspect 1 or more defect DWORD lanes */
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d MC%d PC%d defect DQ lanes bitmap = 0x%"PRIx64"\n", hbm_dev, mc_idx,
+			    pc_idx, err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dq);
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d MC%d PC%d defect DM lanes bitmap = 0x%x\n", hbm_dev, mc_idx,
+			    pc_idx, err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dm);
+
+		/*
+		 * Analyze whether lanes are repairable by walking over error bitmap from LSB to
+		 * MSB in byte-pairs sections
+		 */
+		for (pair_idx = 0; pair_idx < BYTE_PAIRS_PER_PC; pair_idx++) {
+			mask_offset = pair_idx * DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S;
+			dq = FIELD_GET(DQ_BYTES_PAIR_M << mask_offset, err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dq);
+			mask_offset = pair_idx * DM_BYTES_PAIR_S;
+			dm = FIELD_GET(DM_BYTES_PAIR_M << mask_offset, err_bitmaps[pc_idx].dm);
+
+			byte_pair = dq | (dm << DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S);
+
+			if (hweight32(byte_pair) > 1) {
+				LOG_ERR("MCBIST - Unrepairable FAIL - Multiple lane errors in byte-pair %d - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+					pair_idx, hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_idx);
+				return MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+			} else {
+				proposed_remap |= encode_remap_from_bitmap(byte_pair) <<
+						  (pair_idx * BYTES_PER_PC);
+			}
+		}
+
+		phy_ch = phy_ch_mapping(mc_idx);
+		dw_idx = pc_to_dw(pc_idx);
+
+		/* Read existing remap info per PC */
+		phy_ch_offset = phy_ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+		pc_current_remap = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+				      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP)
+				      + phy_ch_offset + (dw_idx + 1) * PHY_DW_OFFSET);
+		pc_current_remap <<= DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S;
+		pc_current_remap |= hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+				      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP)
+				      + phy_ch_offset + dw_idx * PHY_DW_OFFSET);
+
+		/* Check whether repair is consistent with existing remap */
+		combined_remap = pc_current_remap & proposed_remap;
+		if (is_valid_repair(pc_current_remap, proposed_remap)) {
+			analyzer_rc = MCBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE;
+			LOG_ERR("MCBIST - Repairable FAIL - remap code = 0x%08x - HBM%d MC%d PC%d",
+				combined_remap, hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_idx);
+
+			/* Save the required remap in global struct */
+			hbm_cfg.slr_info[phy_ch].dw[dw_idx] =
+						FIELD_GET(DQ_BYTES_PAIR_M, combined_remap);
+			hbm_cfg.slr_info[phy_ch].dw[dw_idx + 1] =
+						FIELD_GET(DQ_BYTES_PAIR_M << DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S,
+							  combined_remap);
+		} else {
+			LOG_ERR("MCBIST - Unrepairable FAIL - inconsistency with existing remap - "
+				"HBM%d MC%d PC%d", hbm_dev, mc_idx, pc_idx);
+			LOG_ERR("Proposed remap: 0x%08x, Current remap: 0x%08x", proposed_remap,
+				pc_current_remap);
+			return MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return analyzer_rc;
+}
+
+static int perform_known_lane_repair_from_rma_entry(enum rma_tables_type table_type, void *data,
+						    int entry_idx, void *arg)
+{
+	const int *hbm_dev = (const int *)arg;
+	const struct rma_hbm_lane_repair_entry *entry =
+				(struct rma_hbm_lane_repair_entry *)data;
+
+	if (entry->hbm_num != *hbm_dev)
+		return 0;
+
+	LOG_DBG("HBM known lane repair: HBM %d MC-PC %u repair-code 0x%08x",
+		entry->hbm_num, entry->mc_pc, entry->repair_code);
+
+	return hbm_drv_lane_repair(entry->hbm_num, entry->mc_pc,
+				   entry->repair_code, REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT);
+}
+
+int perform_known_lane_repair(int hbm_dev)
+{
+	return rma_table_walk(RMA_HBM_LANE_REPAIR_TABLE,
+			      perform_known_lane_repair_from_rma_entry,
+			      &hbm_dev);
+}
+
+int hbm_mcbist_io_ac(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	struct pc_err_bitmap err_bitmaps[NUM_PC_PER_HBM];
+	struct mcbist_cfg mcbist_cfg;
+	uint32_t mcbist_rc, io_ac_rc, mcbist_config_rc;
+	enum mcbist_io_patterns mcbist_iter;
+
+	if (pldm)
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+
+	for (mcbist_iter = 0; mcbist_iter < MCBIST_IO_AC_PATTERNS_NUM; mcbist_iter++) {
+		mcbist_config_rc = hbm_mcbist_io_ac_config(hbm_dev, mcbist_iter, &mcbist_cfg);
+		if (mcbist_config_rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST IO AC test (iter%d) FAILED to config pattern",
+				hbm_dev, mcbist_iter);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+		mcbist_rc = hbm_mcbist(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps, &mcbist_cfg);
+		io_ac_rc = mcbist_analyzer(hbm_dev, mcbist_rc, err_bitmaps);
+
+		if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_ERR)
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+		if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE) {
+			LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST IO AC test (iter%d) FAILED Unrepairable", hbm_dev,
+				mcbist_iter);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		} else if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST IO AC test (iter%d) FAILED Repairable", hbm_dev,
+				mcbist_iter);
+			io_ac_rc = post_mcbist_lane_repair(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps);
+			if (io_ac_rc)
+				return STATUS_FAIL;
+			/* Re-run MCBIST after SOFT lane repiar(SLR) */
+			mcbist_rc = hbm_mcbist(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps, &mcbist_cfg);
+			io_ac_rc = mcbist_analyzer(hbm_dev, mcbist_rc, err_bitmaps);
+			if (io_ac_rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d IO AC (iter%d) re-run after SOFT lane repair FAILED",
+					hbm_dev, mcbist_iter);
+				return STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+			LOG_INF("HBM%d IO AC test (iter%d) RE-RUN PASSED after SOFT lane repair",
+				hbm_dev, mcbist_iter);
+			return STATUS_PASS;
+		} else if (io_ac_rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d IO AC test (iter%d) return code: %d, is invalid FAILED",
+				hbm_dev, mcbist_iter, io_ac_rc);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		HBM_DBG("HBM%d IO AC test (iter%d) PASSED\n", hbm_dev, mcbist_iter);
+	}
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+uint32_t hbm_mcbist_full_mem_scrub(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum mcbist_data_mode data_pattern)
+{
+	struct pc_err_bitmap err_bitmaps[NUM_PC_PER_HBM];
+	/*** DO NOT CHANGE ANY OF FOLLOWING PARAMS OTHERWISE SCRUBBING WILL NOT BE FULL! ***/
+	/* MCBIST configuration for full memory coverage to perform scrubbing */
+	struct mcbist_cfg mcbist_cfg = {
+		.data_mode = data_pattern,
+		.poly = MCBIST_RD_WR_DATA_POLY,
+		.seed = MCBIST_RD_WR_DATA_SEED,
+		.transaction_num = MCBIST_FULL_MEM_TRANSACTION_NUM,
+		/* AXLEN and AXSIZE configured to guarantee transaction size of 256B */
+		.axlen = MCBIST_AXI_AXLEN_256B,
+		.axsize = MCBIST_AXI_AXSIZE_256B,
+		.loop_num = MCBIST_TRANSACTION_LOOP_NUM,
+		/* block_size * rep_num = 1GB = PC capacity */
+		.rep_num = MCBIST_FULL_MEM_TRANSACTION_REP_NUM,
+		.block_size = MCBIST_FULL_MEM_BLOCK_SIZE,
+		.stop_on_err = STOP_ON_ERR_ENABLED,
+		.dbi_mode = DBI_ENABLED,
+		.mcbist_mode = HBM_MCBIST_SCRUBBING,
+		.start_addr = MCBIST_FULL_MEM_START_ADDR,
+		/* stride between transactions */
+		.incr_stride = MCBIST_STRIDE_BETWEEN_TRANASCTIONS,
+		/* stride between blocks */
+		.mem_stride = 0x0,
+		/* stride between loops */
+		.loop_stride =  MCBIST_FULL_MEM_LOOP_STRIDE
+	};
+	uint32_t mcbist_rc, io_ac_rc;
+
+	if (pldm)
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+
+	mcbist_rc = hbm_mcbist(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps, &mcbist_cfg);
+	io_ac_rc = mcbist_analyzer(hbm_dev, mcbist_rc, err_bitmaps);
+
+	if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_ERR) {
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST Full Memory Scrubbing FAILED to run", hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+	if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_FAIL_UNREPAIRABLE) {
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST Full Memory Scrubbing FAILED Unrepairable", hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+	if (io_ac_rc == MCBIST_STS_FAIL_REPAIRABLE) {
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST Full Memory Scrubbing FAILED Repairable", hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+	if (io_ac_rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d MCBIST Full Memory Scrubbing return code: %d, is invalid FAILED",
+			hbm_dev, io_ac_rc);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d Full Memory Scrubbing PASSED\n", hbm_dev);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+/* SW WA for SIV-1361, avoid updating remap for invalid remap code*/
+void validate_hbm_lane_remap(int hbm_dev, struct lane_remap_info *remap_info)
+{
+	uint32_t ch, dw, mask, bpair_idx, dw_bpair_repair_code,
+		 aw_row_repair_code, aw_col_repair_code;
+	bool repair_code_is_valid;
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch][dw] = remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw];
+			if (remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw] != DW_DEFAULT_REMAP_CODE) {
+				LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d remap code:0x%04x", hbm_dev, ch, dw,
+					remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw]);
+				for (bpair_idx = 0; bpair_idx < BYTE_PAIRS_PER_DW; bpair_idx++) {
+					mask = LANE_BPAIR_REMAP_MASK << (8 * bpair_idx);
+					dw_bpair_repair_code =
+					  FIELD_GET(mask, remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw]);
+					repair_code_is_valid =
+					  is_dw_bpair_repair_code_mode2_valid(dw_bpair_repair_code);
+					if (dw_bpair_repair_code == LANE_REMAP_BPAIR_DEFAULT_CODE) {
+						continue;
+					} else if (repair_code_is_valid) {
+						LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d BPAIR%d valid remap code:0x%x, will keep current remap",
+							hbm_dev, ch, dw, bpair_idx,
+							dw_bpair_repair_code);
+					} else {
+						LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d BPAIR%d invalid remap code:0x%x, will modify back to default code(0xff)",
+							hbm_dev, ch, dw, bpair_idx,
+							dw_bpair_repair_code);
+						remap_info->dw_remap_fix_code[ch][dw] |= mask;
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch] = remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch];
+		if (remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch] != AW_DEFAULT_REMAP_CODE) {
+			LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW remap code: 0x%02x", hbm_dev, ch,
+				remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+			mask = HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_ROWREMAP_M;
+			aw_row_repair_code = FIELD_GET(mask, remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+			if (aw_row_repair_code != LANE_REMAP_DEFAULT_CODE) {
+				repair_code_is_valid =
+					 is_aw_row_repair_code_valid(remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+				if (repair_code_is_valid) {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW valid Row remap code:0x%x, keeping current remap",
+						hbm_dev, ch, aw_row_repair_code);
+				} else {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW invalid Row remap code:0x%x, modifying back to default code(0xf)",
+						hbm_dev, ch, aw_row_repair_code);
+					remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch] |= mask;
+				}
+			}
+			mask = HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_COLREMAP_M;
+			aw_col_repair_code = FIELD_GET(mask, remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+			if (aw_col_repair_code != LANE_REMAP_DEFAULT_CODE) {
+				repair_code_is_valid =
+					 is_aw_col_repair_code_valid(remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+				if (repair_code_is_valid) {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW valid Col remap code:0x%x, keeping current remap",
+						hbm_dev, ch, aw_col_repair_code);
+				} else {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW invalid Col remap code:0x%x, modifying back to default code(0xf)",
+						hbm_dev, ch, aw_col_repair_code);
+					remap_info->aw_remap_fix_code[ch] |= mask;
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+
+#if CONFIG_HL_HBM_STORE_TEST_INFO
+void hbm_drv_get_status(const struct device *dev, uint8_t hbm_dev, uint8_t channel, uint8_t dword,
+			void *res)
+{
+	struct hl_hbm_drv_data *hbm_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(dev);
+	struct hbm_status_res_snapshot *result = (struct hbm_status_res_snapshot *)res;
+
+	memcpy(&result->vref_train, &hbm_data->hbm_res.status_res[hbm_dev].vref_train,
+	       sizeof(struct vref_training));
+	memcpy(&result->ca_res, &hbm_data->hbm_res.status_res[hbm_dev].chan[channel].ca_res,
+	       sizeof(struct lcdl));
+	memcpy(&result->dword,
+	       &hbm_data->hbm_res.status_res[hbm_dev].chan[channel].dword[dword],
+	       sizeof(struct dw_training));
+}
+
+static int mcbist_cfg_verify(const struct mcbist_cfg *cfg)
+{
+	const char *prefix = "Invalid MCBIST configuration:";
+
+	if (!cfg) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s NULL pointer provided", prefix);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (cfg->data_mode >= MCBIST_DATA_MODE_MAX) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s invalid data mode %d", prefix, cfg->data_mode);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (cfg->block_size > HBM_MCBIST_MAX_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s block size 0x%x exceeds max value 0x%x",
+			prefix, cfg->block_size, HBM_MCBIST_MAX_BLOCK_SIZE);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (cfg->rep_num > HBM_MCBIST_MAX_REP_NUM) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s rep num 0x%x exceeds max value 0x%x",
+			prefix, cfg->rep_num, HBM_MCBIST_MAX_REP_NUM);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (cfg->loop_num > HBM_MCBIST_MAX_LOOP_NUM) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s loop num 0x%x exceeds max value 0x%x",
+			prefix, cfg->loop_num, HBM_MCBIST_MAX_LOOP_NUM);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (cfg->mcbist_mode >= HBM_MCBIST_MODE_MAX) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s invalid MCBIST mode %d", prefix, cfg->mcbist_mode);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+int hbm_drv_run_mcbist(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev, const void *cfg, void *res,
+		       unsigned int res_len)
+{
+	uint32_t rc;
+	struct pc_err_bitmap err_bitmaps[NUM_PC_PER_HBM];
+	const struct mcbist_cfg *config = (const struct mcbist_cfg *)cfg;
+	struct hl_hbm_drv_data *hbm_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(dev);
+	const unsigned int mcbist_result_length = sizeof(hbm_data->hbm_res.mcbist_res[hbm_dev]);
+
+	if (!res) {
+		LOG_ERR("NULL pointer provided to store MCBIST result");
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (mcbist_cfg_verify(config) != STATUS_PASS)
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+	if (res_len < mcbist_result_length) {
+		LOG_ERR("Insufficient memory allocated for MCBIST result (%u / %u)",
+			res_len, mcbist_result_length);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+
+	rc = hbm_mcbist(hbm_dev, err_bitmaps, config);
+	mcbist_analyzer(hbm_dev, rc, err_bitmaps);
+
+	memcpy(res, &hbm_data->hbm_res.mcbist_res[hbm_dev], mcbist_result_length);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+#endif /* CONFIG_HL_HBM_STORE_TEST_INFO */
+
+
+int hbm_drv_lane_repair(int hbm_dev, int mc_pc, uint32_t pc_repair_code, enum repair_type type)
+{
+	uint32_t lanerepair_ctrl, lanerepair_sts, phy_ch_offset, ref_mode;
+	uint32_t phy_ch, dw_idx, rc, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	bool valid_input;
+
+	valid_input = IN_RANGE(hbm_dev, 0, GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS - 1) &&
+			   IN_RANGE(mc_pc, 0, NUM_PC_PER_HBM - 1) &&
+			   type == REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT &&
+			   is_repair_code_mode2_valid(pc_repair_code);
+	if (!valid_input) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM lane repair FAILED! Invalid input: hbm_dev %d, mc_pc %d type %d code 0x%x\n",
+			hbm_dev, mc_pc, type, pc_repair_code);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	/* Save current refresh mode and shut down refresh */
+	ref_mode = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE) + hbm_offset, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE), 0x0);
+
+	phy_ch = phy_ch_mapping(mc_pc / 2);
+	phy_ch_offset = phy_ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+	dw_idx = pc_to_dw(mc_pc);
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP) +
+		      phy_ch_offset + dw_idx * PHY_DW_OFFSET, pc_repair_code & DQ_BYTES_PAIR_M);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP) +
+		      phy_ch_offset + (dw_idx + 1) * PHY_DW_OFFSET,
+		      (pc_repair_code >> DQ_BYTES_PAIR_S) & DQ_BYTES_PAIR_M);
+
+
+	phy_pubmode(hbm_dev, PUBMODE_ENTER);
+
+	lanerepair_ctrl = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRRUN_M |
+			 (type == REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT ?
+			 HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRSOFTEN_M :
+			 HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRHARDEN_M) |
+			 HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_DWREMAPMODE_M;
+
+	lanerepair_sts = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRDONE_M |
+			 (type == REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT ?
+			 HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRSOFTDONE_M :
+			 HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRHARDDONE_M);
+
+	rc = phy_trigger_lanerepair_fsm(hbm_dev, lanerepair_ctrl, lanerepair_sts);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to issue lane repair - HBM%d MC_PC%d repair-code: 0x%x",
+			hbm_dev, mc_pc, pc_repair_code);
+		phy_pubmode(hbm_dev, PUBMODE_EXIT);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	phy_pubmode(hbm_dev, PUBMODE_EXIT);
+
+	/* Restore Refresh */
+	/* If original mode was REFSB (2), need to issue a REF command in order to reset the
+	 * REFSB cycle in the HBM, Therefore, the procedure is:
+	 *  1. go first to Refresh-All mode
+	 *  2. spend ~8us in this mode to ensure a REF command has been issued
+	 *  3. disable Refresh again to reset the Reset FSM
+	 *  4. go to REFSB mode
+	 */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE), 0x1);
+	k_busy_wait(REFRESH_CMD_DELAY_US);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE), 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_REF_MODE), ref_mode);
+
+	LOG_INF("Lane repair PASSED - HBM%d MC-PC%d repair-code: 0x%x", hbm_dev, mc_pc,
+		pc_repair_code);
+	add_lane_remap_info(hbm_dev, mc_pc);
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}

b/drivers/hbm/gaudi3/phy/phy.c

+
+#include <phy.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_DECLARE(gaudi3_hbm);
+
+#ifndef HBM_DBG
+#define HBM_DBG(...)								    \
+	do {									            \
+		if (hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)					    \
+			LOG_INF(__VA_ARGS__);					    \
+		else								            \
+			LOG_DBG(__VA_ARGS__);					    \
+	} while (0)
+#endif
+
+static struct phy_indirect_access phy_access;
+
+
+static uint8_t phy_get_chiplet_type_and_offset(uint32_t addr)
+{
+	uint8_t chiplet_type;
+
+	if (IN_RANGE(addr, mmD0_HBM0_PHY_P0_MASTER_BASE, mmD0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_BASE - 1)) {
+		chiplet_type = PHY_CHIPLET_MASTER;
+		phy_access.offset = PHY_CHIPLET_MASTER_OFFSET;
+	} else if (IN_RANGE(addr, mmD0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_BASE, mmD0_HBM1_MC0_BASE - 1)) {
+		chiplet_type = PHY_CHIPLET_INITENG;
+		phy_access.offset = PHY_CHIPLET_INITENG_OFFSET;
+	} else {
+		chiplet_type = PHY_CHIPLET_CH;
+		phy_access.offset = PHY_CHIPLET_CH_OFFSET;
+	}
+
+	return chiplet_type;
+}
+
+inline void hbm_phy_write(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t addr, uint32_t val)
+{
+	uint8_t chiplet_type;
+	uint32_t phy_addr, hbm_offset, reg_val;
+
+	hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	chiplet_type = phy_get_chiplet_type_and_offset(addr);
+	phy_addr = hbm_offset + addr - phy_access.offset;
+
+	/* set chiplet type only if needed */
+	if (chiplet_type != phy_access.chiplet) {
+		phy_access.chiplet = chiplet_type;
+		reg_val = FIELD_PREP(CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS_PSTATE_M, phy_access.pstate);
+		reg_val |= phy_access.chiplet;
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS) +
+									hbm_offset, reg_val);
+	}
+	CFG_WREG32(phy_addr, val);
+}
+
+inline uint32_t hbm_phy_read(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t addr)
+{
+	uint8_t chiplet_type;
+	uint32_t phy_addr, hbm_offset, reg_val;
+
+	hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	chiplet_type = phy_get_chiplet_type_and_offset(addr);
+	phy_addr = hbm_offset + addr - phy_access.offset;
+
+	/* set chiplet type only if needed */
+	if (chiplet_type != phy_access.chiplet) {
+		phy_access.chiplet = chiplet_type;
+		reg_val = FIELD_PREP(CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS_PSTATE_M, phy_access.pstate);
+		reg_val |= phy_access.chiplet;
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS) +
+									hbm_offset, reg_val);
+	}
+
+	return HBM_RREG32(phy_addr, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+}
+
+static uint32_t read_hbm_lane_remap(int hbm_dev, struct lane_remap_info *remap_info)
+{
+	uint32_t wdr[HBM_REMAP_REGS_SIZE], val, rc, ch, dw;
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, ch, ieee1500_soft_lane_repair, wir_read, wdr, 0);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d CH%d: IEEE1500 soft lane repair read instruction FAILED",
+				hbm_dev, ch);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		/*
+		 * remap data is 72 bits (3 wdr registers) aranged as follow:
+		 * dw0 -> wdr[0][0:15]
+		 * dw1 -> wdr[0][16:31]
+		 * aw -> wdr[1][32:39], COL-[32:35] ROW-[39:36]
+		 * dw2 -> wdr[1][40:55]
+		 * dw3 -> wdr[1][56:63], wdr[2][64:71]
+		 * hence the following parsing for 1 aword and 4 dwords per channel
+		 */
+
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			if (dw == 0)
+				val = FIELD_GET(0xffff, wdr[0]);
+			else if (dw == 1)
+				val = FIELD_GET(0xffff0000, wdr[0]);
+			else if (dw == 2)
+				val = FIELD_GET(0xffff00, wdr[1]);
+			else {
+				val = FIELD_GET(0xff000000, wdr[1]) |
+				       FIELD_PREP(0xff00, FIELD_GET(0xff, wdr[2]));
+			}
+			remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw] = val;
+		}
+
+		remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch] = FIELD_GET(WDR_LANE_REPIAR_AW_ROW_REMAP, wdr[1]) |
+					  FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP_COLREMAP_M,
+					  FIELD_GET(WDR_LANE_REPIAR_AW_COL_REMAP, wdr[1]));
+	}
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static void phy_set_pstate(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum phy_pstate pstate)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+
+	phy_access.pstate = pstate;
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS_PSTATE_M, phy_access.pstate);
+	reg_val |= phy_access.chiplet;
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS) + hbm_offset,
+											reg_val);
+}
+static void phy_preload_initeng(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t instr_dly[3];
+
+	/* Program Disable flags for initeng groups */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDISABLEFLAG0), 0xfff0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDISABLEFLAG1), 0xffff);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDISABLEFLAG2), 0xfff0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDISABLEFLAG3), 0xffef);
+
+	/* Program DFI Frequency mappings */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDFIFREQXLAT0), 0x3210);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDFIFREQXLAT7), 0x4000);
+
+	/* Program INITENG instructions delays according to:
+	 * INITSEQDLY0 for all PSTATE - 10 * freq / 4 for PLL Tlock of 10us
+	 * INITSEQDLY1 for all PSTATE - freq / 4 for PLL Tlock of 10us
+	 * INITSEQDLY2 for all PSTATE - 0.5 * freq / 4 for PLL Tlock of 10us
+	 */
+	switch (hbm_cfg.ck_freq) {
+	case HBM_PLL_1200:
+		instr_dly[0] = 0xbb8;
+		instr_dly[1] = 0x12c;
+		instr_dly[2] = 0x96;
+		break;
+
+	case HBM_PLL_1600:
+		instr_dly[0] = 0xfa0;
+		instr_dly[1] = 0x190;
+		instr_dly[2] = 0xc8;
+
+	case HBM_PLL_1800:
+	default:
+		instr_dly[0] = 0x1194;
+		instr_dly[1] = 0x1c2;
+		instr_dly[2] = 0xe1;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	phy_set_pstate(hbm_dev, BCAST);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDLY0_P0),
+										instr_dly[0]);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDLY1_P0),
+										instr_dly[1]);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQDLY2_P0),
+										instr_dly[2]);
+
+	/* Program Pre sequencer instructions */
+	phy_set_pstate(hbm_dev, PSTATE_0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQPRE0S2),
+											0x400);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQPRE1S2),
+											0x400);
+}
+static void debug_print_mrs(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t i;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d Mode Registers (MR) configurations:", hbm_dev);
+	for (i = 0; i < PHY_MASTER_MR_REGS_NUM; i++) {
+		HBM_DBG("HBM%d MR%d: 0x%x", hbm_dev, i,
+			hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR0)
+									+ (i * BYTE_OFFSET)));
+	}
+	HBM_DBG("\n");
+}
+static void debug_print_pub_dramtiming(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t i;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d Dram Timing parameters configurations:", hbm_dev);
+	for (i = 0; i < PHY_MASTER_DRAMTIMING_REGS_NUM; i++) {
+		HBM_DBG("HBM%d DramTiming%d: 0x%x", hbm_dev, i,
+			hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING0)
+				+ (i * BYTE_OFFSET)));
+	}
+	HBM_DBG("\n");
+}
+
+void debug_print_pub_deviceid(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t i;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_DBG("\nHBM%d DramDeviceId CSRs:", hbm_dev);
+	for (i = 0; i < PHY_MASTER_DRAMDEVICEID_REGS_NUM; i++) {
+		HBM_DBG("DramDeviceId%d: 0x%x", i, hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMDEVICEID0) + (i * BYTE_OFFSET)));
+	}
+	HBM_DBG("\n");
+}
+
+static void phy_config_mode_registers(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	const uint32_t mr3_tras_mask = 0x3f, mr4_parity_lat_mask = 0xc, mr4_ext_wl_mask = 0x10,
+		       mr4_ext_rl_mask = 0x20, mr4_ecc_mask = 0x3;
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+	bool ext_wl = hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat > 8, ext_rl = hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat > 33;
+
+	/* PHY mode registers */
+	/* wr_dbi, rd_dbi, TCSR, dq_rd_par, dq_wr_par, ca_par - ALL ENABLED */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR0), 0x77);
+
+	/* Drive strength & write recovery */
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(0xe0, hbm_cfg.hbm_io_drive);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(0x1f, hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.twr);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR1), reg_val);
+
+	/* WL & RL */
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(0x7, ext_wl ? hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat - 9 :
+			      hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat - 1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(0xf8, ext_rl ? hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat - 34 :
+			      hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat - 2);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR2), reg_val);
+
+	/* tRAS, BG enable, BL 4 */
+	reg_val = 0xc0;
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(mr3_tras_mask, hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tras * HDR_TO_SDR);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR3), reg_val);
+
+	/* ECC cfg, extended RL/WL bits, parity latency */
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(mr4_ecc_mask, hbm_cfg.ecc_enable ? 0x3 : 0x0);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(mr4_ext_wl_mask, ext_wl ? 1 : 0);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(mr4_ext_rl_mask, ext_rl ? 1 : 0);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(mr4_parity_lat_mask, hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.parity_lat);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MR4), reg_val);
+
+	/* DramParam0/1 */
+	reg_val = 0x1;		/* BL = 4 */
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0_READLATENCY_M,
+				ext_rl ? hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat - 34 :
+				hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat - 2);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0_WRITELATENCY_M,
+				ext_wl ? hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat - 9 :
+				hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat - 1);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0_PARITYLATENCY_M,
+				hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.parity_lat);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM0_P0), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = 0x01c;	/* Enable rd/wr/cmd parity */
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_EXTREADLATENCY_M,
+				!!ext_rl);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_EXTWRITELATENCY_M,
+				!!ext_wl);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_READDBI_M,
+				hbm_cfg.dbi_enable);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDBI_M,
+				hbm_cfg.dbi_enable);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_ECC_M,
+				hbm_cfg.ecc_enable);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0_WRITEDATAMASK_M,
+				0x1);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DRAMPARAM1_P0), reg_val);
+}
+
+
+static void phy_config_dram_timing(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+
+	/* For CK freq <= 1800MHz */
+	reg_val = (pldm) ? 0x28 : 0x57e4;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING0), reg_val);
+	reg_val = (pldm) ? 0x50 : 0xdbba;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING1), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = 0x1000;	/* Flashbolt tMRD = 8nCK for all CK values*/
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING2_TINIT5_M,
+			      hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tinit5);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING2), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING3_TMOD_M,
+			      hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tmod);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING3_TXS_M,
+			      (hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txs + 1) * HDR_TO_SDR);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING3), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING4_TWTR_M,
+			      hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.twtr_l);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING4_TCKSRE_M,
+			      (hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksre + 1) * HDR_TO_SDR);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING4_TRCD_M,
+			      hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_rd);
+	/* tRCD is the only MC timing parmeter given in SDR. No need to convert */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING4), reg_val);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(0x1f, hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trp * HDR_TO_SDR);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(0xffe0, hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.t_pwreset);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING5), reg_val);
+
+	/* tCCDS, tCCDL, tCCDR - from Flashbolt spec. Frequency independent */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMTIMING8), 0x72);
+}
+
+static void phy_config_zq_cal(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum drive_strength phy_drive_strength)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val, numerator, denominator, base;
+
+	/* Set numerator, denominator and base to determine the phy drive strength */
+	switch (phy_drive_strength) {
+	case DRIVE_STRENGTH_6MA:
+		numerator = 1;
+		denominator = 1;
+		base = 16;
+		break;
+	case DRIVE_STRENGTH_9MA:
+		numerator = 3;
+		denominator = 2;
+		base = 16;
+		break;
+	case DRIVE_STRENGTH_15MA:
+		numerator = 14;
+		denominator = 5;
+		base = 16;
+		break;
+	case DRIVE_STRENGTH_18MA:
+		numerator = 7;
+		denominator = 2;
+		base = 16;
+		break;
+	case DRIVE_STRENGTH_12MA:
+	default:
+		numerator = 15;
+		denominator = 7;
+		base = 16;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	if (hbm_cfg.fast_zq_cal) {
+		/* Set LinearSearchStep and CalNumVotes*/
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALMISC), 0x8);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALMISC2), 0x2);
+
+		reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL));
+		reg_val &= ~(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALENUM_M |
+			    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALEDEN_M |
+			    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALEBASE_M);
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALENUM_M,
+				      numerator);
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALEDEN_M,
+				      denominator);
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL_CALSCALEBASE_M,
+				      base);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSCALECTRL),
+											reg_val);
+
+		reg_val = FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALINITVALS_CALINITNINT_M, 0x28);
+		reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALINITVALS_CALINITPEXT_M, 0x14);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALINITVALS),
+											reg_val);
+	}
+
+	switch (hbm_cfg.ck_freq) {
+	case HBM_PLL_1200:
+		break;
+
+	case HBM_PLL_1600:
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+				D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALCMPSTARTUPTIME_P0), 1603);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+				D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSAMPLETIME_P0), 321);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALOFFSETSAMPLETIME_P0), 241);
+		break;
+
+	case HBM_PLL_1800:
+	default:
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALCMPSTARTUPTIME_P0), 1812);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALSAMPLETIME_P0), 363);
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CALOFFSETSAMPLETIME_P0), 272);
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+static void phy_config_internal_pll(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PLLCTRL4_P0), 0xd8);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PLLCTRL0), 0x86);
+
+	reg_val = (hbm_cfg.ck_freq == HBM_PLL_1200) ? 0x225 : 0xa25;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PLLTESTMODE_P0), reg_val);
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PLLCTRL2_P0), 0x18);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_PLLCTRL1_P0), 0x21);
+}
+
+static void phy_config_lcdl_cal(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_LCDLGAINCTL_P0), 0x62);
+
+	switch (hbm_cfg.ck_freq) {
+	case HBM_PLL_1200:
+		reg_val = 0x22c;
+		break;
+	case HBM_PLL_1600:
+		reg_val = 0x1a0;
+		break;
+	case HBM_PLL_1800:
+	default:
+		reg_val = 0x172;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_LCDLLOCKPARAM_P0), reg_val);
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_LCDLTRAINPARAM),
+			 hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.extend_phased_time);
+}
+
+static void phy_load_initeng(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	/* Part 1 - LP2 Entry Sequence */
+
+	/* Set     [0]=>CalRun   = 1'b1, Start the calibration engine */
+	/* Writing to Address CalRun */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=CalRun,*/
+	/* DATA=1, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG0S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG0S1),
+											0x0268);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG0S2),
+											0x8410);
+
+	/* Set     [0]=>CalRun   = 1'b0, Clear CalRun */
+	/* Writing to Address CalRun */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=CalRun*/
+	/* DATA=0, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG1S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG1S1),
+											0x0268);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG1S2),
+											0x8410);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>TxRxPtrInit = 8'bff, stopping the fifo pointers */
+	/* Writing to Address TxRdPtrInit */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=1, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=TxRdPtrInit*/
+	/* DATA=0xff, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG2S0),
+											0x07f8);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG2S1),
+											0x0368);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG2S2),
+											0x8c10);
+
+	/* Set     [3]=>PllOutBypEn = 1'b1, Put pll in bypass */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=1, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0, */
+	/* DATA=0x0086, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG3S0),
+											0x0430);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG3S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG3S2),
+											0x8c10);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>DfiInitComplete = 80x0 for all channels */
+	/* Writing to Address DfiInitComplete */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=1, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=DfiInitComplete, */
+	/* DATA=0,      DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG4S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG4S1),
+											0x0220);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG4S2),
+											0x8c10);
+
+	/* Part 2 - LP3(S3)  Entry Sequence */
+
+	/* Waiting for 64 cycles before LP3 entry */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP,   GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=16 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG5S0),
+											0x0010);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG5S1),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG5S2),
+											0x1c00);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>TxRxPtrInit       = 8'bff, stopping the fifo pointers*/
+	/* Writing to Address TxRdPtrInit */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=TxRdPtrInit*/
+	/* DATA=0xff, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG6S0),
+											0x07ff);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG6S1),
+											0x0368);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG6S2),
+											0x9c10);
+
+	/* Set [5]=>PllBypClkSel = 1'b1   [3]=>PllOutBypEn = 1'b1*/
+	/* Put pll in bypass */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x0096, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG7S0),
+											0x04b0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG7S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG7S2),
+											0x9c10);
+
+	/* Reset   [0]=>PllPwrDn          = 1'b1,  Pll in Power Down */
+	/* Writing to Address PllPwrDn */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllPwrDn */
+	/* DATA=1, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG8S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG8S1),
+											0x0338);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG8S2),
+											0x9c10);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>DfiInitComplete   = 80x0,  for all channels */
+	/* Writing to Address DfiInitComplete */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=DfiInitComplete */
+	/* DATA=0,      DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG9S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG9S1),
+											0x0220);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG9S2),
+											0x9c10);
+
+	/* Part 3 - Wait instruction for Detecting Start De-assertion of LP2 */
+	/* and LP3 exit */
+
+	/* Adding Wait instruction which will wait here for the Start to be */
+	/* de-asserted */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WAIT,  GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG10S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG10S1),
+											0x0001);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG10S2),
+											0x0400);
+
+	/* Part 4 - LP3(S3) Exit  Sequence */
+
+	/* Setting [7:0]=>DfiInitComplete = 80xff for all channels */
+	/* Writing to Address DfiInitComplete */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=3, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=DfiInitComplete */
+	/* DATA=0xff,   DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG11S0),
