+											0x07f8);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG11S1),
+											0x0220);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG11S2),
+											0x9c10);
+
+	/* Part 5 - LP2 Exit Sequence + Dfi Initialization */
+
+	/* Ensuring Pstate bits get written with the dfi_frequency value */
+	/* Writing to Address PState */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRVAL, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PState */
+	/* REGSEL=0, DELAY_S=3 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG12S0),
+											0x0003);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG12S1),
+											0x006a);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG12S2),
+											0x1410);
+
+	/* Set   [2:0]=>InitSeqWaitCondSel = 3'b4,  */
+	/* Configure to wait for Impedance Calibration Done */
+	/* Writing to Address InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* DATA=4, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG13S0),
+											0x0020);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG13S1),
+											0x0788);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG13S2),
+											0x8448);
+
+	/* Waiting for 1us before de-asserting PwrDn or Standby */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP_R, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, REGSEL=1 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG14S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG14S1),
+											0x0004);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG14S2),
+											0x0400);
+
+	/* Reset   [0]=>PllPwrDn    = 1'b0, Pll out of power down */
+	/* Writing to Address PllPwrDn */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllPwrDn */
+	/* DATA=0, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG15S0),
+											0x0007);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG15S1),
+											0x0338);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG15S2),
+											0x8410);
+
+	/* Reset   [0]=>PllStandby  = 1'b0,  Pll out of standby */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=1, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x0086,   DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG16S0),
+											0x0437);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG16S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG16S2),
+0x8c10);
+
+	/* Set     [5]=>GearShit = 1'b1 and [3]=>Preset = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x00AE,   DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG17S0),
+											0x0570);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG17S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG17S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 1us before de-asserting Preset */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP_R, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, REGSEL=1 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG18S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG18S1),
+											0x0004);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG18S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Reset   [3]=>Preset = 1'b0 */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x00A6,   DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG19S0),
+											0x0530);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG19S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG19S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 0.5us before de-asserting Gearshift */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP_R, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, REGSEL=2 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG20S0),
+											0x0010);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG20S1),
+											0x0004);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG20S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Reset  [5]=>Gearshift = 1'b0 */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x0086,   DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG21S0),
+											0x0430);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG21S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG21S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 10us for the PLL to lock */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP_R, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, REGSEL=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG22S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG22S1),
+											0x0004);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG22S2),
+											0x0400);
+
+	/* Waiting for 0.5us for the Pll to lock */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP_R, GRP=1, FLAG=CONT, REGSEL=2 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG23S0),
+											0x0010);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG23S1),
+											0x0004);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG23S2),
+											0x0c00);
+
+	/* Reset   [2]=>PllBypMode = 1'b1, Pull pll out of bypass */
+	/* Writing to Address PllCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PllCtrl0, BYPDAT=0x0096 */
+	/* DATA=0x0082, DELAY_S=0 */
+	if (pldm)
+		/* PLL bypass */
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG24S0), 0x04b0);
+	else
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG24S0), 0x0410);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG24S1),
+											0x02d0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG24S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Adding Wait instruction which will wait here for the */
+	/* ZQ Calibration to be done */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WAIT,  GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG25S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG25S1),
+											0x0001);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG25S2),
+											0x0400);
+
+	/* Set   [2:0]=>InitSeqWaitCondSel = 3'b7,  Configure to wait for */
+	/* LCDL Calibration Done */
+	/* Writing to Address InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* DATA=7, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG26S0),
+											0x0038);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG26S1),
+											0x0788);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG26S2),
+											0x9448);
+
+	/* Set     [0]=>PClkEnAsyncCtrl = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address ClockingCtrl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=ClockingCtrl */
+	/* DATA=1, DELAY_S=3 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG27S0),
+											0x000b);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG27S1),
+											0x0040);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG27S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Set     Set [0]=>LcdlResetRelock = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address LcdlControl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=LcdlControl */
+	/* DATA=1, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG28S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG28S1),
+											0x0208);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG28S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Set     [1]=>LcdlTrackEnCtrl = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address ClockingCtrl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=ClockingCtrl */
+	/* DATA=3, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG29S0),
+											0x0018);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG29S1),
+											0x0040);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG29S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Reset   Set [0]=>LcdlResetRelock = 1'b0 */
+	/* Writing to Address LcdlControl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=LcdlControl */
+	/* DATA=0, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG30S0),
+											0x0007);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG30S1),
+											0x0208);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG30S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Adding Wait instruction which will wait here for the LCDL */
+	/* Calibration to be done */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WAIT,  GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG31S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG31S1),
+											0x0001);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG31S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Reset   [1]=>LcdlTrackEnCtrl = 1'b0 */
+	/* Writing to Address ClockingCtrl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=ClockingCtrl */
+	/* DATA=1, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG32S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG32S1),
+											0x0040);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG32S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Reset   [0]=>PclkEnAsyncCtrl = 1'b0 */
+	/* Writing to Address ClockingCtrl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=ClockingCtrl */
+	/* DATA=0, DELAY_S=3 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG33S0),
+											0x0003);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG33S1),
+											0x0040);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG33S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 24 cycles to allow Calibration to complete final cycle */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP,   GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=6 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG34S0),
+											0x0006);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG34S1),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG34S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>TxRxPtrInit = 8'b0 , starting the fifo pointers */
+	/* Writing to Address TxRdPtrInit */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=TxRdPtrInit */
+	/* DATA=0, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG35S0),
+											0x0007);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG35S1),
+											0x0368);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG35S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 68 cycles (for Rx I/O power-up) before triggering */
+	/* reset rxdat fifos */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP,   GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=17 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG36S0),
+											0x0011);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG36S1),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG36S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Set [0]=>PLLockDone = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address PorControl */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=PorControl */
+	/* DATA=0x0001, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG37S0),
+											0x0008);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG37S1),
+											0x0360);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG37S2),
+											0x8410);
+
+	/* Write CmdSeqReg0 Instruction cmd=FIFO_RST, addr=DWORD DATA RX */
+	/* Writing to Address CmdSeqReg0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=CmdSeqReg0 */
+	/* DATA=0x00B6, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG38S0),
+											0x05b0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG38S1),
+											0x0070);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG38S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Set CmdSeqRun=1, CmdSeqStartAddr=0, CmdSeqStopAddr=0 */
+	/* Writing to Address CmdSeqCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=CmdSeqCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x0001, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG39S0),
+											0x000f);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG39S1),
+											0x00f0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG39S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Set CmdSeqRun=0, CmdSeqStartAddr=0, CmdSeqStopAddr=0 */
+	/* Writing to Address CmdSeqCtrl0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=CmdSeqCtrl0 */
+	/* DATA=0x0000, DELAY_S=7 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG40S0),
+											0x0007);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG40S1),
+											0x00f0);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG40S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Waiting for 68 cycles to allow CmdSeq time to complete */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP,   GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, DELAY_L=17 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG41S0),
+											0x0011);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG41S1),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG41S2),
+											0x1400);
+
+	/* Set     [1]=>LcdlTrackEnCtrl = 1'b1 */
+	/* Writing to Address ClockingCtrl  */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=ClockingCtrl */
+	/* DATA=2, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG42S0),
+											0x0010);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG42S1),
+											0x0040);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG42S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Set   [7:0]=>DfiInitComplete = 80xff for all channels */
+	/* Writing to Address DfiInitComplete */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=DfiInitComplete */
+	/* DATA=0xff, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG43S0),
+											0x07f8);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG43S1),
+											0x0220);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG43S2),
+											0x9410);
+
+	/* Part 6 - Enabling LP2 sequence */
+
+	/* Set   [2:0]=>InitSeqWaitCondSel = 3'b2, Configure to wait for */
+	/* Init Start = 0 */
+	/* Writing to Address InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=2, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=InitSeqWaitCondSel */
+	/* DATA=2, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG44S0),
+											0x0010);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG44S1),
+											0x0788);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG44S2),
+											0x9448);
+
+	/* Set  [15:0]=>InitSeqDisableFlag = 160xfff0 */
+	/* Writing to Address InitSeqDisableFlag1 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=InitSeqDisableFlag1 */
+	/* DATA=0xfff0, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG45S0),
+											0xff80);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG45S1),
+											0x06ff);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG45S2),
+											0x8448);
+
+	/* Set  [15:0]=>InitSeqDisableFlag = 160xffff */
+	/* Writing to Address InitSeqDisableFlag0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=WRITE, GRP=0, FLAG=CONT, ADDR=InitSeqDisableFlag0 */
+	/* DATA=0xffff, DELAY_S=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG46S0),
+											0xfff8);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG46S1),
+											0x06f7);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG46S2),
+											0x8448);
+
+	/* Terminating by setting the Terminate Flag to 0 */
+	/* INSTR = OP=NOP,   GRP=0, FLAG=TERM, DELAY_L=0 */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG47S0),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG47S1),
+											0x0000);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_INITENG_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_INITENG_INITSEQREG47S2),
+											0x0000);
+}
+
+static void phy_clear_errors(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DATACTRL),
+						HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DATACTRL_CLRSTATUS_M);
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DATACTRL), 0x0);
+
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL), 0x1,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRCLRSTATUS_M);
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL), 0x0,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRCLRSTATUS_M);
+}
+
+static int phy_error_aword(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t ch)
+{
+	int rc = STATUS_PASS;
+	uint32_t reg_val, ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWDATABITERR0) + ch_offset);
+	if (reg_val) {
+		HBM_DBG("CH%d AwDataBitErr0: 0x%x", ch, reg_val);
+		rc++;
+	}
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWDATABITERR1) + ch_offset);
+	/* Skip ARFU[1] & ARFU[3] */
+	if (reg_val != 0x28 && reg_val != 0) {
+		HBM_DBG("CH%d AwDataBitErr1: 0x%x", ch, reg_val);
+		rc++;
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+static int phy_error_dword(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t ch, uint32_t dw)
+{
+	int rc = STATUS_PASS;
+	uint32_t byte, reg_val, ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+	uint32_t dw_offset = ch_offset + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWDATASTATUS0) + dw_offset);
+
+	if (reg_val & HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWDATASTATUS0_DWDATAERR_M) {
+		HBM_DBG("Errors found on CH%d DW%d. DwDataStatus0: 0x%x", ch, dw, reg_val);
+		for (byte = 0; byte < 4; byte++) {
+			reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWDB0DATABITERR)
+					       + dw_offset + byte * BYTE_OFFSET);
+			if (!reg_val)
+				continue;
+
+			HBM_DBG("      %sCH%d DW%d byte%d DataBitErr: 0x%x", SPACES10, ch, dw, byte,
+				      reg_val);
+		}
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+/* return 0 if no errors found */
+static int phy_error(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int size = 0, rc = STATUS_PASS;
+	uint32_t reg_val, temp_err, cattrip_err, ch_idx, dw_idx;
+	char str[100] = {0};
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CHNSTATUS));
+	if (reg_val) {
+		size += snprintk(str + size, sizeof(str) - size,
+				 "HBM%d PHY errors found on channels: ", hbm_dev);
+		for (ch_idx = 0; ch_idx < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch_idx++) {
+			if (reg_val & BIT(ch_idx))
+				size += snprintk(str + size, sizeof(str) - size, "%d ", ch_idx);
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("%s", str);
+	}
+
+	for (ch_idx = 0; ch_idx < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch_idx++) {
+		rc |= phy_error_aword(hbm_dev, ch_idx);
+
+		for (dw_idx = 0; dw_idx < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw_idx++)
+			rc |= phy_error_dword(hbm_dev, ch_idx, dw_idx);
+
+		/* TEMP[2:0] and CATTRRIP */
+		reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_WSOREADSTATUS));
+		temp_err = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_WSOREADSTATUS_TEMPERR_M, reg_val);
+		cattrip_err = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_WSOREADSTATUS_CATTRIPERR_M, reg_val);
+		if (temp_err || cattrip_err) {
+			/* This is due to Cadance known design bug, Jira - [SW-93017] */
+			if (!pldm) {
+				LOG_ERR("Errors found on MIDSTACK signals: HBM%d CH%d TempERR: 0x%x, CattripERR: 0x%x",
+					hbm_dev, ch_idx, temp_err, cattrip_err);
+				rc |= STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	phy_clear_errors(hbm_dev);
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int phy_test_loopback(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : PHY_TIMEOUT_US;
+	uint32_t sleep = pldm ? PLDM_POLL_INTERVAL : POLL_INTERVAL_US;
+	uint32_t val, bist_status_valid;
+
+	if (!PHY_LOOPBACK_EN)
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+
+	/* Fix density on PUB DeviceId CSR - 16Gb per channel */
+	if (pldm)
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_DRAMDEVICEID4),
+											0xA100);
+
+	bist_status_valid = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTDONE_M |
+			    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBDWDONE_M |
+			    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBAWDONE_M;
+	if (!pldm)
+		bist_status_valid |= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBDWTRAINEYEDONE_M |
+				     HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBDWTRAINRDLATDONE_M |
+				     HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBAWTRAINEYEDONE_M |
+				     HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTLBAWTRAINRDLATDONE_M;
+
+	/* loads the EXTEST_RX command through the P1500
+	 * in order to tristate the DRAM outputs
+	 */
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL), 0x1,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL_TRISTATEDRAMEN_M);
+
+	k_msleep(PHY_LOOPBACK_TIMEOUT_MS);
+
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL), 0x0,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL_TRISTATEDRAMEN_M);
+
+	/* BistTrainNumWords = 12; BistMode = concurrent AW/DW */
+	val = FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTMODE_M, 0x3);
+	val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTTRAINNUMWORDS_M, 0xc);
+
+	if (!pldm) {
+		/* AWORD and DWORD read latency and eye training */
+		val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTAWTRAINRDLATEN_M, 0x1);
+		val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTAWTRAINEYEEN_M, 0x1);
+		val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTDWTRAINEYEEN_M, 0x1);
+		val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTDWTRAINRDLATEN_M, 0x1);
+	}
+
+	/* Trigger BIST run */
+	val |= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTINSTR_M;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0), val);
+
+	/* Reset BIST trigger */
+	val ^= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0_BISTINSTR_M;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTCTRL0), val);
+
+	rc = PHY_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS), val,
+				 (val & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_BISTSTATUS_BISTDONE_M), sleep, timeout);
+
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("Timeout while waiting for PHY loopback BIST to complete");
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (val != bist_status_valid) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY loopback BIST failed. BistStatus: 0x%x", hbm_dev, val);
+		phy_error(hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	/* Should be considered only if BISTSTATUS showed erros */
+	if (phy_error(hbm_dev))
+		LOG_ERR("Loopback passed but mismatches have been FOUND");
+
+	phy_clear_errors(hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d PHY internal loopback PASSED. BistStatus: 0x%x", hbm_dev, val);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+int phy_trigger_lanerepair_fsm(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t ctrl, uint32_t expected_status)
+{
+	uint32_t timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : PHY_TIMEOUT_US;
+	uint32_t sleep = pldm ? PLDM_POLL_INTERVAL : POLL_INTERVAL_US;
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+	int rc;
+
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL), ctrl);
+	ctrl ^= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRRUN_M;
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL), ctrl);
+
+	rc = PHY_POLLING_TIMEOUT(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS),
+				 reg_val,
+				 (reg_val & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRDONE_M),
+				 sleep, timeout);
+
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("Timeout while polling for PHY LaneRepair FSM completion");
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (reg_val != expected_status) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d LANEREPAIR FSM failed. LaneRepairCtrl = 0x%x. LaneRepairStatus = 0x%x",
+			hbm_dev, expected_status, reg_val);
+		phy_error(hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	if (phy_error(hbm_dev)) {
+		LOG_ERR("Broken lanes were detected during LaneRepair FSM operation. LaneRepairStatus = 0x%x",
+			reg_val);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+
+static int poll_on_phy_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t mc_offset, mc_idx, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), reg_val = 0;
+	uint32_t timeout = pldm ? PLDM_TIMEOUT : PHY_TIMEOUT_US;
+	uint32_t sleep = pldm ? PLDM_POLL_INTERVAL : POLL_INTERVAL_US;
+
+	for (mc_idx = 0; mc_idx < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc_idx++) {
+		mc_offset = hbm_offset + mc_idx * MC_OFFSET;
+		rc = HBM_POLLING_TIMEOUT(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_INIT_COMPLETE) + mc_offset,
+					 reg_val,
+					 ((reg_val & MC_CH_DFI_PHY_INIT_COMPLETE_VAL_M) == 0x1),
+					 sleep, timeout);
+
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("Timeout while polling on HBM%d dfi_init_complete assertion",
+				hbm_dev);
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+/* assert/deassert dfi_init_start towards all MCs channels
+ * PHY will not complete init unless all 8 channels asserted dfi_init_start
+ * Therefore, both MCs must assert dfi_init_start at the same time
+ */
+int phy_dfi_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Disable PHY updates */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH_BCAST_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH_BCAST_AW_DFIPHYUPD), 0x0);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_INIT_START), 0x1);
+
+	k_busy_wait(PHY_INIT_START_US);
+
+	if (poll_on_phy_init(hbm_dev))
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_INIT_START), 0x0);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+inline void hbm_phy_rmw(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t addr, uint32_t val, uint32_t mask)
+{
+	uint8_t chiplet_type;
+	uint32_t phy_addr, hbm_offset, reg_val;
+
+	hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	chiplet_type = phy_get_chiplet_type_and_offset(addr);
+	phy_addr = hbm_offset + addr - phy_access.offset;
+
+	/* set chiplet type only if needed */
+	if (chiplet_type != phy_access.chiplet) {
+		phy_access.chiplet = chiplet_type;
+		reg_val = FIELD_PREP(CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS_PSTATE_M, phy_access.pstate);
+		reg_val |= phy_access.chiplet;
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_INDIRECT_ACCESS) +
+									hbm_offset, reg_val);
+	}
+	HBM_RMWREG32(phy_addr, val, mask, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+}
+
+
+void phy_reset(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Cold reset */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_POWERGOOD), 0x0);
+	/* Warm reset */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_HBMPHY_RESET), 0x1);
+	/* APB */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_PRESETN), 0x0);
+	/* DFI */
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_DFI_RESET_N), 0x0);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_POWERGOOD), 0x1);
+
+	/* 64 DfiClks */
+	k_busy_wait(PHY_POWERGOOD_US);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_HBMPHY_RESET), 0x0);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_PRESETN), 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_DFI_RESET_N), 0x1);
+	/* Secured + Privilliged */
+#if defined(CONFIG_SVE_FLAVOR_HOST_DIRECT) || defined(CONFIG_SVE_FLAVOR_HOST_INDIRECT)
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_PPROT_PIN), 0x0);
+#else
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_PHY_PPROT_PIN), 0x1);
+#endif
+}
+
+/* returns the precentage of the eye relative to 1UI */
+static int calc_eye_ratio_ui(uint16_t min_reg, uint16_t max_reg)
+{
+	if (!min_reg && !max_reg)
+		return 0;
+
+	if (min_reg & BIT(RD_EYE_PREV_UI))
+		return ((NUM_TAPS_PER_1UI - (min_reg & RD_EYE_MIN_DLY_M) + max_reg + 1) * 100) /
+			 NUM_TAPS_PER_1UI;
+	/* else */
+	return ((max_reg - min_reg + 1) * 100) / NUM_TAPS_PER_1UI;
+}
+
+static void debug_print_lcdls_1ui(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int size = 0, str_len = 500;
+	uint32_t ch_offset, offset, ch, dw;
+	char *s = NULL;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints || !PRINT_LCDL_1UI_EN)
+		return;
+
+	s = k_calloc(str_len, sizeof(char));
+	if (!s) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to allocate kernel memory for print LCDLs 1UI results");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s<0> *** HBM%d LCDLs 1UI ***", SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("  CH   :%sAddress/Command %s   CK", SPACES10, SPACES5);
+	HBM_DBG("  ---- : ---------------------------------------");
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+		HBM_DBG("  CH-%d :     %s0x%03x  %s0x%03x", ch, SPACES10,
+			hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXACLCDL1UI) + ch_offset), SPACES10,
+			hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXCKLCDL1UI) + ch_offset));
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n     CH%s%s%sDW-0%s%s%sDW-1%s%s%s  DW-2%s%s%sDW-3", SPACES10,
+		      SPACES10, SPACES5, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10,
+		      SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10);
+
+	HBM_DBG(" ----- : --------%s---%s---%s---%s--", DASHES30, DASHES30, DASHES30, DASHES30);
+	HBM_DBG("%s%s%s %s   %s %s   %s %s   %s",
+		SPACES10, SPACES5, UI_PRINT_HEADER, SPACES5, UI_PRINT_HEADER, SPACES5,
+		UI_PRINT_HEADER, SPACES5, UI_PRINT_HEADER);
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+		size = 0;
+		size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "      ");
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			offset = ch_offset + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 " 0x%02x      0x%02x      0x%02x        ",
+					 hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKLCDL1UI) +
+						      offset),
+					 hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNLCDL1UI) +
+						      offset),
+					 hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQLCDL1UI) +
+						      offset));
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("  CH-%d :%s", ch, s);
+	}
+
+	k_free(s);
+}
+
+
+static void print_training_dw(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct dw_training *dw_res)
+{
+	int size = 0, str_len = 500;
+	uint32_t ratio = 0, dw_res_offset, ratio_sum = 0, ratio_min = 100, ch, pc, dw;
+	char *s = NULL;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	s = k_calloc(str_len, sizeof(char));
+	if (!s) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to allocate kernel memory for print training results");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s<2> *** HBM%d DWORDs DFIM read latency ***", SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("   CH%sDW-0  DW-1  DW-2  DW-3", SPACES10);
+	HBM_DBG("  ----- : -------------------------------------");
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		size = 0;
+		size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "      ");
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "0x%02x  ",
+					 dw_res[ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + dw].rx.read_latency);
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("   CH-%d :%s", ch, s);
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n\n%s<3> *** HBM%d DWORDs RDQS rising edge delay ***",
+		SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("  %s%sDW-0%s%s%sDW-1%s%s%sDW-2%s%s%sDW-3",
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10,
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10);
+	HBM_DBG(" ----- : --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------");
+	HBM_DBG("  CH   :  result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max",
+		SPACES5, SPACES5, SPACES5);
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		size = 0;
+		size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "  CH-%d :", ch);
+		for (pc = 0;  pc < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc++) {
+			dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + pc * NUM_PC_PER_CH;
+			/* 1st DWORD */
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.max);
+			/* 2nd DWORD */
+			dw_res_offset++;
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.max);
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("%s", s);
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n\n%s<4> *** HBM%d DWORDs RDQS falling edge delay ***",
+		SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("  %s%sDW-0%s%s%sDW-1%s%s%sDW-2%s%s%sDW-3",
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10,
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10);
+	HBM_DBG(" ----- : --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------");
+	HBM_DBG("  CH   :  result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max",
+		SPACES5, SPACES5, SPACES5);
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		size = 0;
+		size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "  CH-%d :", ch);
+		for (pc = 0;  pc < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc++) {
+			dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + pc * NUM_PC_PER_CH;
+			/* 1st DWORD */
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.max);
+			/* 2nd DWORD */
+			dw_res_offset++;
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.max);
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("%s", s);
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s%s%s%sFor all DWORDs - RD eye avg ratio: %d%% RD eye min ratio: %d%%\n",
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES5, ratio_sum /
+		(NUM_RDQS_EDGES * NUM_DW_PER_HBM), ratio_min);
+
+	ratio_sum = 0;
+	ratio_min = 100;
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s<5> *** HBM%d DWORDs DQ transmit delay ***",
+		SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("  %s%sDW-0%s%s%sDW-1%s%s%sDW-2%s%s%sDW-3", SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10,
+		      SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10,
+		      SPACES10);
+	HBM_DBG(" ----- : --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------");
+	HBM_DBG("  CH   :  result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max%s result (ratio)   Min  -  Max",
+		SPACES5, SPACES5, SPACES5);
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		size = 0;
+		size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size, "  CH-%d :", ch);
+		for (pc = 0;  pc < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc++) {
+			dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + pc * NUM_PC_PER_CH;
+			/* 1st DWORD */
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.max);
+			/* 2nd DWORD */
+			dw_res_offset++;
+			ratio = dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.ratio_1ui;
+			ratio_sum += ratio;
+			ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+			size += snprintk(s + size, str_len - size,
+					 "   0x%02x   (%02d%%)    0x%02x - 0x%02x    ",
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.final,
+					 ratio,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.min,
+					 dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.max);
+		}
+		HBM_DBG("%s", s);
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s%s%s%sFor all DWORDs - WR eye avg ratio: %d%% WR eye min ratio: %d%%\n",
+		SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES5, ratio_sum / NUM_DW_PER_HBM, ratio_min);
+
+	k_free(s);
+}
+
+static void print_training_ca(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct lcdl *ca_res, uint32_t *ck_res)
+{
+	int ratio = 0, ratio_sum = 0, ratio_min = 100;
+	uint32_t ch;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_DBG("\n\n%s<1> *** HBM%d Command-Address transmit delay ***", SPACES40, hbm_dev);
+	HBM_DBG("  CH   :   result (ratio)%sMin  -  Max", SPACES5);
+	HBM_DBG(" ----- : -------------------------------------");
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		ratio = ca_res[ch].ratio_1ui;
+		ratio_sum += ratio;
+		ratio_min = ratio < ratio_min ? ratio : ratio_min;
+		HBM_DBG("  CH-%d :    0x%02x   (%02d%%)      0x%02x - 0x%02x",
+			ch,
+			ca_res[ch].final,
+			ratio,
+			ca_res[ch].min,
+			ca_res[ch].max);
+	}
+	HBM_DBG("\n%s%s%s%sFor all Channels - CA eye avg ratio: %d%% CA eye min ratio: %d%%\n",
+		      SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES10, SPACES5, ratio_sum / HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM,
+		      ratio_min);
+}
+
+static void print_training_vref(uint32_t hbm_dev, struct vref_training *vref_res)
+{
+	/* Ignore PHY VREF training results for other training types */
+	if (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.training_type != TRAIN_TYPE_HW_ONLY)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_DBG("<6> VREF training\n");
+	HBM_DBG("VREF-Min = 0x%04x       VREF-Max = 0x%04x\n", vref_res->min, vref_res->max);
+}
+
+static int phy_get_training_results(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	struct vref_training *vref_res = NULL;
+	struct dw_training *dw_res = NULL;
+	struct lcdl *ca_res = NULL;
+	int rc = STATUS_PASS, fail_cntr = 0;
+	uint32_t *ck_res = NULL, ch_offset, dw_offset, dw_res_offset, reg_val, ch, dw;
+
+	ca_res = k_calloc(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM, sizeof(struct lcdl));
+	ck_res = k_calloc(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM, sizeof(uint32_t));
+	dw_res = k_calloc(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM,
+			  sizeof(struct dw_training));
+	vref_res = k_calloc(HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM, sizeof(uint32_t));
+	if (!dw_res || !ca_res  || !ck_res || !vref_res) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to allocate kernel memory for HW training results");
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+		goto free_train_mem;
+	}
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+
+		/* Command Address */
+		ca_res[ch].min = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXACMINDLY) + ch_offset);
+		ca_res[ch].max = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXACMAXDLY) + ch_offset);
+		ca_res[ch].final = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXACDLY_P0) + ch_offset);
+
+		ca_res[ch].ratio_1ui = calc_eye_ratio_ui(ca_res[ch].min, ca_res[ch].max);
+		if (ca_res[ch].ratio_1ui < PHY_MIN_TRAINING_EYE_TH)
+			fail_cntr++;
+
+		/* CK */
+		ck_res[ch] = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		  D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_TXCKDLY_P0) +  ch_offset);
+
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			dw_offset = ch_offset + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + dw;
+
+			/* Read latency */
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.read_latency =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DFIMRL_P0) + dw_offset);
+
+			/* Write eye */
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.min =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.max =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.final =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+		     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.wdqs.final =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQSDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.ratio_1ui =
+					calc_eye_ratio_ui(dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.min,
+							  dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.max);
+			if (dw_res[dw_res_offset].tx.dq.ratio_1ui < PHY_MIN_TRAINING_EYE_TH)
+				fail_cntr++;
+
+			/* Read eye */
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.min =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.max =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.final =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.min =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.max =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.final =
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+	     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.ratio_1ui =
+					calc_eye_ratio_ui(dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.min,
+							  dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.max);
+			if (dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_rise.ratio_1ui < PHY_MIN_TRAINING_EYE_TH)
+				fail_cntr++;
+
+			dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.ratio_1ui =
+					calc_eye_ratio_ui(dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.min,
+							  dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.max);
+
+			if (dw_res[dw_res_offset].rx.rdqs_fall.ratio_1ui < PHY_MIN_TRAINING_EYE_TH)
+				fail_cntr++;
+		}
+	}
+
+	if (fail_cntr) {
+		LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d - %d violations of eye width (<%d%%)", hbm_dev, fail_cntr,
+			    PHY_MIN_TRAINING_EYE_TH);
+	}
+
+	/* VREF */
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFINDACSTATUS));
+	vref_res->min = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFINDACSTATUS_GLOBALVREFINDACMIN_M,
+				  reg_val);
+	vref_res->max = FIELD_GET(HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFINDACSTATUS_GLOBALVREFINDACMAX_M,
+				  reg_val);
+
+	HBM_DBG("\nMiscStatus: 0x%x", hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MISCSTATUS)));
+	HBM_DBG("\nChnStatus: 0x%x", hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CHNSTATUS)));
+
+	debug_print_lcdls_1ui(hbm_dev);
+	print_training_ca(hbm_dev, ca_res, ck_res);
+	print_training_dw(hbm_dev, dw_res);
+	print_training_vref(hbm_dev, vref_res);
+
+free_train_mem:
+	k_free(ca_res);
+	k_free(ck_res);
+	k_free(dw_res);
+	k_free(vref_res);
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+static void phy_set_read_latency(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint16_t rd_lat)
+{
+	uint32_t dw_offset, ch, dw;
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			dw_offset =  ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DFIMRL_P0) + dw_offset,
+				      rd_lat & HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DFIMRL_P0_VAL_M);
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+static void phy_set_vref(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint8_t vref_val)
+{
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFINGLOBAL_P0), vref_val,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFINGLOBAL_P0_GLOBALVREFINDAC_M);
+	/* VREF settling time is up to 2.5us */
+	k_busy_wait(PHY_VREF_SETTLING_US);
+}
+
+static uint32_t get_avg_eye_ratio(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t dw_rise_edge_ratio, dw_fall_edge_ratio, offset, phy_ch, dw, sum_eyes_ratios = 0;
+
+	for (phy_ch = 0; phy_ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; phy_ch++) {
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			offset = phy_ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			dw_rise_edge_ratio =
+				calc_eye_ratio_ui(hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMINDLY) +
+					     offset),
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMAXDLY) +
+					     offset));
+			dw_fall_edge_ratio =
+				calc_eye_ratio_ui(hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMINDLY) +
+					     offset),
+				hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMAXDLY) +
+					     offset));
+			sum_eyes_ratios += dw_rise_edge_ratio + dw_fall_edge_ratio;
+		}
+	}
+
+	return sum_eyes_ratios / (HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM * NUM_RDQS_EDGES);
+}
+
+static void debug_print_train_param_status(uint32_t hbm_dev, uint32_t train_status,
+					   enum train_params train_param)
+{
+	uint32_t train_done_mask, train_err_mask;
+	char *status_str, *train_param_str;
+
+	switch (train_param) {
+	case TRAIN_CA:
+		train_param_str = "CA";
+		train_done_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINCADONE_M;
+		train_err_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINCAERR_M;
+		break;
+	case TRAIN_RD_LAT:
+		train_param_str = "RD LAT";
+		train_done_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINRLDONE_M;
+		train_err_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINRLERR_M;
+		break;
+	case TRAIN_RD_EYE:
+		train_param_str = "RD EYE";
+		train_done_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINREYEDONE_M;
+		train_err_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINREYEERR_M;
+		break;
+	case TRAIN_VREF:
+		train_param_str = "VREF";
+		train_done_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINVREFDONE_M;
+		train_err_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINVREFERR_M;
+		break;
+	case TRAIN_WR_EYE:
+		train_param_str = "WR EYE";
+		train_done_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINWEYEDONE_M;
+		train_err_mask = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINWEYEERR_M;
+		break;
+
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("No such train param num %d, FAILED", train_param);
+		break;
+	}
+
+	if ((train_status & train_done_mask) && !(train_status & train_err_mask))
+		status_str = "PASSED";
+	else
+		status_str = "FAILED";
+
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d %s training %s", hbm_dev, train_param_str, status_str);
+}
+
+static void debug_print_training_status(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t train_status, train_ctrl;
+
+	if (!hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)
+		return;
+
+	train_status = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS)) &
+				    PHY_TRAINSTATUS_M;
+	train_ctrl = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,  D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL));
+
+	if (train_ctrl & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINCAEN_M)
+		debug_print_train_param_status(hbm_dev, train_status, TRAIN_CA);
+
+	if (train_ctrl & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINRLEN_M)
+		debug_print_train_param_status(hbm_dev, train_status, TRAIN_RD_LAT);
+
+	if (train_ctrl & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINREYEEN_M)
+		debug_print_train_param_status(hbm_dev, train_status, TRAIN_RD_EYE);
+
+	if (train_ctrl & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINVREFEN_M)
+		debug_print_train_param_status(hbm_dev, train_status, TRAIN_VREF);
+
+	if (train_ctrl & HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINWEYEEN_M)
+		debug_print_train_param_status(hbm_dev, train_status, TRAIN_WR_EYE);
+}
+
+static void hbm_set_vref(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum hbm_vref vref_val)
+{
+	uint32_t mc;
+	const uint8_t vref_mr = 15;
+
+	for (mc = 0; mc < GAUDI3_NUM_OF_MC_PER_HBM; mc++) {
+		mc_mrs_cmd(hbm_dev, mc, vref_mr, vref_val);
+	}
+}
+
+
+static int phy_trigger_hw_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t train_ctrl, train_status_valid, reg_val;
+	int rc;
+
+	train_status_valid = 1 << HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINDONE_S |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINCADONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINRLDONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINREYEDONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINVREFDONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_TRAINWEYEDONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.hbm_reset <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_DRAMINITRSTDONE_S) |
+			     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.mrs <<
+			      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS_DRAMINITMRSDONE_S);
+
+	train_ctrl = hbm_cfg.train_cfg.training_type == TRAIN_TYPE_SEMI_AUTO ? 1 : 0;
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCFG), train_ctrl,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCFG_RDLATTRAINMODE_M);
+
+	train_ctrl = 1 << HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINRUN_S |
+		     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca << HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINCAEN_S) |
+		     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl << HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINRLEN_S) |
+		     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye <<
+		      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINREYEEN_S) |
+		     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref <<
+		      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINVREFEN_S) |
+		     (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye <<
+		      HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL_TRAINWEYEEN_S);
+
+	/* trigger H/W training */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), train_ctrl);
+
+	/* de-assert TrainRun for future training and/or DRAM init */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCTRL), train_ctrl & (~1));
+
+	rc = phy_poll_on_training(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		return rc;
+
+	reg_val = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINSTATUS)) & PHY_TRAINSTATUS_M;
+	if (reg_val != train_status_valid)
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+
+static int semi_auto_read_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t phy_vref, dev_avg_eye_ratio, sum_valid_vref_eyes = 0,
+		weighted_sum_valid_vref_eyes = 0, passing_regions_cnt = 0,
+		opt_phy_vref, valid_vref_start = UINT32_MAX, valid_vref_end = UINT32_MAX,
+		passing_region_size = 0, passing_region_max = 0, final_opt_vref = DEFAULT_PHY_VREF,
+		phy_vref_valid_ratio = 0, training_fail_cnt = 0;
+	bool training_fail;
+#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+	struct hl_hbm_drv_data *drv_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(DEVICE_DT_GET(DT_DRV_INST(0)));
+	struct single_hbm_train_result *single_hbm_res = &(drv_data->train_res->hbm_dev_tr_res[hbm_dev]);
+#endif
+
+	int rc;
+
+	/* Enable RD training only */
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.hbm_reset = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.mrs = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye = 1;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye = 0;
+
+	for (phy_vref = 1; phy_vref <= PHY_VREF_OPTIONS; phy_vref++) {
+		phy_set_vref(hbm_dev, phy_vref);
+		training_fail = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+		/*if training_fail....*/
+		training_fail |= hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CHNSTATUS));
+
+		if (training_fail || phy_vref == PHY_VREF_OPTIONS) {
+			if (valid_vref_start != UINT32_MAX) {
+				/* Passing region had ended. Calculate relevant parameters */
+				valid_vref_end = phy_vref - 1;
+				passing_region_size = valid_vref_end - valid_vref_start + 1;
+				opt_phy_vref = weighted_sum_valid_vref_eyes / sum_valid_vref_eyes;
+				passing_regions_cnt++;
+				HBM_DBG("Passing region #%d: Range: (%d - %d), Optimal PHY VREF: (%d)",
+					passing_regions_cnt, valid_vref_start, valid_vref_end,
+					opt_phy_vref);
+				#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+					single_hbm_res->rd_vref.max =  valid_vref_end;
+					single_hbm_res->rd_vref.min =  valid_vref_start;
+				#endif
+				if (passing_region_size > passing_region_max) {
+					passing_region_max = passing_region_size;
+					final_opt_vref = opt_phy_vref;
+					#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+					single_hbm_res->opt_dr_rd_vref = final_opt_vref;
+					#endif
+				}
+				/* Initialize to reset value for next possible passing region */
+				valid_vref_start = UINT32_MAX;
+				weighted_sum_valid_vref_eyes = 0;
+				sum_valid_vref_eyes = 0;
+			}
+			training_fail_cnt++;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		/* If we got here, read-eye training passed with current PHY VREF */
+		dev_avg_eye_ratio = get_avg_eye_ratio(hbm_dev);
+
+		/* Should not occur, but still protect against it */
+		if (dev_avg_eye_ratio == 0)
+			continue;
+
+		/* Set passing region start */
+		if (valid_vref_start == UINT32_MAX)
+			valid_vref_start = phy_vref;
+
+		sum_valid_vref_eyes += dev_avg_eye_ratio;
+		weighted_sum_valid_vref_eyes += phy_vref * dev_avg_eye_ratio;
+		#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+
+		int bit_shift (int x){
+    		return (1 << x);
+		}
+
+		int sign_conversion(unsigned int val){
+    		if (val & bit_shift(6))
+        		return -(0x1f - (val & ~bit_shift(6)) + 1);
+    		else
+        		return val;
+		}
+
+		uint32_t ch_offset, dw_offset, dw_res_offset, ch, dw;
+		for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+			ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+
+			for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+				dw_offset = ch_offset + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+				dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + dw;
+				LOG_INF("driver read: HBM = %d", hbm_dev);
+				LOG_INF("VREF = %d", phy_vref);
+				LOG_INF("DW = %d", dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM);
+				single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].min = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].max = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].final = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->rd_fall[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].min = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+				single_hbm_res->rd_fall[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].max = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->rd_fall[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].final = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_RXCLKNDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+				// LOG_INF("dw_offset = %d", dw_offset);
+				LOG_INF("rd_rise:min(HEX) = %x", single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].min);
+				LOG_INF("rd_rise:min(converted) = %d", sign_conversion(single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].min));
+				LOG_INF("rd_rise:max = %d", single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].max);
+				LOG_INF("rd_rise:final = %d", single_hbm_res->rd_rise[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][phy_vref-1].final);
+			}
+		}
+		#endif
+    }
+
+	if (training_fail_cnt == PHY_VREF_OPTIONS) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d - Training FSM failed for all PHY VREF values", hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	phy_vref_valid_ratio = (passing_region_max * 100 + PHY_VREF_OPTIONS/2) / PHY_VREF_OPTIONS;
+	if (phy_vref_valid_ratio < PHY_VREF_RATIO_TH) {
+		LOG_INF("HBM%d PHY VREF passing regsion of %d%% does NOT meet TH(%d%%)",
+			hbm_dev, phy_vref_valid_ratio, PHY_VREF_RATIO_TH);
+	}
+
+	phy_set_vref(hbm_dev, final_opt_vref);
+
+	/* Train read eye again with optimal PHY VREF */
+	rc = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+	debug_print_training_status(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		return rc;
+
+	/* Train read latency with optimal PHY VREF */
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl = 1;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye = 0;
+	rc = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+	debug_print_training_status(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		return rc;
+	HBM_DBG("Final PHY VREF: %d. Passing region size: %u\n", final_opt_vref,
+		passing_region_max);
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static int semi_auto_write_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t phy_ch, dw, offset, dev_avg_eye_ratio, sum_dev_eyes_ratios = 0, hbm_vref,
+		opt_hbm_vref = UINT32_MAX, max_dev_avg_eye_ratio = 0, training_fail_cnt = 0;
+	bool training_fail;
+	int rc;
+#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+	struct hl_hbm_drv_data *drv_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(DEVICE_DT_GET(DT_DRV_INST(0)));
+	struct single_hbm_train_result *single_hbm_res = &(drv_data->train_res->hbm_dev_tr_res[hbm_dev]);
+#endif
+
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.hbm_reset = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.mrs = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye = 1;
+
+	for (hbm_vref = 0; hbm_vref < HBM_VREF_STEPS; hbm_vref++) {
+		hbm_set_vref(hbm_dev, hbm_vref);
+
+		training_fail = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+		training_fail |= hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CHNSTATUS));
+		if (training_fail) {
+			training_fail_cnt++;
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		/* If we got here, write-eye training passed with current HBM VREF
+		 * we sum TX-DQ eyes ratio for all DWORDS in the HBM device
+		 */
+		for (phy_ch = 0; phy_ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; phy_ch++) {
+			for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+				offset = phy_ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+				sum_dev_eyes_ratios +=
+					calc_eye_ratio_ui(hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMINDLY) +
+						     offset),
+					hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+			     D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMAXDLY) +
+						     offset));
+			}
+		}
+
+		dev_avg_eye_ratio = sum_dev_eyes_ratios / (HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM *
+				    HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM);
+		if (dev_avg_eye_ratio == 0)
+			continue;
+
+		/* Select the HBM VREF value that produces the widest average write-eye */
+		if (dev_avg_eye_ratio > max_dev_avg_eye_ratio) {
+			max_dev_avg_eye_ratio = dev_avg_eye_ratio;
+			opt_hbm_vref = hbm_vref;
+		}
+
+		sum_dev_eyes_ratios = 0;
+
+		#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+		uint32_t ch_offset, dw_offset, dw_res_offset, ch, dw;
+		static int min_flag= 0;
+
+		if(!min_flag) { /* if we got here that means it's the first VREF value for training */
+			min_flag = 1;
+			single_hbm_res->wr_vref.min = hbm_vref;
+		}
+
+		for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+			ch_offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+			/*Check with Yoav: about the the relative 0 to each result*/
+			for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+				dw_offset = ch_offset + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+				dw_res_offset = ch * HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM + dw;
+				single_hbm_res->wr[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][hbm_vref].min = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMINDLY) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->wr[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][hbm_vref].max = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQMAXDLY) + dw_offset);
+			    single_hbm_res->wr[dw+ch*HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM][hbm_vref].final = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_TXDQDLY_P0) + dw_offset);
+			}
+		}
+		#endif
+	}
+	#if defined(CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION)
+	single_hbm_res->wr_vref.max =  hbm_vref - 1;
+	single_hbm_res->opt_dr_wr_vref = opt_hbm_vref;
+	#endif
+
+	if (training_fail_cnt == HBM_VREF_STEPS) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d - Training FSM failed for all HBM VREF values", hbm_dev);
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	hbm_set_vref(hbm_dev, opt_hbm_vref);
+
+	/* Train write eye again with optimal HBM VREF */
+	rc = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+	debug_print_training_status(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		return rc;
+
+	HBM_DBG("Final HBM VREF: %d",	opt_hbm_vref);
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+static int semi_auto_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	//int rc, get_training_rc; //not relevant for HBM_CHARACTERIZATION
+	int rc;
+
+	/* Set initial DFIMRL manually */
+	phy_set_read_latency(hbm_dev, DEFAULT_RD_LAT);
+
+	/* Enable CA training only */
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.hbm_reset = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.mrs = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca = 1;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref = 0;
+	hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye = 0;
+	rc = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+	debug_print_training_status(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d semi auto CA training FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		goto exit;
+	}
+
+	rc = semi_auto_read_training(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d semi auto read training FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		goto exit;
+	}
+
+	rc = semi_auto_write_training(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d semi auto write training FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		goto exit;
+	}
+
+exit:
+	rc = phy_get_training_results(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d semi auto get training results FAILED", hbm_dev);
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int phy_training(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+
+	/* Training is not supported on PLDM HBM model or Coral Sim */
+	if (pldm)
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+
+	/* Common training configuration */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_CMDREPEAT), hbm_cfg.train_cfg.cmd_repeat);
+	hbm_phy_rmw(hbm_dev,
+		D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCFG), hbm_cfg.train_cfg.data_pattern,
+		    HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_TRAINCFG_TRAINDATAPATTERN_M);
+
+	switch (hbm_cfg.train_cfg.training_type) {
+	case TRAIN_TYPE_HW_ONLY:
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.hbm_reset = 0;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.mrs = 0;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_ca = 1;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rl = 1;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_rdeye = 1;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_vref = 1;
+		hbm_cfg.train_cfg.train_wreye = 1;
+		/* Increase vref settling time delay */
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_VREFSETTLINGTIME_P0),
+											0x1194);
+
+		rc = phy_trigger_hw_training(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc)
+			LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d HW training FAILED\n", hbm_dev);
+
+		debug_print_training_status(hbm_dev);
+		rc = phy_get_training_results(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc)
+			LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d HW training FAILED to get results", hbm_dev);
+		break;
+
+	case TRAIN_TYPE_SEMI_AUTO:
+		rc = semi_auto_training(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc)
+			LOG_ERR("\nHBM%d semi auto training FAILED\n", hbm_dev);
+		break;
+
+	case TRAIN_TYPE_SW:
+		/* TODO - implement SW training based on MCBIST */
+		LOG_ERR("software training is not supported");
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+		break;
+
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("Un-supported training type!");
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+
+static uint32_t phy_io_dc_analyzer(int hbm_dev, struct lane_remap_info *remap_info, bool *slr_is_needed)
+{
+	uint32_t ch, dw, bpair_idx, aw_idx, mask, offset, curr_remap_code, hbm_remap_code,
+		 phy_remap_code, rc = STATUS_PASS;
+	bool new_broken_lanes_found = false, new_broken_lane_fix, valid_fix, burnt_efuse_fix;
+
+	/*
+	 * Cases for remap code(HBM eFuse, PHY remap after EXTEST) where options are
+	   valid/invalid/default code for each:
+	 * 1. Both are the same default value or valid code - no action is needed since it was
+	      already aligned by the EXTEST
+	 * 2. HBM eFuse is default and PHY remap is valid fix code - Failure since no infield fix is
+	      allowed by current Habana policy
+	 * 3. Both are valid fix code but different value - Failure since no infield fix is
+	      allowed by current Habana policy
+	 * 4. HBM eFuse is invalid fix code and PHY remap is valid fix code - Failure since no
+	      infield fix is allowed by current Habana policy
+	 * 5. HBM eFuse is invalid/valid fix code and PHY remap code is default - Need to perform
+	      SLR since this was done due to SIV-1361
+	 */
+
+	/* Recheck PHY remap AW values after EXTEST for new broken lanes */
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		slr_is_needed[ch] = false;
+		offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET;
+		curr_remap_code = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+					D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_AW_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_AW_AWREMAP)
+					+ offset);
+		for (aw_idx = 0; aw_idx < AW_BUS_TYPE_NUM; aw_idx++) {
+			mask = LANE_REMAP_MASK << aw_idx;
+			hbm_remap_code = FIELD_GET(mask, remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+			phy_remap_code = FIELD_GET(mask, curr_remap_code);
+
+			/* Fix is hard lane repair done on ATE or default value */
+			valid_fix = (hbm_remap_code == phy_remap_code);
+			/* Fix is due to new broken lane that was detected during EXTEST */
+			new_broken_lane_fix = (phy_remap_code != LANE_REMAP_DEFAULT_CODE &&
+					hbm_remap_code != phy_remap_code);
+			/* Fix is due to burnt eFuse bug */
+			burnt_efuse_fix = (hbm_remap_code != LANE_REMAP_DEFAULT_CODE &&
+					phy_remap_code == LANE_REMAP_DEFAULT_CODE);
+
+			if (valid_fix)
+				continue;
+			else if (new_broken_lane_fix) {
+				LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW new remap code: 0x%02x original remap code: 0x%02x",
+					hbm_dev, ch, curr_remap_code,
+					remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+				new_broken_lanes_found = true;
+				if (!DRIVER_SLR_EN)
+					rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+			} else if (burnt_efuse_fix) {
+				LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d AW need to perform SLR due to bad AW eFuse value",
+					hbm_dev, ch);
+				slr_is_needed[ch] = true;
+			} else {
+				LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d Invalid AW state during IO-DC analyzer - AW new remap code: 0x%02x original remap code: 0x%02x",
+					hbm_dev, ch, curr_remap_code,
+					remap_info->aw_remap_code[ch]);
+				rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	/* Recheck PHY remap DW values after EXTEST for new broken lanes */
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		for (dw = 0; dw < HBM_CHANNEL_DWORDS_NUM; dw++) {
+			offset = ch * PHY_CH_OFFSET + dw * PHY_DW_OFFSET;
+			curr_remap_code = hbm_phy_read(hbm_dev,
+				      D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_CH0_DW0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_CHANNELS_CH0_DW0_DWREMAP)
+				      + offset);
+			for (bpair_idx = 0; bpair_idx < BYTE_PAIRS_PER_DW; bpair_idx++) {
+				mask = LANE_BPAIR_REMAP_MASK << (8 * bpair_idx);
+				hbm_remap_code = FIELD_GET(mask, remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw]);
+				phy_remap_code = FIELD_GET(mask, curr_remap_code);
+
+				/* Fix is hard lane repair done on ATE or default value */
+				valid_fix = (hbm_remap_code == phy_remap_code);
+				/* Fix is due to new broken lane that was detected during EXTEST */
+				new_broken_lane_fix = (phy_remap_code !=
+								LANE_REMAP_BPAIR_DEFAULT_CODE &&
+						       hbm_remap_code != phy_remap_code);
+				/* Fix is due to burnt eFuse bug */
+				burnt_efuse_fix = (hbm_remap_code != LANE_REMAP_BPAIR_DEFAULT_CODE
+						&& phy_remap_code == LANE_REMAP_BPAIR_DEFAULT_CODE);
+
+				if (valid_fix)
+					continue;
+				else if (new_broken_lane_fix) {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d new remap code: 0x%04x original remap code: 0x%04x",
+						hbm_dev, ch, dw, curr_remap_code,
+						remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw]);
+					new_broken_lanes_found = true;
+					if (!DRIVER_SLR_EN)
+						rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+				} else if (burnt_efuse_fix) {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d need to perform SLR due to bad DW eFuse value",
+						hbm_dev, ch, dw);
+					slr_is_needed[ch] = true;
+				} else {
+					LOG_INF("HBM%d CH%d DW%d Invalid DW state during IO-DC analyzer - DW new remap code: 0x%04x original remap code: 0x%04x",
+						hbm_dev, ch, dw, curr_remap_code,
+						remap_info->dw_remap_code[ch][dw]);
+					rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if (new_broken_lanes_found)
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY IO-DC test FAILED: found new broken lanes", hbm_dev);
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+/* SW WA for SIV-1361, avoid updating remap for invalid remap code*/
+
+int phy_test_io_dc(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	struct lane_remap_info remap_info;
+	uint32_t ch, lanerepair_ctrl, lanerepair_status;
+	int rc;
+	bool slr_is_needed[HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM];
+
+	/* Read and store PHY remapping info */
+	rc = read_hbm_lane_remap(hbm_dev, &remap_info);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY IO-DC read and validate hbm lane remap before EXTEST FAILED",
+			hbm_dev);
+		return rc;
+	}
+
+	/* Validating that all PHY remap info is valid due to SIV-1361 */
+	validate_hbm_lane_remap(hbm_dev, &remap_info);
+
+	/* EXTEST */
+	lanerepair_ctrl = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRRUN_M	|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIREXTESTEN_M		|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_DRAMPOWERUPRSTEN_M		|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_DWREMAPMODE_M			|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRREMAPEN_M;
+
+	lanerepair_status = HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRDONE_M		|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIREXTESTDONE_M		|
+		HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRREMAPDONE_M;
+
+	/* PLDM HBM model doesn't support Tx on Rx-only IOs (nor Rx on Tx-only) */
+	if (!pldm)
+		lanerepair_ctrl |= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_EXTESTRXDIFFOUTEN_M		|
+			HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_EXTESTTXDIFFINEN_M			|
+			HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_EXTESTRXOUTEN_M			|
+			HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_EXTESTTXINEN_M;
+
+	if (DRIVER_SLR_EN) {
+		lanerepair_ctrl |= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRCTRL_LANEREPAIRSOFTEN_M;
+		lanerepair_status |= HBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_LANEREPAIRSTATUS_LANEREPAIRSOFTDONE_M;
+	}
+
+	rc = phy_trigger_lanerepair_fsm(hbm_dev, lanerepair_ctrl, lanerepair_status);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY IO-DC test FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		return rc;
+	}
+
+	rc = phy_io_dc_analyzer(hbm_dev, &remap_info, slr_is_needed);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY IO-DC anlalyze results after EXTEST FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		return rc;
+	}
+
+	for (ch = 0; ch < HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM; ch++) {
+		if (slr_is_needed[ch]) {
+			rc = align_hbm_lane_remap(hbm_dev, ch, &remap_info);
+			if (rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY IO-DC align hbm remap after EXTEST FAILED",
+					hbm_dev);
+				return rc;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("HBM%d PHY IO-DC test PASSED", hbm_dev);
+	return rc;
+}
+
+void phy_init_config(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	/* Set initial pstate */
+	phy_set_pstate(hbm_dev, BCAST);
+	phy_config_mode_registers(hbm_dev);
+
+	phy_config_dram_timing(hbm_dev);
+
+	phy_config_zq_cal(hbm_dev, hbm_cfg.phy_io_drive);
+
+	/* HDR mode ON + AWORD_MISR_CONFIG + DeviceIdReadOnRst */
+	hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_MASTERCTRL), 0x1804);
+
+	phy_config_internal_pll(hbm_dev);
+
+	phy_config_lcdl_cal(hbm_dev);
+
+	phy_preload_initeng(hbm_dev);
+
+	phy_load_initeng(hbm_dev);
+
+	debug_print_mrs(hbm_dev);
+	debug_print_pub_dramtiming(hbm_dev);
+}

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0 */

+/*
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.

+#include <hbm_rma_ecc.h>
+#include <gaudi3/gaudi3_hbm.h>
+
+int hbm_drv_add_row_rep(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep,
+			enum repair_type repair_type)
+{
+	struct hbm_strides strides;
+
+	strides.device_stride = NUM_CMN_PER_HBM * NUM_CH_PER_CMN * NUM_PC_PER_CH *
+				HBM_STACKS_NUM * HBM_BANKS_PER_PC;
+	strides.pc_stride = HBM_STACKS_NUM * HBM_BANKS_PER_PC;
+	strides.sid_stride = HBM_BANKS_PER_PC;
+
+	return hbm_add_row_rep(row_rep, repair_type, strides);
+}
