b/drivers/hbm/gaudi3/rma/hbm_rma_ecc.c

@@ -0,0 +1,411 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0 */
+
+/*
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#include <hbm_rma_ecc.h>
+#include <rma_if_dev.h>
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(hbm_rma);
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+
+struct hbm_row_repair_data {
+	struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry curr_iter_entry;
+	int num_of_used_rows;
+	int row_repair_idx_in_rma_table;
+	int non_derr_idx_in_rma_table;
+	int multi_serr_idx_in_rma_table;
+};
+
+static struct hbm_row_repair_data row_repair_data;
+
+static int modify_row_rep_entry(int rma_tbl_idx, enum repair_type repair_type)
+{
+	int rc = ROW_REP_STS_PASS;
+
+	if (rma_tbl_idx != -1) {
+		rma_table_entry_remove(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, rma_tbl_idx);
+		row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.repair_type = repair_type;
+		row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.header.validity = 1;
+		row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.header.count = 1;
+		rc = rma_table_entry_add(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE,
+		(struct generic_entry *)&(row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry));
+	} else {
+		rc = ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_BANK_LIMIT;
+	}
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+static int hbm_check_for_row_rep_bank_limit(enum rma_tables_type table_type,
+					    void *data, int entry_idx, void *arg)
+{
+	struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep =
+				(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *)data;
+	struct hbm_row_repair_data *curr_row_repair = (struct hbm_row_repair_data *)arg;
+
+	/* repair resources are per bank per channel. Need to count matching channel number */
+	int remove_ch = curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.pc / 2;
+	int entry_ch = row_rep->pc / 2;
+
+	if (curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.device == row_rep->device &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.bank == row_rep->bank &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.sid == row_rep->sid &&
+		remove_ch == entry_ch) {
+
+		if ((curr_row_repair->non_derr_idx_in_rma_table == -1) &&
+			(row_rep->header.derr_cause == 0)) {
+			curr_row_repair->non_derr_idx_in_rma_table = entry_idx;
+		}
+
+		if ((curr_row_repair->multi_serr_idx_in_rma_table == -1) &&
+			(row_rep->header.serr_cause == 1)) {
+			curr_row_repair->multi_serr_idx_in_rma_table = entry_idx;
+		}
+		curr_row_repair->row_repair_idx_in_rma_table = entry_idx;
+		curr_row_repair->num_of_used_rows++;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static int hbm_check_if_same_row_exist(enum rma_tables_type table_type,
+				       void *data, int entry_idx, void *arg)
+{
+	struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep =
+				(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *)data;
+	struct hbm_row_repair_data *curr_row_repair = (struct hbm_row_repair_data *)arg;
+
+	if (curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.device == row_rep->device &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.bank == row_rep->bank &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.sid == row_rep->sid &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.row == row_rep->row &&
+		curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.pc == row_rep->pc) {
+		curr_row_repair->row_repair_idx_in_rma_table = entry_idx;
+		curr_row_repair->num_of_used_rows++;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static void print_row_entry(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep)
+{
+	LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: hbm:%d, sid:%d, mc_pc:%d, bank:%d, row:%d",
+		row_rep->device, row_rep->sid, row_rep->pc, row_rep->bank, row_rep->row);
+}
+
+#ifndef TARGET_IS_PLDM
+static int hbm_check_for_row_repair_ecc_threshold(enum rma_tables_type table_type,
+						  void *data, int entry_idx, void *arg)
+{
+	struct rma_hbm_ecc_entry *ecc_entry = (struct rma_hbm_ecc_entry *)data;
+	struct hbm_row_repair_data *curr_row_repair = (struct hbm_row_repair_data *)arg;
+
+	if ((ecc_entry->device == curr_row_repair->curr_iter_entry.device) &&
+		(ecc_entry->header.count > ROW_REPAIR_ECC_THRESH)) {
+		curr_row_repair->row_repair_idx_in_rma_table = entry_idx;
+		curr_row_repair->num_of_used_rows++;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static void hbm_set_rma_row_repair(enum row_repair_cause cause)
+{
+	struct rma_entry rma_info;
+
+	LOG_ERR("[hbm_rma]: RMA indication !! Type: ROW_REP, Cause: %d", cause);
+
+	memset(&rma_info, 0, sizeof(struct rma_entry));
+	rma_info.rma_failure.hbm_init = 1;
+	rma_info.hbm_init_err.data = HBM_KNOWN_ROW_REPAIR_FAIL;
+	rma_info.rma_failure.row_replacement = 1;
+	rma_info.hbm_row_repair_err.data = cause;
+	rma_info.hbm_row_repair_err.err_during_boot_or_margin_tool = 0;
+
+	rma_table_write_rma_indication(&rma_info);
+}
+
+static void hbm_clear_rma_row_repair(void)
+{
+	int ret, num_entries = 0;
+	struct rma_entry rma_info;
+
+	rma_table_get_num_entries(RMA_HEADER_TABLE, &num_entries);
+	if (num_entries == 0)
+		return;
+
+	memset(&rma_info, 0, sizeof(struct rma_entry));
+	ret = rma_table_entry_read(RMA_HEADER_TABLE, 0, (struct generic_entry *)&rma_info,
+				sizeof(rma_info));
+	if (!ret) {
+		if ((rma_info.rma_failure.hbm_init == 1)  &&
+			(rma_info.hbm_init_err.data == HBM_KNOWN_ROW_REPAIR_FAIL)) {
+
+			rma_table_entry_remove(RMA_HEADER_TABLE, 0);
+			rma_table_write();
+
+			LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: Clear HBM init failure rma indication");
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+static void hbm_set_rma_hbm_table_full(void)
+{
+	struct rma_entry rma_info;
+
+	LOG_ERR("[hbm_rma]: RMA indication table is full");
+
+	memset(&rma_info, 0, sizeof(struct rma_entry));
+	rma_info.rma_failure.hbm_ecc = 1;
+	rma_info.hbm_row_repair_err.ecc_table_full = 1;
+
+	rma_table_write_rma_indication(&rma_info);
+}
+
+static int hbm_scan_ecc_for_row_repair(uint8_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int ret = 0;
+	struct rma_hbm_ecc_entry hbm_ecc;
+	struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry row_rep;
+	uint32_t num_entries = 0, ecc_entry_index, cnt;
+	bool replacement_done = false;
+
+	ret = rma_table_get_num_entries(RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE, &num_entries);
+
+	if (num_entries == 0)
+		return 0;
+
+	LOG_DBG("[hbm_rma]: scan_ecc_for_row_repair. entries: %d", num_entries);
+
+	row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.device = hbm_dev;
+
+	for (cnt = 0; cnt < num_entries; cnt++) {
+		row_repair_data.num_of_used_rows = 0;
+		row_repair_data.row_repair_idx_in_rma_table = -1;
+		rma_table_walk(RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE, hbm_check_for_row_repair_ecc_threshold,
+						(void *)&row_repair_data);
+
+		if (row_repair_data.row_repair_idx_in_rma_table >= 0) {
+			memset((void *)&hbm_ecc, 0, sizeof(hbm_ecc));
+			ret = rma_table_entry_read(RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE,
+						row_repair_data.row_repair_idx_in_rma_table,
+						(struct generic_entry *)&hbm_ecc, sizeof(hbm_ecc));
+			if (ret) {
+				LOG_ERR("[hbm_rma]: HBM_ECC_TABLE index[%d] copy failed !!",
+						row_repair_data.row_repair_idx_in_rma_table);
+				continue;
+			}
+
+			ecc_entry_index = row_repair_data.row_repair_idx_in_rma_table;
+			memcpy((void *)&row_rep, (void *)&hbm_ecc,
+				sizeof(hbm_ecc));
+			ret = hbm_drv_add_row_rep(&row_rep, REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT);
+			if (ret == ROW_REP_STS_PASS) {
+				LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: Removing ECC entry");
+				ret = rma_table_entry_remove(RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE,
+								ecc_entry_index);
+				if (ret)
+					LOG_ERR("[hbm_rma]: HBM ecc rma entry[%d] remove failed !!",
+									ecc_entry_index);
+				replacement_done = true;
+			} else {
+				/*
+				 * failure when rma indication or row repair failure
+				 * need to break and perform rma flash update
+				 */
+				break;
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	if (replacement_done)
+		rma_table_write();
+
+	return ret;
+}
+#endif /* TARGET_IS_PLDM */
+
+int hbm_add_row_rep(struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry *row_rep,
+		    enum repair_type repair_type, struct hbm_strides strides)
+{
+	uint32_t entry_idx, num_entries = 0;
+	uint8_t total_spare_rows;
+	int rc = ROW_REP_STS_PASS;
+	struct rma_hbm_spare_row_entry spare_row_entry = {};
+
+	memcpy((void *)&(row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry), (void *)row_rep, sizeof(row_rep));
+
+	row_repair_data.num_of_used_rows = 0;
+	rma_table_walk(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, hbm_check_if_same_row_exist,
+						(void *)&row_repair_data);
+	if (row_repair_data.num_of_used_rows) {
+		if (row_rep->header.derr_cause) {
+			/* Current row is already in replacement table and we got another DERR */
+			rc = ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_ROW_ALREADY_REPAIRED;
+		} else {
+			/*
+			 * If the current cause is mult-serr / mbist, we can stop the flow and
+			 * return pass from here so that ECC entry is deleted in caller function.
+			 */
+			LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: Got multi-serr for row repaired already");
+			print_row_entry(row_rep);
+
+			rc = ROW_REP_STS_PASS;
+		}
+
+		return rc;
+	}
+
+	entry_idx = row_rep->device * strides.device_stride;
+	entry_idx += row_rep->pc * strides.pc_stride;
+	entry_idx += row_rep->sid * strides.sid_stride;
+	entry_idx += row_rep->bank;
+	rma_table_entry_read(RMA_HBM_SPARE_ROWS_TABLE, entry_idx,
+			(struct generic_entry *)&spare_row_entry, sizeof(spare_row_entry));
+	total_spare_rows = spare_row_entry.spare_rows;
+	LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: hbm:%d pc:%d, sid:%d, bank:%d - spare rows left: %d", row_rep->device,
+					row_rep->pc, row_rep->sid, row_rep->bank, total_spare_rows);
+
+	row_repair_data.num_of_used_rows = 0;
+	row_repair_data.non_derr_idx_in_rma_table = -1;
+	row_repair_data.multi_serr_idx_in_rma_table = -1;
+	rma_table_walk(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, hbm_check_for_row_rep_bank_limit,
+						(void *)&row_repair_data);
+	if (!total_spare_rows) {
+		if (row_rep->header.derr_cause) {
+			rc = modify_row_rep_entry(
+				row_repair_data.non_derr_idx_in_rma_table, repair_type);
+			LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: swap low prio non-derr idx with the new derr row");
+		} else if (row_rep->header.mbist_cause) {
+			rc = modify_row_rep_entry(
+				row_repair_data.multi_serr_idx_in_rma_table, repair_type);
+			LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: swap low prio multi-serr idx with the new mbist row");
+		} else {
+			/*
+			 * If the current cause is mult-serr, we can stop the flow and return pass
+			 * from here so that ECC entry is deleted in caller function.
+			 */
+			LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: Got multi-serr for bank with all spare rows used");
+			print_row_entry(row_rep);
+			rc = ROW_REP_STS_PASS;
+		}
+		goto add_row_rep_exit;
+	}
+
+	rma_table_get_num_entries(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, &num_entries);
+
+	if (num_entries >= MAX_ROW_REPLACE_ENTRIES) {
+		rc = ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_TABLE_LIMIT;
+		return rc;
+	}
+
+	LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: adding row replacement entry");
+	print_row_entry(&(row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry));
+	row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.repair_type = repair_type;
+	row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.header.validity = 1;
+	row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry.header.count = 1;
+	rma_table_entry_add(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE,
+			(struct generic_entry *)&(row_repair_data.curr_iter_entry));
+
+	/* Update spare rows info for entry_idx as a new row is added to the replacement table*/
+	spare_row_entry.spare_rows = total_spare_rows - 1;
+	rma_table_entry_update(RMA_HBM_SPARE_ROWS_TABLE, entry_idx,
+				(struct generic_entry *)&spare_row_entry, sizeof(spare_row_entry));
+	LOG_INF("[hbm_rma]: New spare rows for entry idx:%d is %d", entry_idx,
+									spare_row_entry.spare_rows);
+
+add_row_rep_exit:
+	rma_table_write();
+
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int hbm_perform_known_row_repair(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev)
+{
+#ifdef TARGET_IS_PLDM
+	return 0;
+#else
+	uint32_t num_entries = 0, cntr = 0, i, ret = 0;
+	struct hl_hbm_repair_vec *repair_vec;
+	struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry row_rep;
+
+	ret = hbm_scan_ecc_for_row_repair(hbm_dev);
+	if (ret) {
+		hbm_set_rma_row_repair(ret);
+		return ret;
+	}
+
+	ret = rma_table_get_num_entries(RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE, &num_entries);
+	if (!ret && num_entries >= MAX_HBM_ECC_ENTRIES) {
+		hbm_set_rma_hbm_table_full();
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	/*
+	 * If hbm_scan_ecc_for_row_repair is successful in succeeding trials, either due to hbm_init
+	 * retry logic from applicaiton layer or it is successful when a reset triggered by user
+	 * after a RMA indication, clear row repair rma which was added before.
+	 */
+	hbm_clear_rma_row_repair();
+
+	LOG_DBG("[hbm_rma]: hbm_row_repair_flash_list");
+
+	rma_table_get_num_entries(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, &num_entries);
+	if (num_entries == 0)
+		return 0;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < num_entries; i++) {
+		rma_table_entry_read(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, i,
+				(struct generic_entry *)&row_rep, sizeof(row_rep));
+
+		if (row_rep.device == hbm_dev)
+			cntr++;
+	}
+
+	if (cntr == 0)
+		return 0;
+
+	repair_vec = (struct hl_hbm_repair_vec *)
+			k_malloc(cntr * sizeof(struct hl_hbm_repair_vec));
+	/* Ideally malloc fail should not happen here at preboot stage. Adding to fix coverity */
+	if (repair_vec == NULL) {
+		LOG_ERR("[hbm_rma]: Unable to malloc hbm_repair_vec of length: %d", cntr);
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	for (i = 0; i < cntr; i++) {
+		rma_table_entry_read(RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE, i,
+				(struct generic_entry *)&row_rep, sizeof(row_rep));
+
+		if (row_rep.device == hbm_dev) {
+			repair_vec[i].sid = row_rep.sid;
+			repair_vec[i].pc = row_rep.pc;
+			repair_vec[i].bank = row_rep.bank;
+			repair_vec[i].row = row_rep.row;
+		}
+	}
+
+	ret = hbm_drv_row_repair(dev, hbm_dev, repair_vec, cntr, REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT);
+	if (ret) {
+		hbm_set_rma_row_repair(ret);
+	} else {
+		/*
+		 * If multi_row_repair is successful in succeeding trials, either due to hbm_init
+		 * retry logic from applicaiton layer or it is successful when a reset triggered by
+		 * user after a RMA indication, clear row repair rma which was added before.
+		 */
+		hbm_clear_rma_row_repair();
+	}
+
+	k_free(repair_vec);
+
+	return ret;
+#endif /* TARGET_IS_PLDM */
+}

+	do {									            \
+		if (hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints)					    \
+			LOG_INF(__VA_ARGS__);					    \

+/*** Static functions and structs for drive row repair ***/

+static struct sec_repair_data_reg mbist_convert_hl_to_sec(struct hl_hbm_repair_vec *hl_vec)
+{
+	struct sec_repair_data_reg sec_repair_dr = {0};
+
+	sec_repair_dr.sid = hl_vec->sid;
+	sec_repair_dr.rb = hl_vec->bank;
+	sec_repair_dr.ra = hl_vec->row;
+	sec_repair_dr.dq_position = hl_vec->pc & 0x1;
+	sec_repair_dr.ch = (hl_vec->pc & 0xe) >> 1;
+
+	return sec_repair_dr;
+}

+static void mc_p1500_reset(int hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset, 0x0,
+		 HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_WRST_N_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	k_busy_wait(MC_P1500_RST_US);
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset, 0x1,
+		 HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_WRST_N_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+}
+
+// #endregion
+
+/*** Static functions and structs for both init and row repair ***/
+// #region Init and Row
+
+static void auto_temp_enable(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	if (pldm || !AUTO_TEMP_EN)
+		return;
+
+	/*
+	 * Auto temp interval defines the sampling rate of the auto temperature mechanism.
+	 * The value is in WRCK cycles (WRCK is the IEEE1500 bus clock which is 28MHz for HBM POR
+	 * freq of 1800MHz) where each value below 28 decimal will result in ~1us sampling rate due
+	 * to polling mechanism as part of IEEE1500 (this is the fastest sampling rate available).
+	 * Calculation is interval * WRCK = sampling rate.
+	 * Desired sampling rate is 1ms hence interval should be 28,000 = 0x6d60.
+	 */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP_INTERVAL) + hbm_offset,
+									AUTO_TEMP_1MS_INTERVAL);
+
+	/*
+	 * read the auto temp configuration reg to keep the WIR field and avoid RMW reg to
+	 * avoid doing WR_INFLIGHT polling.
+	 */
+	reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset,
+									NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP_ENABLE_M, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+	LOG_INF("HBM%d Auto-Temperature is enabled", hbm_dev);
+}
+
+// #endregion
+
+/*** Static functions and structs for hbm init ***/
+// #region HBM Init
+
+static void mc_assert_cke(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val;
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	reg_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_MC0_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH) + hbm_offset,
+									POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	reg_val |= MC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH_DFI_TRIGGER_CKE_FLIP_M;
+
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_DFI_PHY_CFG_CH), reg_val);
+}

+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_l = 0x2;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_s = 0x2;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tras = 0x19;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trp = 0xe;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trc = 0x25;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_rd = 0x1b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_wr = 0x10;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtp = 0x3;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfc = 0x167;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfcsb = 0x9f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrefd = 0x6;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_l = 0x1;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_s = 0x0;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_r = 0x1;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_s = 0x8;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_l = 0xc;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtrap = 0x12;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtw = 0x10;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtp = 0x15;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[0] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[1] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[2] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[3] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[4] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[5] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[6] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[7] = 0xc2f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[0] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[1] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[2] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[3] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[4] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[5] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[6] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[7] = 0x5f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tmod = 0xb;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksre = 0x7;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksrx = 0x7;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tckpde = 0x5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txs = 0x16f;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txp = 0x5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.refsb_gap = 0xa;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.twr = 0x1d;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.extend_phased_time = 0x1;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.t_pwreset = 1600;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.twtr_l = 13;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tinit5 = 320;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tmod = 24;
+		break;
+
+	case HBM_PLL_1800:
+	default:
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat = 40;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat = 11;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.parity_lat = 2;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tovwso = 0x14;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tfaw = 0xa;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_l = 0x2;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrd_s = 0x2;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tras = 0x1c;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trp = 0xf;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trc = 0x2a;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_rd = 0x1e;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trcd_wr = 0x12;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtp = 0x3;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfc = 0x194;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trfcsb = 0xb3;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trrefd = 0x7;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_l = 0x1;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_s = 0x0;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tccd_r = 0x1;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_s = 0x9;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtr_l = 0xe;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtrap = 0x14;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trtw = 0x12;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.twtp = 0x17;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[0] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[1] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[2] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[3] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[4] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[5] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[6] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefi[7] = 0xdb5;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[0] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[1] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[2] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[3] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[4] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[5] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[6] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.trefisb_adj[7] = 0x6b;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tmod = 0xd;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksre = 0x8;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tcksrx = 0x8;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tckpde = 0x6;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txs = 0x19d;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.txp = 0x6;
+		hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.refsb_gap = 0xa;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.twr = 0x1f;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.extend_phased_time = 0x2;
+		hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.t_pwreset = 1800;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.twtr_l = 15;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tinit5 = 360;
+		hbm_cfg.hbm_ac_param.tmod = 27;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	/* DFI timing calc */
+	hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.tphy_wr_lat = hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.wr_lat - 2;
+	hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.trd_data_en = (hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.rd_lat - 2) - 17;
+	hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.ctrlupd_max_t = 54 + hbm_cfg.phy_ac_param.extend_phased_time;
+}
+
+static void mc_p1500_init(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val = 0, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset,
+		     hbm_cfg.mc_ac_param.tovwso, HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_TOVWSO_M,
+		     POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG2_TSF_M, P1500_TMN_CFG2_TSF_VAL);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG2_THF_M, P1500_TMN_CFG2_THF_VAL);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG2_TSR_M, P1500_TMN_CFG2_TSR_VAL);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG2_THR_M, P1500_TMN_CFG2_THR_VAL);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG2) + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+	/*
+	 * Config and enable WRCK from the MC:
+	 * Divider value is calcucated so that the IEEE1500 bus frequency will be accordding to the
+	 * defined standard (up to50MHz).
+	 * Calculation - MC_freq / div <= 50MHz, where MC_freq = HBM_freq / 2.
+	 * Divider resolution in TMN_CFG1 are according to WRCK_DIV field configuration: 0-16,
+	 * 1-32, 2-64, 3-128, hence up to 1600MHz 16 (config 0) is enough since it is exactly 50MHz,
+	 * above 1600MHz divider will need to be increased.
+	 * For Gaudi3 POR (HBM_freq of 1800MHz) IEEE1500 bus frequency will be 28MHz (divider = 32)
+	 * which is slower comaperd to Gaudi2 (50MHZ IEEE1500 bus frequency).
+	 */
+	reg_val = hbm_cfg.ck_freq == HBM_PLL_1800 ? DIV_32 : DIV_16;
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset, reg_val,
+		 HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_WRCK_DIV_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset, 0x1,
+		     HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_WRCK_EN_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	k_busy_wait(WRCK_CLK_DELAY_US);
+
+	/* De-assert WRSTn */
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1) + hbm_offset, 0x1,
+		     HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TMN_CFG_1_WRST_N_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	k_busy_wait(WRST_N_DEASSERTION_DELAY_US);
+}
+
+static void phy_config_p1500(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	/* Disable traffic throttling, rst val is 2-slave which should be 0-disable in that stage */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_THROT_MON) + hbm_offset, 0x14100);
+
+	/* Due to STAR 3402501 - Need to keep WRCK period larger then 40ns */
+	if (hbm_cfg.ck_freq == HBM_PLL_1800)
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_P1500CTRL), 0x6);
+	else
+		hbm_phy_write(hbm_dev, D0_HBM0_PHY_P0_REG(mmHBM_PHY_P0_MASTER_P1500CTRL), 0x5);
+}
+
+static void traffic_throttling_enable(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t reg_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	if (pldm || !TRAFFIC_THROT_EN)
+		return;
+
+	/*
+	 * Limiting traffic to 2/3 capacity in case temperature TH crossed.
+	 * For configured value on SEQ_MONI_THROT_ON we enable traffic and then back pressure
+	 * the traffic for configured value on SEQ_MONI_THROT_OFF.
+	 * 60 cycles enable Schd, 30 cycles back-pressure Schd produce 2/3 traffic capacity.
+	 */
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MONI_THROT_ON) + hbm_offset,
+									SCHD_ENABLE_CYCL_NUM);
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SEQ_MONI_THROT_OFF) + hbm_offset,
+								SCHD_BACK_PRESSURE_CYCL_NUM);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_THROT_MON_THROT_MODE_M, 0x1);
+	/* Setting TH of 85C for limiting traffic and 65C to unlimit back */
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_THROT_MON_THROT_TEMP_DIS_M, DISENGAGE_TEMP_C);
+	reg_val |= FIELD_PREP(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_THROT_MON_THROT_TEMP_EN_M, ENGAGE_TEMP_C);
+
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_THROT_MON) + hbm_offset, reg_val);
+
+	HBM_DBG("Traffic Throttling in HBM%d is enabled: engage: %dC disengage: %dC ON: %d OFF: %d",
+		hbm_dev, ENGAGE_TEMP_C, DISENGAGE_TEMP_C, SCHD_ENABLE_CYCL_NUM,
+		SCHD_BACK_PRESSURE_CYCL_NUM);
+}
+
+static void patrol_scrubber_enable(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev), pc_idx, pc_offset;
+
+	if (!PATROL_SCRUBBER_EN)
+		return;
+
+	for (pc_idx = 0; pc_idx < NUM_PC_PER_CH; pc_idx++) {
+		pc_offset = hbm_offset + pc_idx * PC_OFFSET;
+
+		if (MC_COMPRESSION_EN) {
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_ARUSER_0) + pc_offset, 0x0);
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_AWUSER_0) + pc_offset, 0x0);
+			CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_WUSER_0) + pc_offset, 0x0);
+		}
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_START_ADDR_0) + pc_offset,
+			   PATROL_SCRB_START_ADDR);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_END_ADDR_0) + pc_offset,
+			   PATROL_SCRB_END_ADDR);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_CONFIG_0) + pc_offset,
+			   MC_CH_SCRB_CONFIG_AUTORESTART_M);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_CNTR_0) + pc_offset,
+			   PATROL_SCRB_DLY_CNTR);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_RELOAD_0) + pc_offset,
+			   PATROL_SCRB_RELOAD_EN);
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_BCAST_MC_REG(mmMC_CH_SCRB_ENABLE_0) + pc_offset,
+			   PATROL_SCRUBBER_EN);
+	}
+
+}
+
+static void mc_enable_compression(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_offset, val;
+
+	/* Due to HW issue with TPC, 128Byte transaction size where compression expected 256 Byte
+	 * which is causing partial access(decreases in some performance, but in total still
+	 * improves the results of workloads)
+	 */
+	if (!MC_COMPRESSION_EN)
+		return;
+
+	hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	val = FIELD_PREP(MC_CMN_COMPRS_MOD_M, 0xFFFF);
+	val |= FIELD_PREP(MC_CMN_COMPRS_EN_M, 0x1);
+	CFG_WREG32(hbm_offset + D0_HBM0_BCAST_CMN_REG(mmMC_CMN_COMPRS), val);
+}
+
+static void hif_config(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hif_offset = hbm_dev * HDCORE_OFFSET, i;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < MC_RD_INFLIGHT_NUM; i++) {
+		CFG_WREG32(hif_offset + HD0_HIF_REG(mmH9_HIF_MC_MAX_RD_INFLIGHT_0) +
+			(i * BYTE_OFFSET), MAX_RD_INFLIGHT);
+	}
+
+	for (i = 0; i < MC_WR_CRED_NUM; i++) {
+		CFG_WREG32(hif_offset + HD0_HIF_REG(mmH9_HIF_MC_MAX_WR_CRED_0) +
+			(i * BYTE_OFFSET), MAX_WR_CREDIT);
+	}
+
+	/* MC Enable traffic */
+	CFG_WREG32(hif_offset + HD0_HIF_REG(mmH9_HIF_HBM_AXI_GLBL_TAP_EN), 0xf);
+}
+
+static int phy_pre_training_tests(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+
+	if (pldm)
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+
+	rc = phy_test_loopback(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		goto exit_pre_train;
+
+	rc = phy_test_io_dc(hbm_dev);
+	/* IO DC test is triggering hbm reset which resets the efuse WP */
+	hbm_efuse_write_protect(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc)
+		goto exit_pre_train;
+
+	/* PHY trigges HBM reset after EXTESTs, need to re-set mode-registers */
+	rc = hbm_mrs(hbm_dev);
+
+exit_pre_train:
+	return rc;
+}
+
+#ifdef HBM_DUMP_BUS
+#include <psoc_boot_conf_masks.h>
+#include <psoc_boot_conf_regs.h>
+#include <global_conf_regs.h>
+
+#define DUMP_SEL_HBM_M				0x7e00
+#define DUMP_SEL_COMPONENT_M			0xc0
+#define DUMP_SEL_MC_CMN_COMPONENT_M		0x30
+#define DUMP_SEL_MC_DFI_M			0xc
+#define DUMP_SEL_SIGNAL_M			0x3
+
+enum general_dump_component {
+	GENERAL_DUMP_MC_DFI = 0x0,
+	GENERAL_DUMP_GSKT_CLK = 0x1,
+	GENERAL_DUMP_GSKT_RST = 0x2,
+};
+
+enum sub_dump_component {
+	SUB_DUMP_MC = 0x0,
+	SUB_DUMP_DFI = 0x1,
+};
+
+enum mc2ch_dump_component {
+	MC_CMN01_DUMP = 0x0,
+	MC_CMN23_DUMP = 0x1,
+	MC_CMN45_DUMP = 0x2,
+	MC_CMN67_DUMP = 0x3,
+};
+
+/*
+ * 8 signals will be directed to GPIOs 50:57 per configuration
+ * signal should be value 0-3
+ * see FC Dump Bus.xls HBM sheet, MC spec(table 10.4.3, 10.4.4) for more details on the  signals
+ * that are supported per configuration
+ */
+static void hbm_dump_bus(uint32_t hbm_dev, enum general_dump_component general_component,
+			 enum mc2ch_dump_component mc2ch, enum sub_dump_component sub_component,
+			 uint8_t signal)
+{
+	const uint32_t dump_hbm_offset = 20, psoc_gpio_func0 = 0;
+	uint32_t gpio_idx, reg_val;
+	uint32_t die_offset = (hbm_dev / GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS_PER_DIE) * DIE_OFFSET;
+
+	if (pldm)
+		return;
+
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_PSOC_BOOT_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_BOOT_CONF_BOOT_STRAP_PINS_L) + die_offset, 0x0,
+		     PSOC_BOOT_CONF_BOOT_STRAP_PINS_L_DUMP_DIS_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+	reg_val = FIELD_PREP(DUMP_SEL_HBM_M, hbm_dev + dump_hbm_offset)		|
+		  FIELD_PREP(DUMP_SEL_COMPONENT_M, general_component)		|
+		  FIELD_PREP(DUMP_SEL_MC_CMN_COMPONENT_M, mc2ch)		|
+		  FIELD_PREP(DUMP_SEL_MC_DFI_M, sub_component)			|
+		  FIELD_PREP(DUMP_SEL_SIGNAL_M, signal);
+
+	HBM_RMWREG32(D0_PSOC_BOOT_CONF_REG(mmPSOC_BOOT_CONF_BOOT_STRAP_PINS_L) + die_offset,
+		     reg_val, PSOC_BOOT_CONF_BOOT_STRAP_PINS_L_DUMP_SEL_M, POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+
+	/* configure GPIO_50:57  to func0. signals should be drived out from the pins */
+	for (gpio_idx = 0; gpio_idx < 8; gpio_idx++)
+		CFG_WREG32(D0_PSOC_GLOBAL_CONF_REG(mmGLOBAL_CONF_PAD_SEL_50) + die_offset
+			   + gpio_idx * 4, psoc_gpio_func0);
+
+	LOG_ERR("HBM dump bus is enabled. this feature is dangerous to the host!!!\n");
+}
+#endif
+
+// #endregion
+
+/*** Static functions and structs for hbm driver fuse scan ***/
+// #region Fuse Scan
+
+static bool is_auto_temp_enabled(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t auto_temp_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	auto_temp_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset,
+				   POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	return FIELD_GET(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP_ENABLE_M, auto_temp_val);
+}
+
+static void auto_temp_disable(uint32_t hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t auto_temp_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+
+	auto_temp_val =  HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset,
+				   POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	auto_temp_val &= ~HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP_ENABLE_M;
+	CFG_WREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_AUTO_TMP) + hbm_offset, auto_temp_val);
+
+	LOG_DBG("HBM%d Auto-Temperature is disabled", hbm_dev);
+}
+
+static int hbm_drv_initialize(const struct device *dev)
+{
+	return 0;
+}
+
+// #endregion
+
+// #region HBM APIs
+
+int hbm_drv_run_hbm_init(const struct device *dev)
+{
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t hbm_dev;
+	uint8_t hbm_dev_num = hbm_info.num_of_dies * GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS_PER_DIE;
+	enum hbm_pll_freqs freq_e;
+	struct hl_hbm_drv_data *hbm_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(dev);
+
+	if (hbm_info.num_of_dies == 0) { /* if (hbm_info_is_not_valid) */
+		LOG_ERR("HBM driver failed to get hbm_info!");
+		return -EINVAL;
+	}
+
+	HBM_DBG("**** HBM init start ****");
+	HBM_DBG("number of active HBMs: 0x%x, number of dies:0x%x", hbm_info.num_active_hbm,
+		hbm_info.num_of_dies);
+
+	memset(&hbm_cfg.slr_info, JEDEC_NO_LANE_REMAP_CODE, HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM *
+		sizeof(struct hbm_ch_lane_remap_info));
+
+	/* Convert Hz to MHz */
+	hbm_info.hbm_freq /= MHz;
+	freq_e = freq_to_enum(hbm_info.hbm_freq);
+	if (freq_e < 0 || freq_e >= HBM_PLL_MAX) {
+		LOG_ERR("Retrieval of PLL frequancy failed. (%dMHz)", hbm_info.hbm_freq);
+		hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_PLL_GET_FAIL;
+		return STATUS_FAIL;
+	}
+	LOG_INF("HBM PLL frequency: %dMHz", hbm_info.hbm_freq);
+
+	/* Check if running from Coral Sim and if so skip HBM Init */
+	if (hl_is_sim_mode()) {
+		LOG_INF("HBM driver skipped on simulator");
+		hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_NO_FAIL;
+		return STATUS_PASS;
+	}
+
+	hbm_cfg.ck_freq = freq_e;
+	set_timing_params(hbm_cfg.ck_freq);
+
+	/*
+	 * during testing we see that when HBM6 is being reset it causes HBM4 to reset,
+	 * and when HBM7 is being reset it causes HBM5 to reset.
+	 * as a WA all HBMs are being reset before the initialization loop starts.
+	 */
+	for (hbm_dev = 0; hbm_dev < hbm_info.num_active_hbm; hbm_dev++)
+		mc_reset(hbm_dev);
+
+	for (hbm_dev = 0; hbm_dev < hbm_info.num_active_hbm; hbm_dev++) {
+		phy_reset(hbm_dev);
+
+		mc_config(hbm_dev);
+
+		phy_init_config(hbm_dev);
+
+		rc = phy_dfi_init(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d - FAILED on phy dfi init", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_PHY_INIT_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}
+
+		mc_assert_cke(hbm_dev);
+
+		rc = hbm_reset(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d - FAILED on hbm reset", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_MRS_OR_RST_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}
+
+		rc = hbm_mrs(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d - FAILED on hbm mrs", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_MRS_OR_RST_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}
+
+		phy_config_p1500(hbm_dev);
+		mc_p1500_init(hbm_dev);
+		/* Setting HBM eFuse WP to avoid eFuse corruption as described on SIV-1361 */
+		hbm_efuse_write_protect(hbm_dev);
+
+		if (freq_e == HBM_PLL_800) {
+			LOG_INF("HBM%d PLL is set to 800MHz, not operational mode", hbm_dev);
+#ifdef MBIST_DEBUG
+			struct mbist_data_out mbist_output;
+
+			hbm_drv_mbist(hbm_dev, MBIST_MODE_PBT, &mbist_output);
+			LOG_INF("MBIST result = %d, vec_cnt = %d", mbist_output.completion_status,
+				mbist_output.repair_vec_cnt);
+#endif
+			continue;
+		}
+
+		rc = phy_pre_training_tests(hbm_dev);
+		LOG_INF("phy_pre_training_tests done!!!!!!!!");
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d failure detected during pre-training tests", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_PRE_TRAINING_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}
+
+		if (!pldm) {
+			rc = perform_known_lane_repair(hbm_dev);
+			LOG_INF("perform_known_lane_repair!!!");
+#ifdef CONFIG_SVE_FLAVOR_CORE
+			if (rc) {
+				LOG_ERR("HBM%d failed to perform known lane repair with rc:%d",
+					hbm_dev, rc);
+				hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_KNOWN_LANE_REPAIR_FAIL;
+				return rc;
+			}
+#endif
+		}
+
+		rc = phy_training(hbm_dev);
+		LOG_INF("phy_training done!!!!!!");
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d training FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_TRAINING_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}
+
+		rc = hbm_perform_known_row_repair(dev, hbm_dev);
+		/*if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d failed to perform known row repair with rc:%d",
+				hbm_dev, rc);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_KNOWN_ROW_REPAIR_FAIL;
+			return rc;
+		}*/
+		debug_print_pub_deviceid(hbm_dev);
+
+		rc = hbm_mcbist_io_ac(hbm_dev);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST IO AC test FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_MCBIST_FAIL;
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		mc_enable_compression(hbm_dev);
+
+		/* Full memory AC test */
+		rc = hbm_mcbist_full_mem_scrub(hbm_dev, MCBIST_PRBS);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST Full Memory AC test FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_MCBIST_FAIL;
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		/* Scrubbing the entire DRAM */
+		rc = hbm_mcbist_full_mem_scrub(hbm_dev, MCBIST_FAST_55_AA);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d MCBIST Full Memory Scrubbing FAILED", hbm_dev);
+			hbm_data->hbm_sts = HBM_MCBIST_FAIL;
+			return STATUS_FAIL;
+		}
+
+		auto_temp_enable(hbm_dev);
+
+		traffic_throttling_enable(hbm_dev);
+
+		open_traffic(hbm_dev);
+
+		patrol_scrubber_enable(hbm_dev);
+	}
+
+	/* The HIF blocks idx are not being rearranged in case of binning, hence we configure all */
+	for (hbm_dev = 0; hbm_dev < hbm_dev_num; hbm_dev++)
+		hif_config(hbm_dev);
+
+#ifdef HBM_DUMP_BUS
+	hbm_dump_bus(0, GENERAL_DUMP_MC_DFI, MC_CMN01_DUMP, SUB_DUMP_MC, 0);
+#endif
+
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+/* Force injects to hbm_info */
+void hbm_drv_inject_info(const struct device *dev,  void *info)
+{
+	memcpy(&hbm_info, info, sizeof(hbm_info));
+}
+
+/* HBM temperature range is 0-105C, for lower then 0C will still get 0 value */
+uint8_t hbm_drv_get_temperature(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev)
+{
+	uint32_t auto_temp_val, hbm_offset = CALC_HBM_OFFSET(hbm_dev);
+	uint8_t temp_celsius;
+
+	auto_temp_val = HBM_RREG32(D0_HBM0_CENTRAL_REG(mmHBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TEMP_REP) + hbm_offset,
+			     NO_POLL_WR_INFLIGHT);
+	temp_celsius = FIELD_GET(HBM_CENTRAL_P1500_TEMP_REP_AUTO_TEMP_READ_M, auto_temp_val);
+
+	return temp_celsius;
+}
+
+/* Gets MC_PC/PHY_PC index (0-15) and convert it bi-directional MC_PC <-> PHY_PC */
+int hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel(int pc_idx)
+{
+	const uint32_t hbm_ch_map[] = {0, 1, 4, 5, 2, 3, 6, 7};
+	uint32_t ch_idx = pc_idx / 2, valid_pc;
+
+	valid_pc = IN_RANGE(pc_idx, 0, NUM_PC_PER_HBM - 1);
+	if (!valid_pc) {
+		LOG_ERR("map_pseudo_channel: Invalid PC index provided (%d)", pc_idx);
+		return -1;
+	}
+	return (hbm_ch_map[ch_idx] * 2 + (pc_idx % 2));
+}
+
+/* For all hbm calls mc_reset */
+void hbm_drv_mc_reset_all(const struct device *dev)
+{
+	uint32_t hbm_idx;
+
+	for (hbm_idx = 0; hbm_idx < hbm_info.num_active_hbm; hbm_idx++)
+		mc_reset(hbm_idx);
+}
+
+/* sets hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints true value */
+int hbm_drv_set_debug_mode(const struct device *dev, bool is_debug)
+{
+	hl_hbm_open_dbg_prints = is_debug;
+
+	return STATUS_PASS;
+}
+
+/* After performing fuse_scan an mc_p1500_reset + hbm_reset are needed for operational mode */
+int hbm_drv_fuse_scan(int hbm_dev, struct hl_hbm_fuse_scan_vec *scan_vec, uint8_t *total_spare_rows)
+{
+
+	int rc;
+	uint32_t used_rows_cnt = 0, wdr, ch_group, phy_ch, phy_pc, pc, fuse_idx;
+	bool valid_input, is_initial_auto_temp_en;
+
+	/* converting the idxs for fuse scan arr data struct:
+	 *	ch_group - ch0/1/4/5 -> 0, ch2/3/6/7 -> 1. calculated from given mc_pc input 0-15 by
+	 *		   dividing in factor of 8 so mc_pc 0-7-> 0, 8-15-> 1
+	 *	pc - 0/1 according to ch: mc_pc0/2/4/6/8/10/12/14 -> 0, mc_pc1/3/5/7/9/11/13/15 -> 1
+	 *	     this is required to be 0/1 per channel according to the fuse scan cmd spec
+	 */
+	ch_group = scan_vec->mc_pc / (HBM_PHY_CHANNELS_NUM);
+	phy_ch = phy_ch_mapping(scan_vec->mc_pc / NUM_PC_PER_CH);
+	pc = scan_vec->mc_pc % NUM_PC_PER_CH;
+	phy_pc = hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel(scan_vec->mc_pc);
+
+	/* Check if auto_temp is enabled before this API */
+	is_initial_auto_temp_en = is_auto_temp_enabled(hbm_dev);
+	/* Avoid collision on IEEE1500 bus */
+	if (is_initial_auto_temp_en == true)
+		auto_temp_disable(hbm_dev);
+
+	valid_input = IN_RANGE(scan_vec->bank, 0, HBM_BANKS_PER_PC - 1) &&
+			IN_RANGE(scan_vec->mc_pc, 0, NUM_PC_PER_HBM - 1) &&
+			IN_RANGE(scan_vec->sid, 0, HBM_STACKS_NUM - 1);
+	if (!valid_input) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d fuse scan FAILED! Invalid input - mc_pc number: %d, sid: %d bank number: %d",
+			hbm_dev, scan_vec->mc_pc, scan_vec->sid, scan_vec->bank);
+		rc = STATUS_FAIL;
+		goto exit;
+	}
+
+	for (fuse_idx = 0; fuse_idx < HBM_MAX_SPARE_ROWS_PER_BANK; fuse_idx++) {
+		/* Construct WDR bits[22:1] according to input */
+		wdr = hbm_fuse_scan_arr[ch_group][pc][scan_vec->bank][fuse_idx] << 1;
+
+		/* WDR bits[24:23] are for sid (SID0 -> 01 / SID1 -> 10) */
+		wdr |= (scan_vec->sid == 0 ? BIT(fuse_sid0_bit) : BIT(fuse_sid1_bit));
+
+		LOG_DBG("Fuse scan vector: 0x%x", wdr);
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, phy_ch, ieee1500_fuse_scan, wir_write, &wdr,
+										MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+		k_busy_wait(HBM_ROWS_FUSE_SCAN_DELAY_US);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY_CH%d PHY_PC%d SID%d BA%d FUSE%d: IEEE1500 fuse scan write instruction FAILED",
+				hbm_dev, phy_ch, phy_pc, scan_vec->sid, scan_vec->bank,
+				fuse_idx);
+			goto exit;
+		}
+		wdr = 0;
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, phy_ch, ieee1500_fuse_scan, wir_read, &wdr,
+										MBIST_MODE_OFF);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY_CH%d PHY_PC%d SID%d BA%d FUSE%d: IEEE1500 fuse scan read instruction FAILED",
+				hbm_dev, phy_ch, phy_pc, scan_vec->sid, scan_vec->bank,
+				fuse_idx);
+			goto exit;
+		}
+
+		if (wdr & BIT(FUSE_CHECK_BIT))
+			used_rows_cnt++;
+	}
+	*total_spare_rows = HBM_MAX_SPARE_ROWS_PER_BANK - used_rows_cnt;
+	LOG_DBG("HBM%d PHY_CH%d PHY_PC%d SID%d BA%d has %d spare rows\n", hbm_dev, phy_ch,
+		phy_pc, scan_vec->sid, scan_vec->bank, (int)*total_spare_rows);
+exit:
+	/* re-enable auto_temp if it was enabled before this API */
+	if (is_initial_auto_temp_en == true)
+		auto_temp_enable(hbm_dev);
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int hbm_drv_row_repair(const struct device *dev, int hbm_dev,
+				struct hl_hbm_repair_vec *repair_vec, int repair_vecs_len,
+				enum repair_type repair_type)
+{
+
+	struct hl_hbm_repair_vec curr_repair_vec;
+	int rc, mcbist_rc, ch, vec_idx;
+	uint32_t wdr;
+	struct sec_repair_data_reg sec_repair_vec = {0};
+	bool valid_input;
+
+	if ((repair_vec == NULL) && (repair_vecs_len != 0)) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM row repair FAILED! NULL repair_vec ptr for non-zero repair_vecs_len");
+		return ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_INVALID_INPUT;
+	}
+
+	if (repair_vecs_len == 0)
+		return ROW_REP_STS_PASS;
+
+	valid_input = IN_RANGE(hbm_dev, 0, GAUDI3_NUM_OF_HBMS - 1) &&
+			repair_type == REPAIR_TYPE_SOFT;
+
+	if (!valid_input) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM row repair FAILED! Invalid input: hbm_dev: %d, repair_type: %d",
+			hbm_dev, repair_type);
+		return ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_INVALID_INPUT;
+	}
+
+	for (vec_idx = 0; vec_idx < repair_vecs_len; vec_idx++) {
+		curr_repair_vec = repair_vec[vec_idx];
+		valid_input =  IN_RANGE(curr_repair_vec.pc, 0, NUM_PC_PER_HBM - 1) &&
+				IN_RANGE(curr_repair_vec.sid, 0, HBM_STACKS_NUM - 1) &&
+				IN_RANGE(curr_repair_vec.bank, 0, HBM_BANKS_PER_PC - 1) &&
+				IN_RANGE(curr_repair_vec.row, 0, HBM_ROWS_PER_BANK - 1);
+		if (!valid_input) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM row repair FAILED! Invalid vector: hbm_dev: %d, mc_pc: %d ,sid: %d, bank: %d, row: %d",
+				hbm_dev, curr_repair_vec.pc, curr_repair_vec.sid,
+				curr_repair_vec.bank, curr_repair_vec.row);
+			return ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_INVALID_INPUT;
+		}
+		/* Convert mc_pc input to phy_pc for curr_repair_vec */
+		curr_repair_vec.pc = hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel(curr_repair_vec.pc);
+		/* Construct WDR as required by SEC */
+		sec_repair_vec = mbist_convert_hl_to_sec(&curr_repair_vec);
+		ch = curr_repair_vec.pc / 2;
+		wdr = sec_repair_vec.ra | (sec_repair_vec.rb << ROW_REPAIR_RB_SHIFT) |
+		      (sec_repair_vec.dq_position << ROW_REPAIR_DQ_POSITION_SHIFT) |
+		      (sec_repair_vec.sid << ROW_REPAIR_SID_SHIFT) | (1 << ROW_REPAIR_ENABLE_SHIFT);
+		LOG_DBG("Row repair DR: 0x%x", wdr);
+
+		rc = mc_p1500_inst(hbm_dev, ch, ieee1500_soft_repair, wir_write, &wdr, 0);
+		if (rc) {
+			LOG_ERR("HBM%d PHY_CH%d: IEEE1500 row repair instruction FAILED",
+				hbm_dev, ch);
+			return ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+		}
+		k_busy_wait(HBM_ROW_REPAIR_DELAY_US);
+		LOG_INF("HBM%d MC_PC%d Bank:%d Row:0x%x - IEEE1500 row repair instruction PASSED",
+			hbm_dev, repair_vec[vec_idx].pc, repair_vec[vec_idx].bank,
+			repair_vec[vec_idx].row);
+	}
+
+	mc_p1500_reset(hbm_dev);
+	rc = hbm_mrs(hbm_dev);
+	if (rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d row repair FAILED! hbm_mrs failure", hbm_dev);
+		return ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_P1500;
+	}
+
+	/* Initialize the ECC code */
+	mcbist_rc = hbm_mcbist_full_mem_scrub(hbm_dev, MCBIST_PRBS);
+	if (mcbist_rc) {
+		LOG_ERR("HBM%d memory scrubbing after row repair FAILED", hbm_dev);
+		rc = ROW_REP_STS_FAIL_SCRUB_FAILED;
+	}
+	LOG_INF("HBM%d row repair PASSED for %d rows", hbm_dev, repair_vecs_len);
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int hbm_drv_get_num_active_hbms(const struct device *dev)
+{
+	return hbm_info.num_active_hbm;
+}
+
+#ifdef CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION
+int hbm_drv_set_train_res_data_ptr(const struct device *dev,
+					  void *train_res_p)
+{
+		struct hl_hbm_drv_data *drv_data = HBM_DRV_DATA(dev);
+
+		if (train_res_p == NULL) {
+			LOG_ERR("got NULL train results struct pointer");
+			return -1;
+		}
+
+		drv_data->train_res = (struct hbm_train_result *) train_res_p;
+
+		return 0;
+}
+#endif

+static const struct hl_hbm_driver_api hl_hbm_drv_api = {
+	.hbm_drv_run_hbm_init = hbm_drv_run_hbm_init,
+	.hbm_drv_inject_info = hbm_drv_inject_info,
+	.hbm_drv_lane_repair = hbm_drv_lane_repair,
+	.hbm_drv_get_temperature = hbm_drv_get_temperature,
+	.hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel = hbm_drv_map_pseudo_channel,
+	.hbm_drv_mc_reset_all = hbm_drv_mc_reset_all,
+	.hbm_drv_set_debug_mode = hbm_drv_set_debug_mode,
+	.hbm_drv_fuse_scan = hbm_drv_fuse_scan,
+	.hbm_drv_mbist = hbm_drv_mbist,
+	.hbm_drv_row_repair = hbm_drv_row_repair,
+	.hbm_drv_get_num_active_hbms = hbm_drv_get_num_active_hbms,
+#ifdef CONFIG_HL_HBM_STORE_TEST_INFO
+	.hbm_drv_get_status = hbm_drv_get_status,
+	.hbm_drv_run_mcbist = hbm_drv_run_mcbist,
+#endif
+#ifdef CONFIG_HBM_CHARACTERIZATION
+	.hbm_drv_set_train_res_data_ptr = hbm_drv_set_train_res_data_ptr,
+#endif
+};
+
+static struct hl_hbm_drv_data hbm_data;
+
+DEVICE_DT_INST_DEFINE(0,
+		&hbm_drv_initialize,
+		NULL,
+		&hbm_data,
+		NULL,
+		POST_KERNEL,
+		CONFIG_APPLICATION_INIT_PRIORITY,
+		&hl_hbm_drv_api);
